Conclusion: 因果方法（UDS）是遗忘评估最可靠的方式，提供了将UDS集成到现有基准框架的指南，可简化评估流程。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) unlearning has emerged as a crucial post-hoc mechanism for privacy protection and AI safety, yet auditing whether target knowledge is truly erased remains challenging. Existing output-level metrics fail to detect when this knowledge remains recoverable from internal representations. Recent white-box studies reveal such residual knowledge but often rely on auxiliary training or dataset-specific adaptations, leaving no generalizable metric. To address these limitations, we propose the Unlearning Depth Score (UDS), a metric that quantifies the mechanistic depth of unlearning via activation patching. UDS first identifies layers that encode the target knowledge using a retain model baseline, then measures how much of it is erased in the unlearned model on a 0-1 scale. In a meta-evaluation across 20 metrics on 150 unlearned models spanning 8 methods, UDS achieves the highest faithfulness and robustness, confirming our causal approach as the most reliable for unlearning evaluation. Case studies further reveal that white-box metrics can disagree at the layer level and that erasure depth varies across examples. We provide guidelines for integrating UDS into existing benchmarking frameworks and streamlining the evaluation pipeline. Code and data are available at https://github.com/gnueaj/unlearning-depth-score

</details>


### [38] [HiMed: Incentivizing Hindi Reasoning in Medical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24635)
*Dingfeng Jiang,Han Yan,Chenze Ma,Amit Kumar Jaiswal,Ang Li,Yunxiang Jiang,Xinlei Xiong,Juhao Liang,Hongru Xiao,Xiang Li,Fan Bu,Jiale Han,Ruchir Gupta,Prayag Tiwari,Benyou Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HiMed，一个印地语医学推理语料库和基准测试集，以及HiMed-8B模型，通过衰减支架奖励来提升印地语医学推理能力，缩小英印语言间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学大语言模型在减少医疗差距方面具有潜力，但印地语等低资源语言严重缺乏代表性。现有模型在高资源语言中表现出色，但在印地语，特别是印度传统医学体系上性能急剧下降，需要跨语言医学迁移和印地语推理能力的提升。

Method: 构建HiMed语料库和基准测试集（涵盖西方和印度医学），并提出HiMed-8B模型，通过设计衰减支架奖励（decaying scaffolding reward）进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，HiMed-8B在印地语医学推理性能上显著提升，英印语言之间的准确性差距缩小。消融实验验证了各训练阶段和奖励组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的印地语医学语料库和衰减支架奖励训练，可以有效提升低资源语言医学LLM的推理能力，缩小与高资源语言之间的性能鸿沟。

Abstract: Medical large language models hold promise for reducing healthcare disparities, yet Hindi remains severely underrepresented. While medical LLMs excel in high-resource languages, their performance degrades sharply in Hindi, particularly on Indian systems of medicine. We argue that robust cross-lingual medical transfer requires Hindi reasoning. To this end, we introduce HiMed, a Hindi reasoning medical corpus and benchmark suite covering both Western and Indian medicine. We further propose HiMed-8B, a Hindi-form medical reasoning LLM, through the design of decaying scaffolding reward. Extensive experiments demonstrate improvement in Hindi medical reasoning performance and reduction in the English--Hindi accuracy gap. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each training stage and reward component. All data and code are available on GitHub: https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/HiMed.

</details>


### [39] [Know You Before You Speak: User-State Modeling for LLM Personalization in Multi-Turn Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24647)
*Jiani Luo,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Shuyi Miao,Bingbing Xu,Stefan Konigorski,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PUMA是一个基于自由能原理的框架，将个性化对话形式化为部分可观察下的决策问题，通过显式用户状态建模、信念维护和期望自由能最小化来选择对话动作，在医疗咨询和动机访谈基准上实现了更好的长期对话效果和用户状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆和用户画像的个性化对话方法主要复用可观察的用户信息，难以建模用户状态的动态演化，也无法基于动作对未来用户状态的影响来选择策略。

Method: 提出PUMA框架，基于自由能原理(FEP)，将个性化对话建模为部分可观察马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)，通过维护用户隐藏状态的信念、细化用户状态模型（观测生成和动作条件状态转移），并通过最小化期望自由能来平衡认知目标（探索）和实用目标（达成对话目标）从而选择对话动作。

Result: 在医疗咨询和动机访谈基准上，PUMA在长程对话效果和响应质量上均优于基线；跨数据集研究显示其用户状态估计和下一状态预测更可靠。

Conclusion: 个性化对话应从被动记忆检索转向基于模型的用户演化决策，PUMA通过显式建模用户状态动态和基于自由能原理的动作选择，为这一转变提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Personalized dialogue requires more than recalling explicit user histories: systems also need to infer hidden user states that evolve through interaction and shape appropriate response strategies. Existing memory- and profile-based methods primarily reuse observable user information, offering limited support for modeling user-state dynamics or selecting actions based on how they shape future user states. We propose PUMA (Prospective User-state Modeling for Action selection), a framework grounded in the Free Energy Principle (FEP) that formulates personalization as decision-making under partial observability, centered on an explicit user state model that captures latent user states and their action-conditioned dynamics. At each turn, PUMA maintains a belief over the user's hidden state, refines the user state model for observation generation and action-conditioned state transition, and selects dialogue actions by minimizing expected free energy, balancing epistemic and pragmatic objectives under a unified criterion. This formulation shifts personalization from passive memory retrieval to model-based decision-making over user evolution. We instantiate PUMA on healthcare-oriented counseling and motivational interviewing benchmarks with latent state annotations for rigorous evaluation. Experiments show that PUMA improves long-horizon dialogue outcomes while maintaining strong response quality, and a cross-dataset study demonstrates more reliable user-state estimation and next-state prediction.

</details>


### [40] [Mix-MoE: Improving Multilingual Machine Translation of Large Language Models through Mixed MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24681)
*Bo Li,Tianyu Dong,Shaolin Zhu,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Mix-MoE框架，通过混合专家模型解决多语言机器翻译中大语言模型微调时的参数干扰问题，在双语平行语料上显著提升翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多语言机器翻译中表现优异，但用平行语料微调会导致参数干扰（parameter interference），即学习双语翻译知识时破坏已习得的单语语言知识。

Method: 提出两阶段后预训练的Mix-MoE框架：第一阶段在单语语料上用MoE进行后预训练；第二阶段在平行语料上用MoE进行后预训练。将MoE层分为语言模型专家（LM Experts）和机器翻译专家（MT Experts），分别负责保留单语知识和存储双语翻译知识。引入基于傅里叶变换特征的路由机制，增强专家间的有效交互并利用文本结构模式。

Result: 在多语言机器翻译任务上，Mix-MoE显著优于现有基线方法，在缓解参数干扰方面取得明显进展。

Conclusion: Mix-MoE通过解耦单语和双语知识学习，有效缓解了参数干扰问题，为多语言机器翻译中的LLM微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great promise in multilingual machine translation (MT), even with limited bilingual supervision. However, fine-tuning LLMs with parallel corpora presents major challenges, namely parameter interference. To address these issues, we propose Mix-MoE, a mixed Mixture-of-Experts framework designed to train LLMs for multilingual MT. Our framework operates in two distinct stages: (1) post-pretraining with MoE on monolingual corpora, and (2) post-pretraining with MoE on parallel corpora. Crucially, we divide the MoE layers into two specialized groups: Language Model Experts (LM Experts) and Machine Translation Experts (MT Experts). LM Experts are designed to capture and retain the monolingual knowledge learned by the pre-trained LLM. MT Experts, on the other hand, are specifically trained to acquire and store bilingual translation knowledge. Furthermore, to facilitate effective interaction between these specialized experts and leverage potential underlying structural patterns in text, we introduce a routing mechanism enhanced by Fourier Transform features derived from model representations. The experimental results demonstrate that Mix-MoE excels in multilingual MT, significantly outperforming existing baselines and showing notable progress in mitigating parameter interference.

</details>


### [41] [CP-Agent: A Calibrated Risk-Controlled Agent for Feedback-Driven Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24693)
*Peisong Wang,Bowen Liu,Zehua Li,Yuyao Wang,Zhiwei Ma,Yuhan Li,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CP-Agent，一个无需参数更新的执行反馈驱动框架，通过校准停止过程建模、双重粒度验证、测试增强和自进化机制，将竞赛级编程任务Pass@1从25.8%提升至48.5%，并在成本-精度效率前沿上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在竞赛级编程任务上仍存在困难，现有智能体方法依赖大量推理时采样或昂贵的多阶段后训练，缺乏对执行反馈何时可靠以及其增益机制的理论理解。

Method: 将反馈驱动求解建模为校准的停止过程，识别三个关键量：误接受风险、对抗不良程序的程序级证据、活跃状态成功危险率。基于保留迹标定和预声明有限控制器，推导出结构性证书以在误接受前下界清洁成功概率。实例化为双重粒度验证、测试增强和经验驱动自进化三个机制，构成CP-Agent。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench Pro上Pass@1从25.8%提升至48.5%，在ICPC-Eval上Refine@5提升11.0%。在三个LLM骨干上均位于成本-精度效率前沿。消融实验验证各组件主要影响其对应的证书量。

Conclusion: 无需参数更新的执行反馈驱动方法可以有效提升竞赛级编程性能，且所提出的理论框架能解释各组件的增益机制。

Abstract: Large language models still struggle with contest-level programming, while many agentic remedies rely on massive inference-time sampling or expensive multi-stage post-training. We study when execution feedback reliably helps an LLM CP solver and which mechanisms govern the gains. We model feedback-driven solving as a calibrated stopped process and identify three quantities: false-admission risk, program-level evidence against bad programs, and the active-state success hazard. Under held-out trace calibration and selection from a pre-declared finite controller manifest, the resulting structural certificate lower-bounds the clean success probability before false admission. We instantiate mechanisms targeting these quantities as Dual-Granularity Verification, Test Augmentation, and Experience-Driven Self-Evolving, yielding CP-Agent. Without updating any parameters, CP-Agent raises Pass@1 from 25.8\% to 48.5\% on LiveCodeBench Pro and improves Refine@5 by 11.0\% on ICPC-Eval. Across three LLM backbones, CP-Agent lies on the cost--accuracy efficiency frontier, and ablations show that each component primarily affects its corresponding certificate quantity.

</details>


### [42] [The Path Matters: Learning a Token-Commitment Policy for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24697)
*Bohang Sun,Max Zhu,Francesco Caso,Jindong Gu,Junchi Yu,Philip Torr,Pietro Liò,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TraceLock提出了一种可学习的token承诺策略，通过自监督方式训练轻量级控制器，在冻结的扩散语言模型中动态决定哪些提议token应被提交到部分解码序列中，从而在不重新训练的情况下改善质量-步数权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型虽能并行解码，但面临一个隐藏的控制问题：每一步如何选择哪些提议token应被转移到部分解码序列中（即token承诺问题）。现有方法依赖手工设计的置信度规则或块级接受过滤器，缺乏可学习的统一策略。

Method: TraceLock是一个轻量级插件式控制器，将token承诺建模为可复用的轨迹状态策略。它从未来稳定性中获取自监督信号：步骤t位置i的提议token若与完整解码轨迹后该位置的最终token一致，则标记为稳定。控制器对变长轨迹状态评分，决定提交哪些活跃token。

Result: 在问答、数学推理和代码生成任务上，TraceLock相较启发式方法和基线方法改善了质量-步数权衡，在跨设置部署下表现尤为稳定。诊断分析表明其决策不可简化为标量置信度，证明冻结扩散语言模型存在超越置信度解码的可学习承诺轨迹空间。

Conclusion: Token承诺可以通过自监督学习实现为可复用的策略，TraceLock无需重新训练即可跨局部窗口宽度、生成长度和步数预算部署，在多种生成任务中优于现有基线。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models promise faster generation by refining many token positions in parallel, but this parallelism introduces a hidden control problem: which proposed tokens should be transferred into the partially decoded sequence at each step? We refer to this decision as token commitment. Existing frozen-generator decoders largely rely on hand-designed confidence rules or block-specific acceptance filters. We argue that token commitment can instead be learned as a reusable trace-state policy. We introduce TraceLock, a lightweight plug-in controller that instantiates this policy for a frozen diffusion language model. Since oracle commitment times are unavailable, TraceLock derives self-supervision from future stability: at decoding step t, a proposed token for position i is labeled stable if it matches the final token at position i after the full decoding trace completes. The controller scores variable-length trace states and decides which active token proposals should be committed to the partially decoded sequence. Once trained for a given frozen backbone, the controller can be deployed across local-window widths, generation lengths, and step budgets without retraining or per-setting calibration. Experiments on question answering, mathematical reasoning, and code generation show that TraceLock improves the quality-step tradeoff over heuristic and learned baselines, with particularly stable behavior under cross-setting deployment. Diagnostic analyses show that its decisions are not reducible to scalar confidence, suggesting that frozen diffusion language models expose a learnable space of commitment trajectories beyond confidence-based decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/BobSun98/TraceLock.

</details>


### [43] [TS-Skill: A Benchmark for Evaluating Analytical Skills in Time-Series Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24703)
*Liying Han,Kang Yang,Oliver Wang,Jason Wu,Pengrui Quan,Gaofeng Dong,Ozan Baris Mulayim,Sizhe Ma,Yuyang Yuan,Dezhi Hong,Mario Berges,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TS-Skill是一个用于评估时间序列问答中三种组合分析技能（时间尺度选择、时间定位、跨区间整合）的受控基准，通过SKEvol框架构建，实验揭示了现有LLM和TSLM在技能层面存在显著且不均衡的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列问答基准按任务类型或高层推理类别组织，难以诊断模型在信号层面能力的根本缺陷。

Method: 提出TS-Skill基准，评估三种可组合分析技能：SK1（时间尺度选择）、SK2（时间定位）、SK3（跨区间整合）；开发SKEvol框架，包含领域感知时间序列种子生成、技能控制的问题生成、元数据和代码辅助的答案构建、多阶段信号验证及人工参与筛选。

Result: 在10个先进LLM和TSLM上的实验发现模型在SK1-SK3上存在显著且不均衡的能力差距，SK3对非智能体模型持续具有挑战性，而工具增强的智能体在独立SK3任务上展现出选择性优势。

Conclusion: 技能层面的评估能够揭示被聚合TSQA分数掩盖的时间推理失败，表明细粒度技能诊断对于理解模型能力至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and time-series language models (TSLMs) are increasingly applied to time-series question answering (TSQA). Unlike text-only QA, TSQA requires models to ground answers in temporal signals whose patterns may occur at different scales, specific time locations, or across separated intervals. However, existing benchmarks are typically organized by task types or high-level reasoning categories, making it difficult to diagnose the underlying signal-level capabilities driving model performance. We introduce TS-Skill, a controlled benchmark for evaluating three composable analytical skills in TSQA: temporal scale selection (SK1), temporal localization (SK2), and cross-interval integration (SK3). TS-Skill provides timestamp-aware questions, broad domain coverage, and human-validated QA quality. To construct the benchmark at scale, we develop SKEvol, a skill-guided agentic framework that combines domain-aware time-series seed generation, skill-controlled question generation, metadata- and code-assisted answer construction, multi-phase signal-grounded verification, and human-in-the-loop curation. Experiments on ten state-of-the-art LLMs and TSLMs reveal substantial and uneven capability gaps across SK1-SK3. In particular, SK3 remains consistently challenging for non-agent models, whereas tool-augmented agents show a selective advantage on standalone SK3. These findings demonstrate that skill-level evaluation can uncover temporal reasoning failures that are obscured by aggregate TSQA scores.

</details>


### [44] [The Tokenizer Tax Across 25 European Languages: Domain Invariance, Cross-Lingual Few-Shot Effects, and the Ukrainian Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24718)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统测量了10种基础模型在25种欧洲语言上的分词器生育率（每词token数），发现非英语语言存在2.5倍的隐性成本，并发布了公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词器生育率（每词token数）对非英语NLP施加了隐性成本，但目前缺乏跨语言、跨模型的系统测量与对比。

Method: 在平行文本上测量10种基础模型对25种欧洲语言的分词器生育率，进行亚词分析，并在4种斯拉夫语言上开展跨语言少样本评估。

Result: 生育率从英语的1.2 tokens/word到希腊语/马耳他语的约3.1，跨度达2.5倍；语系层级清晰（罗曼语族1.5-1.7，日耳曼语族1.7-1.9，斯拉夫语族2.2-2.5，乌拉尔/波罗的语族2.7-3.0）；乌克兰语因预训练数据代表性不足，比同源斯拉夫语言高15-18%；生育率排名跨领域不变（rho>0.97）；高生育率tokenizer破坏形态边界；少样本效果是模型固有的，与语言无关。

Conclusion: 分词器生育率是衡量NLP语言公平性的重要指标，揭示了预训练数据代表性不足导致的语言偏见，为改进多语言模型提供实证基础。

Abstract: Tokenizer fertility the number of tokens per word imposes a hidden cost on non-English NLP. We measure fertility for ten foundation models across 25 European languages on parallel text, producing the first controlled tokenizer tax map for the continent. The tax spans 2.5x from English (1.2 tokens/word) to Greek/Maltese (~3.1), following a clear hierarchy: Romance (1.5-1.7), Germanic (1.7-1.9), Slavic (2.2-2.5), Uralic/Baltic (2.7-3.0). Ukrainian (2.7) pays 15-18% more than cognate Slavic languages, reflecting underrepresentation in pre-training data. Fertility rankings are domain-invariant across three text registers (rho > 0.97). A subword analysis reveals that high-fertility tokenizers fragment morphological boundaries rather than preserving them. Cross-lingual few-shot evaluation on four Slavic languages shows that few-shot effects are model-intrinsic, not language-dependent. We release all measurements as a public dataset.

</details>


### [45] [ROC Analysis for Evaluating Translation Quality Estimation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24721)
*Evelyn Y. Garland,Carola F. Berger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用ROC分析来评估自动翻译质量估计（QE）系统的性能，该方法与现行方法结果一致，但能提供支持业务决策的可操作性能洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动翻译质量估计系统的广泛应用需要实用、面向决策的性能评估方法，而现有方法在此方面存在不足。

Method: 采用接收者操作特征（ROC）分析方法对翻译质量估计系统进行性能评估。

Result: ROC分析与当前主流评估方法结果一致，且能提供可操作的性能洞察以支持业务决策。

Conclusion: ROC分析是评估翻译质量估计系统的一种实用且富有优势的方法，尤其适合面向决策的应用场景。

Abstract: The increasing use of automated translation quality estimation (QE) systems calls for practical, decision-oriented methods for evaluating their performance. We propose that Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) analysis is a useful approach for this purpose. Our study shows that ROC analysis not only produces results consistent with currently prevalent methods, but also offers several important advantages, including actionable performance insights that support business decision-making.

</details>


### [46] [StepGap: A Hybrid NLI-LLM Checker for Step-Level Evidence-Gap Detectionin Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24733)
*Yuelyu Ji,Zhuochun Li,Hui Ji,Daqing He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出StepGap混合决策树，通过NLI-LLM协同检测多跳QA中的步骤级证据缺口（矛盾/无关/缺失），在步骤级F1达72.0，并作为GRPO过程奖励提升Qwen2.5-7B的EM性能+3.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答中现有方法难以精确检测步骤级别的证据缺失类型，且LLM-only基线存在竞争误差抵消问题，导致内部诊断不可靠。

Method: 设计混合NLI-LLM决策树StepGap，输出三类标签（矛盾主张CC、无关证据IE、缺失桥梁MB），每类对应具体修复动作。

Result: 在82个多跳问题（181个标注步骤，κ=0.704）上StepGap的sF1=72.0，与LLM-only基线置信区间重叠；消融实验显示StepGap每阶段移除均降低F1，而LLM-only有3/4移除反而提升F1（竞争误差抵消）；作为GRPO过程奖励，Qwen2.5-7B的EM从32.1±0.3提升至35.4±0.9。

Conclusion: StepGap提供了更可分解且可靠的步骤级证据缺口检测结构，避免了LLM-only的竞争误差抵消问题，且能有效作为过程奖励提升多跳推理性能。

Abstract: We present \textbf{StepGap}, a hybrid NLI-LLM decision tree that detects step-level evidence gaps in multi-hop QA and emits one of three typed labels: \textsc{Contradicted Claim} (CC), \textsc{Irrelevant Evidence} (IE), or \textsc{Missing Bridge} (MB), each tied to a concrete repair action. On 82 multi-hop questions (181 annotated steps, $κ{=}0.704$), StepGap reaches sF1$=$72.0, within the bootstrap confidence interval of an LLM-only baseline (70.1) but with a more decomposable structure: every StepGap stage \emph{hurts} F1 when removed, while three of four LLM-only removals \emph{improve} F1 -- a sign of \emph{competing-error cancellation}, where internal stages mask each other's errors. We further expose a \emph{Q-F1 trap}: question-level F1 is mechanically inflated by checkers that flag every step, making step-level F1 the necessary diagnostic. Used as a typed GRPO process reward, StepGap improves Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct Exact Match from $32.1{\pm}0.3$ to $35.4{\pm}0.9$ across three seeds, with the single-run comparison showing a $+5.6$ Avg EM gain over the matched Search-R1 GRPO reproduction.

</details>


### [47] [Who judges the judges? Governance from metrics: a runtime framework for continuous LLM compliance monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24737)
*Jehanne Dussert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于运行时可观测性指标的持续合规治理框架 govllm，通过多监管法官面板（SLM）对 AI 系统进行实时合规评分，并将法官间分歧作为监管不确定性信号引入人工仲裁，以解决 EU AI Act 对持续人工监督和漂移检测的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 合规方法将合规性视为审计时点的二元判定，无法满足 EU AI Act 对部署系统持续人工监督和行为漂移检测的要求，存在结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出"基于指标的治理"原则，将监管合规作为运行时可观测性的连续信号；构建 govllm 框架，实现基于合规评分的治理驱动路由架构；设计由多领域监管标准（EU AI Act、GDPR、ANSSI、可访问性）专家 LLM 法官组成的面板，将法官间分歧重定义为监管不确定性信号；通过 49 条标注提示/回答对在五个监管标准上的评估进行验证。

Result: 四个小语言模型（1.7B-7B 参数）的评分一致率在 51.5%（mistral:7b）到 69.1%（phi4-mini）之间，无单一模型在所有标准上占优；识别出三种结构性故障模式和法官特定位置偏差（降低一致率高达 25 个百分点）。

Conclusion: govllm 作为开源框架发布，支持可复现的 AI 治理研究；多法官面板设计优于单一模型，位置偏差等故障模式需在部署中予以关注。

Abstract: Current approaches to AI compliance treat conformity as a binary, audit-time verdict rather than a continuous, measurable property of production systems. We argue that this compliance fiction is structurally ill-suited to the requirements of the EU AI Act, which demands ongoing human oversight and the detection of emergent behavioural drift in deployed systems. We introduce governance from metrics, a principle whereby regulatory compliance is derived as a continuous signal from runtime observability rather than from static assessments. Building on this principle, we present govllm, an open-source framework implementing a governance-driven routing architecture in which model selection is determined by accumulated compliance scores rather than by latency or cost alone. Central to our approach is a panel of regulatory judges - LLM evaluators specialised per criterion (EU AI Act, GDPR, ANSSI, accessibility) - whose inter-judge disagreement we reframe not as noise but as a regulatory uncertainty signal warranting human arbitration. We validate this approach through a ground truth corpus of 49 annotated prompt/response pairs across five regulatory criteria, evaluated by four small language models (SLMs, 1.7B-7B parameters) running fully on-premise. Agreement rates range from 51.5% (mistral:7b) to 69.1% (phi4-mini), with no single model dominating across all criteria - empirically motivating the Profile-as-jury design. We further document three structural failure modes in small regulatory judges and a judge-specific position bias that degrades agreement by up to 25 percentage points across three question-order conditions (original, reversed, permuted). govllm is released as open-source software to support reproducible AI governance research.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond the Target: From Imitation to Collaboration in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24793)
*Jinze Li,Yixing Xu,Guanchen Li,Jinfeng Xu,Shuo Yang,Yang Zhang,Xuanwu Yin,Dong Li,Edith C. H. Ngai,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoSpec是一种协作式推测解码方法，通过强化学习训练仲裁策略，在推理时选择性接受草稿模型或目标模型的token，在保持加速的同时超越仅使用目标模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码假设目标模型在每个token位置都是更优选择，但实践中草稿模型虽然在整体上更弱，在部分token上却可能做出更正确的预测。现有方法忽略了这种token级的互补性。

Method: 通过强化学习训练一个仲裁策略，在草稿模型与目标模型预测不一致时，决策是接受草稿token还是目标token，核心在于是否有利于得到正确的最终答案。

Result: CoSpec在保持显著加速的同时，超越了仅使用目标模型的性能表现。

Conclusion: CoSpec将推测解码从"模仿"范式转变为"协作"范式，为推测解码提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SPD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by letting a smaller draft model propose multiple future tokens that are verified in parallel by a larger target model. The dominant SPD paradigm treats the target model as the sole reliable teacher, accepting a draft token only when it exactly matches the target prediction. This design implicitly assumes that the target is always the better choice at every position. In practice, this assumption does not hold. Although the draft is the weaker model overall, it is not uniformly inferior at the token level. In a meaningful fraction of cases where draft and target disagree, the draft's choice is the one that leads to the correct final answer. Inspired by this, we introduce \textbf{Collaborative Speculative Decoding (CoSpec)}, a generalization of SPD that no longer treats the target model as the sole token-level authority. CoSpec trains an arbitration policy via reinforcement learning to decide whether to accept tokens from the draft or target model, selectively accepting draft tokens at mismatches when doing so is likely to yield a correct final answer. Experimental results show that CoSpec maintains substantial speedups while surpassing target-only performance. By shifting the emphasis from imitation to collaboration, CoSpec suggests a new perspective on speculative decoding.

</details>


### [49] [Repeated Sequences Reveal Gaps between Large Language Models and Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24850)
*Kumiko Tanaka-Ishii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于重复子序列的熵评估框架，通过分析文本中重复子序列的分布及其与Rényi熵的关系，发现人类文本与GPT生成文本在长程统计组织上存在系统性差异，GPT文本的熵增长模式随模型规模呈现显著偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法主要基于任务表现或短上下文行为，无法有效检测LLM生成文本在长程统计组织上的结构差异，缺乏对文本长程结构保留能力的量化诊断工具。

Method: 提出基于重复子序列的互补评估框架，分析不同尺度下重复子序列的分布，将其与高阶Rényi熵关联，在有限长度条件下探测文本对已建立结构的重用模式。对人类文本和长度匹配的GPT生成文本进行实验比较。

Result: 幂律模型仅在有限的块长度范围内适用，而观测到的熵增长通常更符合对数-幂形式。人类文本的熵增长模式在可访问范围内表现稳定，而GPT生成文本的估计指数随模型大小呈现系统性显著偏移。

Conclusion: 重复子序列熵提供了一种量化结构诊断方法，能够揭示长程组织中的系统性差异，有效区分自然语言与最先进LLM输出，超越了表面流畅性层面的评估。

Abstract: Evaluating whether large language models (LLMs) capture the structure of natural language beyond local fluency remains an open challenge. Existing evaluation methods, largely based on task performance or short-context behavior, provide limited insight into the long-range statistical organization of generated text.
  We propose a complementary evaluation framework based on repeated subsequences. By analyzing their distribution across scales and relating it to higher-order Rényi entropies, we probe how texts reuse previously established structure under finite-length conditions. Experiments on human-written texts and length-matched GPT-generated texts show that, while power-law models can describe restricted ranges of block length, the observed entropy growth is often equally or better characterized by logarithmic--power forms.
  Across datasets, natural language exhibits stable entropy-growth patterns over accessible ranges, with consistent average behavior despite variability across individual texts. In contrast, GPT-generated texts show systematic and statistically significant shifts in estimated exponents with model size. These results demonstrate that repeated-subsequence entropy provides a quantitative structural diagnostic that reveals systematic differences in long-range organization, distinguishing natural language from state-of-the-art LLM outputs beyond surface-level fluency.

</details>


### [50] [Lngram: N-gram Conditional Memory in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24869)
*Yunao Zheng,Guoyang Xia,Xiaojie Wang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lngram是一种在隐空间中学习离散符号并进行N-gram查找的条件记忆模块，它解耦了检索与主干的依赖，去除了对tokenizer ID的依赖，在长上下文语言建模、领域知识注入和多模态任务上均优于Transformer和Engram基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer通过密集计算同时处理组合推理和局部知识检索，效率低下；Engram部分解耦了检索与主干，但其基于token的键仍受限于文本token化和哈希压缩。Lngram旨在彻底摆脱tokenizer依赖，并自然扩展到非文本模态。

Method: Lngram提出一种隐空间条件记忆模块，直接从隐藏状态中学习离散符号，并对这些符号执行N-gram查找。该方法去除了对tokenizer ID的依赖，可自然扩展到视觉-语言和视觉-语言-动作等非文本模态。

Result: 在评估设定中，Lngram在长上下文语言建模上持续降低困惑度，后验添加到预训练模型时可有效注入领域知识，联合训练超越全微调。在视觉-语言和视觉-语言-动作任务上均取得整体提升。

Conclusion: LogitLens和CKA分析表明，Lngram使预测相关信息更早涌现，在有限的推理和内存开销下增加了有效深度。代码已开源。

Abstract: Sequence modeling requires both compositional reasoning and local static knowledge retrieval, yet standard Transformers handle both through dense computation. Engram partially decouples retrieval from the backbone, but its token-based keys remain tied to text tokenization and hash compression. We propose Lngram, a latent-space conditional memory module that learns discrete symbols directly from hidden states and performs N-gram lookup over these symbols. This design removes the dependence on tokenizer IDs and naturally extends to non-text modalities. In our evaluated settings, Lngram outperforms Transformer and Engram baselines, consistently reduces perplexity in long-context language modeling, and effectively injects domain knowledge when added post hoc to pretrained models. Joint training with the backbone further surpasses full fine-tuning, while experiments on vision-language and vision-language-action tasks show overall gains. Analyses with LogitLens and CKA suggest that Lngram enables prediction-relevant information to emerge earlier, increasing effective depth with limited inference and memory overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/zyaaa-ux/Lngram.

</details>


### [51] [Towards a Universal Causal Reasoner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24873)
*Qirun Dai,Xiao Liu,Jiawei Zhang,Dylan Zhang,Hao Peng,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniCo，一个覆盖Pearl因果阶梯18种查询类型的因果数据生成框架，通过将符号化因果示例转化为代码和自然语言形式，并基于精确因果推理和去除捷径案例确保数据质量。基于UniCo生成的66.6K数据进行监督微调后，多个LLM在分布内外因果基准上平均提升22.9%和8.1%，并在医学理解、法律判决等实际任务中 faithfulness 提升20.2%，表明因果中心训练不仅增强因果推理能力，还赋予LLM通用推理中的因果思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推理数据工作多聚焦于特定方面的基准测试，缺乏可用于训练通用因果推理器的数据，限制了大语言模型因果推理能力的培养。

Method: 提出UniCo数据生成框架：(1)覆盖Pearl因果阶梯中18种因果查询类型；(2)将符号化因果示例转化为代码和自然语言形式以模拟真实场景；(3)基于精确因果推理进行答案验证，并过滤存在推理捷径的案例。

Result: 基于66.6K UniCo实例进行监督微调后，Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Instruct在18种分布内查询类型上平均提升22.9%，在7个分布外因果基准上超越SOTA方法8.1%；在医学理解、法律判决等实际任务中，faithfulness指标平均提升20.2%。

Conclusion: 因果中心训练不仅能增强大语言模型的因果推理能力，还能赋予模型在通用推理任务中的因果思维，提升推理轨迹的忠实度。

Abstract: Despite the importance of causal reasoning, training LLMs to reason causally remains underexplored. Existing data efforts mostly focus on benchmarking LLMs on specific aspects of causality, making them less suitable for training generalizable causal reasoners. To address this, we propose UniCo, a data generation framework that both (1) addresses 18 causal query types across Pearl's Causal Ladder and (2) translates natively symbolic examples into code and natural language forms to simulate real-world use cases where causal terms are not explicitly specified. To ensure data quality, UniCo grounds answers with exact causal inference and filters cases with reasoning shortcuts. Upon supervised finetuning with 66.6K UniCo-generated instances, Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Instruct achieve an average of 22.9% improvements across all 18 in-distribution query types, and 8.1% over state-of-the-art causal data generation frameworks on 7 established causal benchmarks outside the training distribution. More importantly, in real-world medical understanding, legal decision, and tabular reasoning, UniCo-trained models consistently display more faithful reasoning traces, outperforming the base models by an average of 20.2% in faithfulness metrics. These suggest that causality-centered training not only strengthens causal reasoning, but also equips LLMs with a causal mindset in general reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [52] [DTO: a Differentiable Training Objective for Effective Counterfactual Story Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24885)
*Amelia Girard,Massimo Piccardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微训练目标（DTO）用于反事实故事改写任务，通过端到端反向传播优化忠实度与语义一致性，在TimeTravel和ART数据集上超越了最大似然基线和偏好学习方法，与当代大语言模型表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实故事改写中所需修改通常规模很小且高度局部化，传统最大似然训练目标容易忽略这些细微变化，而基于强化学习的训练方法则速度慢且难以设置，因此需要一种更高效且有效的训练方法。

Method: 提出可微训练目标（DTO），通过端到端反向传播微调Transformer模型，损失函数联合奖励两个方面：(i) 对参考改写的忠实度，(ii) 与源叙事的语义一致性。

Result: 在TimeTravel和ART数据集上的实验表明，DTO超越了最大似然基线和偏好学习方法，与两个当代大语言模型在所有评估指标上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 任务特定的可微目标函数对于细微、受控的文本生成任务是有效的。

Abstract: Counterfactual story rewriting is a natural language processing task that requires updating an existing story to reflect a chosen alternative event, yet preserving all the unaffected storyline elements and overall coherence. While large language models have recently made remarkable progress on this task, it still remains challenging since the required modifications are typically very small in size and highly localized. As a consequence, models trained in a conventional manner with the maximum-likelihood training objective tend to overlook these nuances. At the same time, more sophisticated training approaches based on reinforcement learning are notoriously slow and difficult to set up. For these reasons, our paper proposes a novel, differentiable training objective (DTO) that directly optimizes for the requisite counterfactual improvements. In our approach, a transformer model is fine-tuned via end-to-end backpropagation against a fully differentiable loss function that jointly rewards (i) fidelity to the reference rewrite and (ii) semantic consistency with the source narrative. The empirical evaluation on the TimeTravel and ART datasets shows that the proposed DTO approach has been able to surpass a maximum-likelihood baseline and a preference-based approach, and perform competitively against two contemporary large language models in all evaluation metrics. These findings substantiate the effectiveness of task-specific differentiable objectives for nuanced, controlled text-generation tasks.

</details>


### [53] [When Reasoning Hurts: Source-Aware Evaluation of Frontier LLMs for Clinical SOAP Note Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24902)
*Faizan Faisal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理增强型LLM在临床SOAP笔记生成任务中的表现，发现在受控实验中，非推理版本的GPT-5.4质量最高，而启用推理反而显著降低了GPT-5.4的性能，表明强推理能力并不必然转化为对保真度敏感的临床文档生成任务的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理增强型LLM在医疗推理基准上表现强劲，但它们是否能将这些能力迁移到结构化临床文档（如SOAP笔记）生成任务中尚不明确，因此需要专门评估。

Method: 采用2x2受控实验设计，独立切换提供者原生推理（provider-native reasoning）和同源检索增强生成（same-source RAG），在OMI Health、ACI-Bench和PriMock57三个数据集上评估GPT-5.4、DeepSeek-V4-Flash和Gemma-4-E4B三个模型，使用7项自动指标和2个参考感知的LLM裁判进行评估。

Result: 非推理配置的GPT-5.4在所有三个数据集上总体质量最高；DeepSeek-V4-Flash在推理增强配置中表现最佳；启用推理显著降低了GPT-5.4的性能；同源RAG带来较小且模型依赖的改进。

Conclusion: 不能假定更强的推理能力会自动提升对保真度敏感的SOAP笔记生成质量，需要进行专门的、任务特定的评估。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled LLMs perform strongly on medical reasoning benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether these gains transfer to structured clinical documentation; we investigate this question using SOAP note generation from clinical dialogue in a source-aware benchmark spanning OMI Health, ACI-Bench, and PriMock57. We evaluate GPT-5.4, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and Gemma-4-E4B in a controlled 2x2 design that independently toggles provider-native reasoning and same-source retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Outputs are assessed using seven automatic metrics alongside two reference-aware LLM judges. Both evaluation approaches agree that a non-reasoning GPT-5.4 configuration achieves the highest overall quality, while DeepSeek-V4-Flash performs best among reasoning-enabled configurations. Enabling reasoning significantly degrades GPT-5.4 performance across all three datasets, whereas same-source RAG yields smaller, model-dependent improvements. Overall, the findings indicate that stronger reasoning capability should not be assumed to improve fidelity-sensitive SOAP note generation without dedicated, task-specific evaluation.

</details>


### [54] [Quantifying the Impact of Translation Errors on Multilingual LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24904)
*Klaudia-Doris Thellmann,Bernhard Stadler,Michael Färber,Jens Lehmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 机器翻译基准中的翻译错误会显著影响LLM评估结果，且现有自动错误检测工具与人类标注一致性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译基准广泛用于评估LLM多语言能力，但其中的翻译错误对评估可靠性的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 比较LLM评判器和xCOMET-XXL基线自动生成的MQM风格错误跨度与专家人工标注的一致性，并分析翻译错误对基准准确率下降的解释程度。

Result: 自动错误跨度与人工标注的一致性在自然发生的基准翻译中并不理想；目标端翻译错误与准确率可测量的百分点下降显著相关。

Conclusion: 翻译错误是导致多语言基准评估准确率下降的重要因素，需要更可靠的翻译质量评估方法。

Abstract: Machine-translated benchmarks are widely used to assess the multilingual capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet translation errors in these benchmarks remain underexplored, raising concerns about the reliability and comparability of multilingual evaluation. We address two practical gaps: (i) how well automatic MQM-style error spans from LLM judges and a span-aware QE baseline (xCOMET-XXL) match expert human span annotations on benchmark translations, and (ii) how strongly translation errors (as opposed to source-side issues in the English original) explain accuracy drops on translated benchmarks. We find that span agreement is non-trivial on naturally occurring benchmark translations, and that target-side translation errors are consistently associated with measurable, percentage-point drops in translated accuracy even after controlling for English correctness and source-side anomalies.

</details>


### [55] [Overview of the PsyDefDetect Shared Task at BioNLP 2026: Detecting Levels of Psychological Defense Mechanisms in Supportive Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24907)
*Hongbin Na,Zimu Wang,Zhaoming Chen,Yining Hua,Rena Gao,Kailai Yang,Ling Chen,Wei Wang,Shaoxiong Ji,John Torous,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PsyDefDetect共享任务——在情感支持对话中检测心理防御机制水平。任务基于临床验证的DMRS框架，将求助者话语分为9个类别。最佳系统宏F1为0.420，存在类别不平衡和过度预测高适应性类别的问题，LLM和理论感知方法表现较好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床心理学中，心理防御机制是评估个体心理健康的重要指标，但NLP领域对其的自动化检测研究尚属空白。本文旨在推动临床心理学与NLP的交叉研究，为情感支持对话中的心理状态评估提供自动化工具。

Method: 构建PsyDefConv语料库（200段对话，2336条求助者话语），基于DMRS框架进行九分类标注（7个防御水平+2个辅助标签）。通过CodaBench平台组织共享任务，吸引172名参与者提交563份结果，21支队伍正式排名。

Result: 最佳系统宏F1为0.420，远超数据集论文中微调基线的表现，但仍存在较大提升空间。分析发现：①模型倾向于过度预测多数类（高适应性）；②准确率与宏F1差距扩大，揭示类别不平衡敏感性；③理论感知方法和LLM方法在细粒度防御功能分类中表现更优。

Conclusion: 心理防御机制自动检测仍具挑战性，类别不平衡和多数类偏置是主要障碍；LLM和理论感知方法具有潜力；该任务为临床心理学与NLP交叉领域提供了基准资源和评估框架，所有材料已公开。

Abstract: We present an overview of PsyDefDetect, the shared task on detecting levels of psychological defense mechanisms in emotional support dialogues, co-located with BioNLP@ACL 2026. Grounded in the clinically validated Defense Mechanism Rating Scales (DMRS) framework, the task asks systems to classify a target seeker utterance, given its preceding dialogue context, into one of nine categories: seven hierarchical DMRS levels plus two auxiliary labels. Participants worked on PsyDefConv, a newly released corpus of 200 dialogues and 2336 help-seeker utterances annotated under DMRS with substantial inter-annotator agreement. The task attracted 172 participants on CodaBench who produced 563 submissions, with 21 teams officially registering their results for the final ranking. The best system achieved a macro F1-score of 0.420, surpassing the strongest fine-tuned baseline reported in the dataset paper by a notable margin, yet leaving clear headroom. Our analysis highlights (i) a persistent tendency to over-predict the majority High-Adaptive class, (ii) a widening gap between accuracy and macro-F1 that reveals class-imbalance sensitivity, and (iii) the value of theory-aware and LLM-based approaches for fine-grained defensive-function classification. We release all task materials and invite the community to continue work on this novel intersection of clinical psychology and NLP.

</details>


### [56] [MultiHaluDet: Multilingual Hallucination Detection via LLM Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24919)
*Riasad Alvi,Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MultiHaluDet 是一个三阶段堆叠框架，通过探测冻结 LLM 的完整隐藏状态轨迹来检测多语言幻觉，无需语言特定微调，在英文基准上达到 98.55% AUROC，并成功跨高、中、低资源语言泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 中的幻觉问题严重阻碍了其可靠部署，且在非英语和资源受限的语言环境中尤为突出。现有的基于输出置信度启发式或单层内部表示的检测方法，难以捕捉跨语言的深层事实不一致性。

Method: 提出 MultiHaluDet，一种三阶段堆叠框架：(1) 提取冻结 LLM 多层隐藏状态的序列特征；(2) 使用多尺度注意力和自注意力池化的混合架构处理特征；(3) 生成 out-of-fold 嵌入并输入校准的经典分类器集成，捕获细粒度和粗粒度的事实不一致模式。

Result: 在英文 HaluEval 和 TriviaQA 基准上，使用 Mistral-7B 和 LLaMA2-7B 架构达到 98.55% AUROC 的 SOTA 检测性能。在跨语言泛化评估中，对高资源（法语）、中资源（孟加拉语）和低资源（阿姆哈拉语）语言均持续优于基线。

Conclusion: MultiHaluDet 展现出卓越的表征鲁棒性，能够成功地将幻觉检测能力跨类型多样的语言层级进行迁移，为无需语言特定微调的多语言幻觉检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) represent a critical barrier to their reliable deployment, a vulnerability heavily exacerbated in non-English and resource-constrained contexts. Existing detection approaches that rely on output confidence heuristics or single-layer internal representations frequently fail to capture deep, complex factual inconsistencies across diverse languages. To address this, we introduce MultiHaluDet, a novel three-stage stacking framework that detects multilingual hallucinations by probing the full hidden state trajectories of frozen LLMs without requiring language-specific fine-tuning. Our method extracts sequential features across multiple layers and processes them via a hybrid architecture using multi-scale attention and self-attention pooling. By generating out-of-fold embeddings that feed into a calibrated classical classifier ensemble, MultiHaluDet captures both fine-grained and coarse-grained patterns of factual inconsistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art detection performance, reaching up to 98.55% AUROC on the English HaluEval and TriviaQA benchmarks using Mistral-7B and LLaMA2-7B architectures. Crucially, we rigorously evaluate our framework's cross-lingual generalization across high (French), medium (Bangla), and low-resource (Amharic) languages. MultiHaluDet demonstrates exceptional representational robustness, consistently outperforming baselines and successfully transferring hallucination detection capabilities across typologically diverse linguistic tiers.

</details>


### [57] [H$^{2}$MT: Semantic Hierarchy-Aware Hierarchical Memory Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24930)
*Maryam Haghifam,Zifan He,Jason Cong,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: H²MT 是一种面向长上下文推理的结构感知方法，通过离线构建语义层次结构并采用自底向上的后序聚合计算节点记忆嵌入，在推理时由粗到细地路由查询以早期剪枝无关分支。在 LongBench QA 和结构化技术文档任务上，H²MT 相比提示压缩、记忆令牌方法和检索增强生成基线，在更低的峰值 GPU 内存和首令牌延迟下实现了有竞争力的 ROUGE-L 和 F1 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer LLM 在处理长输入时面临上下文窗口有限、预填充延迟和内存随提示长度快速增长的问题。平面令牌流处理、基于块的检索以及离线索引 RAG 方法存在计算浪费、上下文预算消耗大、外部存储管理开销高以及增加预填充成本等不足。

Method: H²MT 离线构建语义层次结构，通过自底向上的后序聚合为每个节点计算记忆嵌入；推理时采用由粗到细的路由策略，早期剪枝与查询无关的分支，实现结构感知的长上下文推理。

Result: 在 LongBench QA（NarrativeQA、HotpotQA、QASPER）和两个结构化技术文档场景下，H²MT 相比提示压缩、记忆令牌方法和 RAG 基线，实现了更低的峰值 GPU 内存和首令牌延迟，同时保持有竞争力的 ROUGE-L 和 F1 分数，展现了良好的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: H²MT 通过语义层次结构实现了高效的长上下文推理，在保证质量的同时显著降低了计算和内存开销，为长文本处理提供了一种有前景的结构感知方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based LLMs achieve strong results on many language tasks; however, long inputs remain challenging because context windows are finite, and prefill latency and memory grow rapidly with prompt length. Flat token-stream processing and chunk-based retrieval can therefore spend substantial computation and context budget on text unrelated to the query. Offline-indexed RAG additionally introduces external storage and index management overhead, and typically appends retrieved evidence as raw text, increasing prefill cost and latency. H^{2}MT makes long-context inference structure-aware: it builds a semantic hierarchy offline, computes a memory embedding for each node via bottom-up post-order aggregation, and routes queries coarse-to-fine at inference to prune irrelevant branches early. On LongBench QA (NarrativeQA, HotpotQA, QASPER) and two structured technical-document settings, H MT achieves favorable quality efficiency trade-offs, delivering competitive ROUGE-L and F1 (where applicable) with lower peak GPU memory and time-to-first-token (TTFT) than prompt compression, memory-token methods, and retrieval-augmented generation baselines.

</details>


### [58] [NITP: Next Implicit Token Prediction for LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24956)
*Xiangdong Zhang,Debing Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaohan Qin,Yu Cheng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP)，通过在表示空间中引入密集连续监督来增强标准离散的下一词预测，从而缓解潜在表示空间的欠约束问题。实验表明，在0.5B到9B参数的密集和MoE模型上，NITP以约2%的额外训练FLOPs和零推理开销持续提升下游性能，9B MoE模型在MMLU-Pro上提升5.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准下一词预测仅通过输出logit空间的离散标签监督语言模型，这种稀疏的one-hot监督导致潜在表示空间存在欠约束自由度，使得隐状态容易退化到各向异性的病态构型，从而限制模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP)，在离散预测之外，额外在表示空间中进行密集连续监督。具体地，训练模型预测下一token的隐式语义内容，使用同一模型的浅层表示作为稳定的自监督目标，从而对优化景观进行正则化。

Result: 在0.5B到9B参数的密集和MoE模型上，NITP持续提升下游性能。9B MoE模型在MMLU-Pro上绝对提升5.7%，C3提升6.4%，CommonsenseQA提升4.3%，仅增加约2%的训练FLOPs，且无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: NITP通过密集连续监督有效约束了表示空间，鼓励更紧凑、结构化的表示几何，从而提升语言模型的泛化能力和下游性能，且计算开销极小。

Abstract: Standard next-token prediction (NTP) supervises language models solely through discrete labels in the output logit space. We argue that this sparse one-hot supervision leaves the latent representation space under-constrained, allowing hidden states to drift into degenerate and anisotropic configurations that can limit generalization. To address this issue, we propose Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP), which augments discrete prediction with dense continuous supervision directly in the representation space. NITP trains the model to predict the implicit semantic content of the next token, using shallow-layer representations from the same model as stable self-supervised targets. We provide theoretical analysis showing that NITP regularizes the optimization landscape by mitigating under-constrained degrees of freedom and encouraging a compact, structured representation geometry. Empirically, across dense and MoE models ranging from 0.5B to 9B parameters, NITP consistently improves downstream performance with negligible computational overhead. On a 9B MoE model, NITP achieves a 5.7% absolute improvement on MMLU-Pro, along with gains of 6.4% on C3 and 4.3% on CommonsenseQA, with approximately 2% additional training FLOPs and no additional inference cost. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/aHapBean/NITP.

</details>


### [59] [Investigating the Interplay between Contextual and Parametric Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness under Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24960)
*Jingyi Sun,Qianli Wang,Pepa Atanasova,Nils Feldhus,Isabelle Augenstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架FaithMate，研究思维链(CoT)忠实性的两种范式（上下文忠实性与参数忠实性）之间的关系，发现两者正相关但不对称：优化参数忠实性可同时提升两种范式，而上下文忠实性的提升则更具变异性，且在上下文范式内部不同指标间迁移性差，表明CoT忠实性并非单一目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT忠实性评估分为上下文忠实性和参数忠实性两种独立范式，但此前工作仅进行描述性比较，缺乏对两者之间相互作用的系统性研究。

Method: 提出FaithMate，一个统一的偏好对齐接口，用于优化模型朝向任一忠实性范式。通过在三个模型、两个数据集和六个忠实性指标上进行实验，系统分析两种范式之间的增益泛化情况。

Result: 两种范式正相关但不对称：优化参数忠实性可同时提升两种范式的忠实性；优化上下文忠实性带来的增益更不稳定；上下文范式内部不同指标间增益不能一致迁移。

Conclusion: CoT忠实性不是单一目标，需要多面优化和评估。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) faithfulness, i.e., whether CoTs genuinely reflect large language models' (LLM) underlying behavior, is typically evaluated under two disjoint paradigms: contextual faithfulness, measured by perturbing the input or CoT trace, and parametric faithfulness, assessed by intervening on a model's parametric knowledge. Yet prior work compares them only descriptively. We fill this gap by proposing FaithMate, a unified preference-alignment interface for optimizing models towards either faithfulness paradigm. It enables us to investigate the interplay between the two paradigms, examining whether and to what extent faithfulness gains generalize within and across paradigms. Across three models, two datasets, and six faithfulness metrics, we find that the two paradigms are positively coupled, yet asymmetric: optimizing towards parametric faithfulness yields consistent gains across both paradigms, whereas the contextual counterpart delivers more variable gains. Within the contextual paradigm, faithfulness gains on one metric do not consistently transfer to others, implying that existing contextual metrics capture disjoint facets of faithfulness and exposing inherent trade-offs. These findings imply that CoT faithfulness is not a monolithic objective and therefore requires multifaceted optimization and evaluation.

</details>


### [60] [Large Language Model Selection with Limited Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24981)
*Yavuz Durmazkeser,Patrik Okanovic,Andreas Kirsch,Torsten Hoefler,Nezihe Merve Gürel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SELECT-LLM是一个基于主动学习和期望信息增益的框架，通过少量最具信息量的查询注释即可高效选择最佳大语言模型，无需访问模型权重或架构，在23个数据集、156个模型上实现高达84.78%的标注成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估依赖固定评估集上的昂贵人工注释，当需从众多强候选模型中为特定任务选择最佳模型时，成本过高且效率低下。

Method: 提出基于期望信息增益的查询选择规则，利用候选模型输出之间的成对相似性计算信息量，仅需模型生成响应，不依赖模型架构或权重。

Result: 在23个数据集、156个评估模型上，SELECT-LLM在所有设置中均优于最强基线，最佳模型选择标注成本降低达81.8%，近最佳模型选择成本降低达84.78%。

Conclusion: SELECT-LLM是首个面向LLM的主动模型选择框架，能高效识别最佳模型，适用于开源和闭源黑盒模型，显著降低评估成本。

Abstract: Choosing a Large Language Model (LLM) for a given task requires comparing many strong candidates, yet standard evaluation relies on costly annotations over fixed evaluation sets. To address this challenge, we develop SELECT-LLM, the first framework for active model selection of LLMs. SELECT-LLM aims to find a small set of queries whose annotations are most informative for identifying the best LLM for a given task. To this end, we introduce a query selection rule based on expected information gain, computed from pairwise similarities between candidate model outputs. Because this rule only uses generated model responses, SELECT-LLM can be applied across candidate models without assumptions about their architecture or access to model weights. This makes it suitable for both open-weight and black-box LLMs. We evaluate SELECT-LLM across 23 datasets, 156 evaluated models, diverse task families, and multiple text evaluation metrics. Across all experiments, SELECT-LLM improves over the strongest baseline in every setting, with annotation cost reductions up to 81.8% for best model selection and up to 84.78% for near-best model selection.

</details>


### [61] [Exploring Profiles of Cognitive Distortions Associated with Mental Health Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24996)
*Alina Anikejeva,Kairit Sirts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种心理健康状况下的认知扭曲模式，使用基于n-gram和微调Transformer模型分析了包含九个自报心理健康群体和对照组的Reddit数据集。结果表明，心理健康群体（整体和个体）的认知扭曲发生率均高于对照组，效应量从小到中等。不同状况间的扭曲模式大致相似，但某些群体总体扭曲水平更高。研究发现简单的词汇方法可用于大规模心理健康文本数据的群体趋势探索性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知扭曲与多种心理健康障碍相关，但现有研究主要聚焦于抑郁症。本文旨在探索多种心理健康状况下的认知扭曲特征，填补对抑郁症以外其他精神障碍认知扭曲研究的空白。

Method: 使用基于n-gram的方法和微调Transformer模型，对包含九个自报心理健康群体和对照组的Reddit数据集进行认知扭曲检测与分析。

Result: 心理健康群体（整体和个体）的认知扭曲发生率均高于对照组，效应量从小到中等。不同状况间的扭曲模式大致相似，但某些群体总体扭曲水平更高。

Conclusion: 简单的词汇方法（如n-gram）可用于大规模心理健康文本数据中群体水平的认知扭曲趋势探索性分析，为计算心理健康研究提供了方法学支持。

Abstract: Cognitive distortions, distorted patterns of thinking, have been increasingly studied in computational mental health research. Although they are related to many, if not all, mental health disorders, most existing studies focus primarily on depression. In this work, we explore distortion profiles across multiple mental health conditions. We analyzed a large Reddit-based dataset containing posts from nine self-reported mental health groups as well as a control group using both an n-gram-based method and a fine-tuned transformer model for detecting cognitive distortions. Mental health groups, both when pooled together and when examined individually, showed higher prevalence of cognitive distortions compared to the control group, with the effect sizes ranging from small to moderate. When comparing distortion profiles across conditions, we observed largely similar patterns, although some groups exhibited overall higher levels of distortions than others. These findings suggest that relatively simple lexical approaches can be useful for exploratory analyses of group-level trends in large-scale mental health text data.

</details>


### [62] [Better, Faster: Harnessing Self-Improvement in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24998)
*Qihuang Zhong,Liang Ding,Juhua Liu,Bo Du,Leszek Rutkowski,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HSIR通过验证后退出采样策略解决数据不平衡问题，并设计内在多样性分数过滤过度思考样本，有效提升大推理模型的自我改进效果，在多个后训练范式下实现最高+10.9%性能提升和42.4%推理开销降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进训练方法在复杂推理任务中表现不佳甚至导致模型崩溃，主要存在两个问题：(1)数据不平衡——简单样本过多而关键挑战性样本稀缺；(2)过度思考——包含大量冗余推理步骤的不理想样本被用于自我训练。

Method: 提出HSIR框架，包含两个核心策略：(1)验证后退出采样策略(verify-then-exit)，高效收集困难查询的准确解以缓解数据不平衡；(2)内在多样性分数(Intrinsic Diversity score)，量化过度思考程度并过滤冗余解。此外，提出H-GRPO算法，将内在多样性作为外部奖励融入GRPO强化学习，鼓励简洁多样的推理。

Result: 在多种后训练范式下，HSIR平均带来最高+10.9%的性能提升，同时将相对推理开销降低最多42.4%。

Conclusion: HSIR不仅有效增强了推理性能，还显著提升了推理效率，验证了通过解决数据不平衡和过度思考问题来改进自我训练范式的有效性。

Abstract: Self-improvement training enables the large reasoning models (LRMs) to improve themselves by self-generating reasoning trajectories as training data without external supervision. However, we find that this method often falls short in complex reasoning tasks and even leads to model collapse. Through a series of preliminary analyses, we reveal two problems: (1) data imbalance, where most training samples are simple, but the challenging yet crucial samples are scarce; (2) overthinking, where many undesired samples with redundant reasoning steps are used for self-training. To this end, we propose HSIR, which effectively Harnesses Self-Improvement in large Reasoning models via two simple-yet-effective approaches. Specifically, HSIR introduces a verify-then-exit sampling strategy to mitigate data imbalance by efficiently collecting more accurate solutions for difficult queries, and designs an Intrinsic Diversity score to quantify overthinking and filter out the undesired solutions. We apply HSIR to various post-training paradigms, among which we further propose H-GRPO, an enhanced GRPO algorithm that leverages the intrinsic diversity as an external reward to encourage concise and diverse reasoning via reinforcement learning. Extensive results show that HSIR not only effectively enhances the reasoning performance, i.e., bringing up to +10.9% average performance gains, but also significantly improves the reasoning efficiency by reducing up to 42.4% relative inference overhead.

</details>


### [63] [Language Bias in LVLMs: From In-Depth Analysis to Simple and Effective Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25036)
*Yangneng Chen,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大视觉语言模型中的语言偏置问题，发现其根源在于训练过程中的模态不对齐。提出两种简单有效的方法：语言偏置正则化（LBR）和语言偏置惩罚（LBP），分别用于指令微调和直接偏好优化阶段，在不引入额外数据或模型的情况下，有效缓解语言偏置、减少幻觉并提升模型对齐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs存在幻觉问题，已有研究将其归因于语言偏置（模型过度依赖文本而忽略视觉输入），但大多停留在经验分析层面，缺乏对语言偏置根本原因的深入探究。

Method: 1. 系统分析语言偏置的根本原因：训练过程中的模态不对齐，即视觉指令微调（VIT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）往往优先改进文本能力，导致模型偏向语言建模而非均衡的多模态理解。2. 提出LBR：在指令微调阶段通过正则化缓解语言偏置。3. 提出LBP：在DPO训练过程中对语言偏置施加惩罚。

Result: LBR在十余个通用基准上持续提升性能；LBP显著减少幻觉、提高可信度。两种方法均无需引入额外数据或辅助模型。

Conclusion: 本文不仅揭示了语言偏置的根源在于模态不对齐，还提出了有效且轻量的解决方案，推动了LVLMs的整体对齐性提升。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) extend large language models with visual understanding, but remain vulnerable to hallucination, where outputs are fluent yet inconsistent with images. Recent studies link this issue to language bias-the tendency of LVLMs to over-rely on text while neglecting visual inputs. Yet most analyses remain empirical without uncovering its underlying cause. In this paper, we provide a systematic study of language bias and identify its root in modality misalignment during training. Our analysis shows that both Visual Instruction Tuning (VIT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) often prioritize textual improvements, which may cause LVLMs to overly lean toward language modeling rather than balanced multimodal understanding. To address this, we propose two simple yet effective methods: Language Bias Regularization (LBR) which mitigates language bias through regularization during instruction tuning, and Language Bias Penalty (LBP), which penalizes language bias in the DPO training process. Extensive experiments across diverse models and benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. LBR consistently improves performance on over ten general benchmarks, while LBP significantly reduces hallucination and improves trustworthiness. Together, these methods not only mitigate language bias but also advance the overall alignment of LVLMs, all without introducing any additional data or auxiliary models. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/lab-klc/LVLM-Language-Bias.

</details>


### [64] [TRACE: A taxonomy-grounded synthetic dataset for teaching-program generation and session interpretation in Applied Behavior Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25038)
*Festus Kahunla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE是一个由2,999个合成样本组成的ABA临床指令微调数据集，覆盖教学程序生成和多会话行为解读两大任务，每个样本由基于ABA文献的分类树确定性生成器产生，并附带完整的采样来源信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABA临床文档具有高体量、高形式化的特点，但真实会话数据受HIPAA和职业保密规则保护，无法公开用于训练语料库，因此需要合成数据来支持NLU技术在ABA领域的应用研究。

Method: 采用确定性分类树驱动的数据生成器，基于ABA经典文献构建分类体系，自动生成覆盖离散试训、自然情境教学、任务分析三种教学程序类型，以及12种轨迹模式和13种目标行为的会话数据，每个样本均记录完整的采样来源信息。

Result: 生成了2,999个合成样本，划分为训练集(2,549)、验证集(149)、测试集(281)和合理性检查集(20)，数据集以CC BY-NC 4.0协议发布，代码以MIT协议发布。

Conclusion: TRACE为ABA领域的自然语言处理研究提供了首个公开可用的指令微调数据集，但需注意该数据集为研究性产物，尚未经过临床验证。

Abstract: Applied Behavior Analysis (ABA) is a clinical discipline whose documentation, teaching programs and multi-session behavioral logs, is formulaic and high-volume, yet real session data is HIPAA-protected and bound by professional confidentiality rules, blocking the release of a training corpus. We present TRACE (Taxonomy-Referenced ABA Clinical Examples), a 2,999-example synthetic instruction-tuning dataset covering two ABA tasks: teaching-program generation across Discrete Trial Training, Natural Environment Teaching, and Task Analysis; and multi-session behavioral interpretation across twelve trajectory patterns and thirteen target behaviors. Every example is produced by a deterministic taxonomy-driven generator grounded in the canonical ABA literature, and every example carries complete sampling provenance, the exact taxonomy cells that produced it. The dataset is released under CC BY-NC 4.0 for data and MIT for code, with stratified train (2,549), validation (149), test (281), and sanity (20) splits. TRACE is a research artifact and has not been clinically validated.

</details>


### [65] [LLM Agent Based Renewable Energy Forecasting Using Edge and IoT Data A Review of Solar Wind Weather and Grid Aware Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25141)
*Pavan Manjunath,Thomas Pruefer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了如何利用大语言模型（LLM）智能体增强可再生能源预测，通过整合异构传感器流、天气API数据、历史发电记录和电网约束，构建统一决策支持工作流，并提出了六层分类法和十二项开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测管线无法充分利用IoT和边缘设备产生的海量实时运行数据，亟需新方法提升可再生能源发电预测的可靠性与智能化水平。

Method: 系统综述经典方法（统计时间序列、深度学习、物理混合方法）与新兴LLM智能体框架，提出六层分类体系（数据采集、预处理、特征工程、模型推理、不确定性估计、自然语言报告），识别十二项开放挑战。

Result: LLM智能体能够通过集成异构数据源和上下文推理实现更准确的预测与不确定性沟通，但面临实时部署、模型漂移、幻觉控制、边缘硬件互操作性等挑战。

Conclusion: 建议未来研究聚焦于开放基准测试、物理信息LLM地面实况核对和联邦预测架构。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of renewable energy generation is a foundational requirement for grid stability energy trading battery scheduling and carbon aware operational planning Solar and wind resources are inherently intermittent their output fluctuates with cloud cover wind speed atmospheric turbulence seasonal patterns and local terrain The proliferation of IoT and edge devices spanning smart meters inverters anemometers pyranometers weather stations and grid interface sensors has created an unprecedented volume of real time operational data that conventional forecasting pipelines are ill equipped to exploit fully This review investigates how large language model LLM agents can enhance renewable energy forecasting by integrating heterogeneous sensor streams weather API data historical generation records grid constraints and contextual reasoning into unified decision support workflows We survey classical forecasting methods statistical time series models deep learning architectures physics hybrid approaches and emerging LLM agent frameworks for explanation uncertainty communication and operator guidance A six layer taxonomy is proposed covering data acquisition preprocessing feature engineering model inference uncertainty estimation and natural language reporting The review identifies twelve open challenges spanning real time deployment model drift under distribution shift uncertainty quantification hallucination control in LLM agents interoperability of edge hardware and integration with energy management systems The paper concludes by recommending a research agenda centred on open benchmarks physics informed LLM grounding and federated forecasting architectures

</details>


### [66] [STREAM: A Data-Centric Framework for Mining High-Value Task-Oriented Dialogues from Streaming Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25162)
*Liang Xue,Haoyu Liu,Cheng Wang,Pengyu Chen,Haozhuo Zheng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Stream框架，利用流媒体（直播/短视频）大规模合成高质量垂直领域服务对话数据，发布包含87,498个会话、149.7万轮次的StreamDial数据集，在对话质量和状态追踪上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直领域大语言模型受限于复杂、领域特定任务导向对话数据的稀缺。现有数据获取面临三难困境：专家标注昂贵、真实服务对话受隐私和商业限制、静态语料会过时。

Method: Stream框架通过流媒体挖掘真实交互信号，结合角色接地的人物构建（role-grounded persona construction）与对话蓝图构建（Conversational Blueprint construction）合成对话，并采用检索增强生成（RAG）支持知识感知回复。数据组织为结构化四元组 ⟨P_u, P_a, B, H⟩。

Result: StreamDial包含87,498个对话会话、1,497,320轮次，平均每会话17.11轮，覆盖汽车、餐厅、酒店三个领域。自动评估和下游任务显示StreamDial提升内在对话质量，增强对话状态跟踪；在Qwen3-8B上实现可控训练预算下的多语言迁移。

Conclusion: Stream框架有效解决了垂直领域对话数据的三难困境，利用公开流媒体资源合成高质量、时效性强的大规模对话数据集，为垂直领域对话AI提供了可行的数据方案。

Abstract: Large language models for vertical domains are bottlenecked by the scarcity of complex, domain-specific task-oriented dialogues. Existing data acquisition pipelines face a persistent trilemma: expert annotation is expensive, real-world service conversations are constrained by privacy and commercial restrictions, and static corpora quickly become temporally stale. We propose Stream, a data-centric framework that leverages publicly available streaming media (live streams and short videos) to synthesize high-value service dialogues at scale. Stream mines authentic interaction signals from noisy streams and synthesizes conversations by integrating role-grounded persona construction with Conversational Blueprint construction; it further adopts retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to support knowledge-aware responses. Based on Stream, we release StreamDial, a large-scale multi-domain dataset covering Automotive, Restaurant, and Hotel. StreamDial contains 87,498 dialogue sessions and 1,497,320 turns in total, with an average of 17.11 turns per session and a comparable scale across domains. Each session is organized as a structured quadruplet $\langle P_u, P_a, B, H \rangle$ that pairs dialogue history with explicit user/agent personas and a Conversational Blueprint, capturing realistic service behaviors such as requirement mining, constraint conflicts, negotiation, and recovery. Evaluations with automatic judges and downstream tasks show that StreamDial improves intrinsic dialogue quality over strong baselines, and models trained with StreamDial improve Dialogue State Tracking across backbones; we further report a completed human-evaluation set and encouraging multilingual transfer on Qwen3-8B under a controlled training budget. The data is released in https://github.com/hitxueliang/DialogDataSetBySTREAM.

</details>


### [67] [Re-defining Humor Data Objects for AI Humor Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25171)
*Anna Arnett,Bang Nguyen,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将幽默视为一种社会互动行为，通过改进提示工程让LLM生成更高质量的幽默解释，为AI幽默研究的数据合成与增强奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI幽默研究大多将幽默简单地二分为"存在"或"不存在"，忽略了幽默作为社会互动的语境和解释维度，需要更细粒度的幽默推理理解方法。

Method: 定义了幽默推理数据对象，迭代设计LLM提示词（从早期版本到改进版本），通过处理缺失上下文、多模态和转录问题来提升幽默解释质量，并大规模生成数据对象。

Result: 改进后的提示词版本显著减少了关键错误，生成了大量可用于数据合成和增强的幽默推理数据对象。

Conclusion: 更好的LLM提示能够提高幽默解释质量，建立AI理解幽默作为社会行为的坚实基础，为未来研究铺平道路。

Abstract: In most existing AI humor research, humor was treated as either "present" or "not present." We explore the concept of humor as a social interaction with context and explanations. During this project, we defined a humor reasoning data object and developed a way to prompt LLMs to generate an explanation of humor effective for general population. We iterated from an earlier prompt to an improved prompt, found that the later version reduced important errors, and then scaled generation to a large number of data objects which have the potential to enable data synthesis and data augmentation for AI humor research. Our main takeaway is that better prompting of an LLM improves humor explanation quality, especially by handling missing context, multi-modality, and transcript issues more carefully. These results establish a strong foundation for future work on AI understanding of humor as social behavior.

</details>


### [68] [By Their Fruits You Will Know Them: Comparing Formalizations of Law by the Decisions They Encode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25186)
*Julius Vernie,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种系统比较同一法律条款不同形式化表示的方法，通过SAT求解器枚举分歧边界案例并转化为具体事实场景，应用于9个前沿LLM对10个欧盟条款的形式化，发现行为分歧与结构一致性基本无关，且生成的案例揭示了与法律评论中真实争议相呼应的分歧类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律条款的形式化涉及隐含的解释选择，其后果难以预测，尤其是当形式化由LLM生成时。现有方法缺乏对不同形式化表示进行比较的系统性手段，难以识别和评估它们之间的行为差异。

Method: 给定同一法律条款的多个形式化表示，在节点级别进行匹配，为每对形式化推导共享接口；使用SAT求解器枚举所有分歧的边界案例；将选定的边界案例转化为具体事实场景供法律专家审查。

Result: 对9个前沿LLM生成的10个欧盟条款形式化表示进行分析，发现形式化之间的行为分歧与它们的结构一致性基本不相关；生成的案例揭示了多种类型的定性分歧，包括与法律评论中真实争议相呼应的分歧。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效揭示不同法律形式化表示之间的隐含分歧，为评估和验证LLM生成的法律形式化提供系统工具，并帮助法律专家识别需要进一步解释的关键争议点。

Abstract: Formalizing legal provisions promises machine-accessible law and automated legal reasoning, and recent LLMs make it tempting to generate such formalizations directly from statutory text. However, any formalization makes implicit interpretive choices whose consequences are hard to anticipate, especially if an LLM is the author. We present a method for systematically comparing different formalizations of the same legal provision by their inferences on individual cases. Given multiple formalizations of a provision, we match them at the node level, derive a shared interface for each pair from the matching, and use a SAT solver to enumerate the edge cases on which any two formalizations disagree. Selected edge cases are then verbalized into concrete factual scenarios that a legal expert can examine and act on. We apply our method to formalizations of ten EU provisions generated by nine frontier LLMs. We find that behavioral divergence between formalizations is essentially uncorrelated with their structural agreement and that the verbalized cases reveal qualitatively distinct types of disagreement, including divergences that mirror genuine controversies in the legal commentary.

</details>


### [69] [Clarification Is Not Enough: Post-Clarification Answering Remains the Bottleneck in Multi-Turn QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25204)
*Jinyan Su,Jennifer Healey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多轮问答中的偏好 elicitation 问题，将其分解为澄清策略（决定是否提问）和澄清后回答两个组件。基于PACIFIC基准实验发现，监督微调能快速改善澄清策略，但最终答案准确率仍然偏低，表明正确理解用户回应才是多轮问答系统的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元对齐系统需要适应不同的用户价值观、沟通风格和上下文假设，而实现这种对齐的基础前提是在用户意图表述不明确或模糊时，能够准确地进行偏好 elicitation。

Method: 将多轮问答中的偏好 elicitation 问题分解为两个组件：1) 澄清策略（clarification policy），决定是提出澄清问题还是直接回答；2) 澄清后回答（post-clarification answering），在获得缺失信息后生成最终答案。使用 PACIFIC 基准进行实验评估。

Result: 监督微调可以快速改善澄清策略的性能，但即使在模型采取了正确行动的情况下，最终答案的准确率仍然显著偏低。

Conclusion: 理解并正确解读用户的回应是多轮问答系统中的关键瓶颈，而非澄清策略本身。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment requires systems to adapt to diverse user values, communication styles, and contextual assumptions. We believe that a foundational prerequisite for such alignment enabling accurate preference elicitation from people when their intent is under-specified or ambiguous. We study the problem of preference elicitation in multi-turn question answering by decomposing the problem into two components: a \textbf{clarification policy}, which decides whether to ask a clarifying question or answer directly, and \textbf{post-clarification answering}, which produces the correct final answer once the missing information is provided. We show, using the PACIFIC benchmark, that supervised fine-tuning rapidly improves the clarification policy, however, final answer accuracy remains substantially lower even when the model takes the correct action. This gap indicates that understanding and correctly interpreting the user's response is the critical gap in multi-turn question-answering systems.

</details>


### [70] [They Are Not the Same: Direct Causes Are Not Grounded Emotion Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25208)
*Zhuangzhuang Pan,Yan Xia,Chee Seng Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECPE任务常被简化为二元配对预测，但本文证明该简化只能提取直接触发因素，无法提供有证据支持的情感解释。实验表明，90.9%的正例和95.0%的负例保持了原始边界，但情感语境（emo-context）等证据性内容在二元边界中没有稳定位置，导致模型倾向于奖励便捷归因而非有依据的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前ECPE任务被简化为二元配对预测后，虽然能提取直接因果触发因素，但无法提供真正有证据支持的情感解释。作者试图揭示这种简化带来的根本性问题。

Method: 通过IEMO-MECP数据集验证原始二元边界的保持率（正例90.9%、负例95.0%）；引入"情感语境（emo-context）"概念，分析其在二元边界两侧的分布及靠近不确定性区域的特点；评估现有ECPE模型对直接触发因素与语境支持的恢复能力；通过快捷压力测试考察模型在便利归因与有依据解释之间的偏向。

Result: 原始二元ECPE任务基本保持（90.9%正例、95.0%负例）；但情感语境（emo-context）在二元边界两侧均有分布且集中于不确定性区域，说明边界无法稳定容纳此类证据性内容；模型对直接触发词的恢复更为可靠，但在快捷压力下，二元训练模型倾向于给邻近且词汇相似的非配对候选者更高的配对分数，而非有证据支持但结构更复杂的真实配对。

Conclusion: 高ECPE二元性能只表示模型能识别直接触发因素，并不代表模型真正解释了情感。ECPE的二元简化丢失了情感解释中所需的证据支撑，模型倾向于选择便捷归因而非有根据的解释。

Abstract: Emotion-Cause Pair Extraction (ECPE) was introduced to explain why an emotion occurs, but this goal is now often reduced to binary pair/non-pair prediction. This proxy is useful for direct-cause extraction, yet easy to over-read as evidence grounded emotion explanation. We show that this interpretation is only partially valid. In IEMO-MECP, 90.9% of original positives remain emo-cause and 95.0% of original negatives remain non-pair, confirming that the binary ECPE task is largely preserved. The problem is that direct triggers alone do not constitute a grounded explanation. Emo-context, an utterance that helps interpret a target emotion without directly causing it, appears on both sides of the original boundary and is enriched near binary uncertainty, showing that the binary boundary has no stable place for such discourse evidence. Across evaluated ECPE models, direct triggers are recovered more reliably than contextual support. Under shortcut pressure, this imbalance becomes consequential. Binary-trained models assign higher pair scores to nearby lexically similar non-pair candidates than to evidence supported but structurally harder emo-cause and emo-context pairs. Thus, pair scores can reward convenient attributions over grounded explanations. High binary ECPE performance indicates that a model can identify direct triggers; it does not indicate that the model has explained the emotion. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/panzhzh/ECPExsame.

</details>


### [71] [From Automation to Collaboration: Human-in-the-Loop Methods for Safe and Trustworthy NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25226)
*Most. Sharmin Sultana Samu,MD. Tanvir Ahmed Seum,Md. Rakibul Islam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了人机协作方法在提升大语言模型安全性与可信度方面的应用，涵盖审计、鲁棒性评估、数据构建和模型引导等环节，并指出了可扩展探测、低资源环境及私有系统治理等方面的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险NLP任务中仍存在偏见、幻觉、对抗攻击脆弱性和泛化不可靠等风险，现有自动化方法难以有效验证，人工监督虽必要但成本高昂且难以规模化，亟需系统性梳理人机协作的可行路径。

Method: 采用综述方法，系统梳理近期基于人类参与（human-in-the-loop）的NLP安全性与可信度研究，涵盖探测审计、对抗验证、数据标注和模型引导四个维度。

Result: 研究发现：人类专家在探测验证、对抗鲁棒性评估、领域特定数据构建和模型行为引导方面发挥关键作用；但当前方法在可扩展探测、可持续鲁棒性基准、低资源语言场景和私有系统治理等方面仍存在明显缺口。

Conclusion: 提出三个未来研究方向：自适应审计（adaptive auditing）、协作式评估（collaborative evaluation）和可问责部署（accountable deployment），推动NLP从自动化走向人机协作的安全可信范式。

Abstract: Large language models are widely deployed in high-stakes NLP tasks, yet risks such as bias, hallucination, adversarial vulnerability and unreliable generalization remain. Probe-based auditing reveals inconsistencies in model behavior. Adversarial text generation uncovers robustness gaps, especially in lower-resourced languages with limited benchmarks. Enterprise text-to-SQL settings expose the difficulty of validating outputs over private and large-scale databases. Human supervision is essential for probe validation, adversarial verification and domain-specific annotation, but it is costly and hard to scale. This survey examines recent human-in-the-loop methods that shift NLP from automation toward collaboration for safety and trustworthiness. We review how human expertise supports auditing, robustness evaluation, data construction and model steering. Our findings highlight gaps in scalable probing, sustainable robustness benchmarks, low-resource settings and governance of private systems. We outline practical research directions for adaptive auditing, collaborative evaluation and accountable deployment.

</details>


### [72] [JudgmentBench: Comparing Rubric and Preference Evaluation for Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25240)
*Russell Yang,Ruishi Chen,Pierce Kelaita,Riya Ranjan,Sibo Ma,Charles Dickens,Matthew Guillod,Megan Ma,Julian Nyarko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了JudgmentBench，一个包含30个真实法律任务、1539个评分标准和1530对偏好判断的数据集。研究发现，比较判断（成对偏好）在恢复质量排序上显著优于基于规则的评分（Spearman相关系数0.908 vs 0.150），且标注时间减半。这一模式在人类标注者和LLM自动评分器中均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试中，基于规则的评分和比较判断两种方法广泛使用，但选择其中一种方法很少得到合理论证。缺乏在相同专家和相同项目上比较这两种监督信号的高质量数据集。

Method: 构建JudgmentBench基准，包含30个真实法律任务，由执业律师（包括美国大型律师事务所）提供1539个评分标准和1530对成对偏好判断。使用三种质量水平的LLM生成输出，通过Spearman秩相关系数比较两种方法的排序恢复能力。

Result: 比较判断的Spearman秩相关系数为0.908，远高于基于规则的评分（0.150），估计差异为0.758 [0.494, 1.021]。比较判断所需的标注时间不到基于规则评分的一半。这一模式在人类标注者和LLM自动评分器中均一致。

Conclusion: 比较判断在恢复质量排序方面优于基于规则的评分，且效率更高。该配对数据集支持进一步研究在缺乏可验证真实价值的领域中如何收集、聚合和使用专家判断作为监督信号。

Abstract: Two methodologies dominate current practices of benchmarking: rubric-based scoring evaluates items against predefined criteria, whereas comparative judgment elicits pairwise preferences between outputs. Although both methodologies are widely used, the choice between them is rarely justified. We release JudgmentBench, a benchmark of 30 real-world legal tasks, paired with 1,539 rubric scores and 1,530 pairwise preference judgments collected from practicing attorneys--including at major U.S. law firms--with substantial experience. The annotations constitute the first publicly available dataset in a high-expertise domain in which both supervision signals are elicited from the same experts on the same items. Using LLM-generated outputs at three constructed quality levels, we provide an initial empirical comparison: comparative judgments recover the intended quality ordering substantially better than rubrics (mean Spearman's rank correlation of 0.908 vs. 0.150, estimated difference = 0.758 [0.494, 1.021]) while requiring less than half the annotation time. The patterns hold for human annotators and LLM autograders. Beyond this initial comparison, the paired structure of the dataset supports a broader research agenda on how expert judgment should be elicited, aggregated, and used as supervision in domains without verifiable ground truth.

</details>


### [73] [Inference Time Optimization with Confidence Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25244)
*Yu Wang,Minghao Liu,Jiayun Wang,Jinrui Huang,Ankit Shah,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次发现LLM推理轨迹中正确回答的置信度随时间提升（正置信增益），而错误回答的置信度衰减，基于此提出置信动态增益（CDG）投票方法，在多个开源模型和基准上显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时优化技术（如重复采样）显著提升了LLM的推理能力，但模型不确定性在这些优化策略中的关键作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出置信动态增益（CDG）投票方法，通过追踪推理链条中置信度轨迹的演化（即置信增益），将正确与错误轨迹的置信动态差异作为判别信号，用于改进答案选择。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1、gpt-oss、Gemma-3、Qwen-QwQ四种开源架构上，基于AIME24/25、HMMT25、BRUMO25基准的实验表明，CDG相比基线方法取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: CDG方法提供了鲁棒的判别信号用于改进LLM推理中的答案选择，并提供了相应的理论洞见。

Abstract: Inference time optimization techniques, such as repeated sampling, have significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the critical role of model uncertainty remains largely underexplored in these optimization strategies. In this paper, we investigate the dynamics of confidence along reasoning trajectories and for first time reveal a surprising and unique pattern: correct answer traces tend to exhibit confidence improvement over time (positive confidence gain), while incorrect traces show attenuated or declining confidence as reasoning proceeds. Based on this observation, we propose Confidence Dynamic Gain (CDG) based voting, which incorporates how the confidence trajectory of the response evolves along the reasoning chain. Experiments across four open-source architectures (DeepSeek-R1, gpt-oss, Gemma-3, Qwen-QwQ) on the AIME24/25, HMMT25, and BRUMO25 benchmarks demonstrate that CDG yields a significant performance boost over baselines. These results demonstrate that our method provides a robust discriminative signal for improving answer selection in LLM reasoning. We also provide theoretical insights for this phenomenon. Code will be released at https://github.com/Accenture/CDG.git.

</details>


### [74] [Mimir: Large-scale Multilingual Concept Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25263)
*Elio Musacchio,Lucia Siciliani,Pierpaolo Basile*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Mimir——一个16亿参数的大型概念模型，将语言建模从传统的token预测范式转变为概念建模（concept modeling），直接进行下一概念预测。模型在46种语言的388亿句子上预训练，并在35种语言的6680万句子上进行指令微调，实验对比了同等参数规模的传统语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的语言建模范式虽然性能优异，但学界开始质疑模型如何从token中理解语义，并探索更粗粒度表示（概念）是否能够推动研究进步，因此提出概念建模（Concept Modeling）——从预测下一个token转向预测下一个概念。

Method: 引入Mimir（16亿参数大型概念模型），将输入从细粒度token转换为宽泛概念，直接在概念级别进行序列建模。采用多语言预训练（46种语言，388亿句子）和多轮多语言指令微调（35种语言，6680万句子）两阶段训练。

Result: 在同等参数规模下，将概念模型Mimir与传统语言模型进行多维度对比评估。

Conclusion: 概念建模作为token级语言建模的替代范式具有研究价值，通过将建模粒度从token提升到概念层面，可能更有效地捕获语义信息。

Abstract: Current language modeling approaches are built around tokens. Text corpora are split into tokens, and models are trained by performing computations on these tokens, such as predicting the next token given the preceding ones as context. This paradigm has become the standard in modern language modeling, especially given the outstanding performance obtained by token-based architectures. However, recent works have not only begun to question how language models process and understand meaning from tokens, but also to question whether using higher levels of granularity could advance the research field. This led to the idea of Concept Modeling, that is, to directly train models for next-concept prediction rather than next-token prediction. The goal is to change the input from tokens to concepts, forcing the underlying language model to shift its granularity from fine-grained tokens to broad concepts. In this work, we introduce Mimir, a 1.6B Large Concept Model trained for multilingual concept understanding and generation. We leverage a large-scale multilingual pre-training corpus (38,883,987,240 sentences) spanning 46 languages and a large-scale multi-turn and multilingual instruction-tuning dataset (66,816,428 sentences) covering a total of 35 languages. We extensively evaluate model performance against a language model with a comparable number of parameters.

</details>


### [75] [READER: Reasoning-Enhanced AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25281)
*Pingfan Su,Kai Ye,Shijin Gong,Erhan Xu,Jin Zhu,Giulia Livieri,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: READER是一个仅1.5B参数的推理增强型AI文本检测器，通过先推理后检测的策略，在分布偏移下持续超越GPT-5.2等千倍规模的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测器在分布偏移下性能严重下降，且缺乏可解释性，难以提供检测决策的依据。

Method: 构建READ监督数据集（包含推理链和判决），在此基础上微调LLM得到READER模型，使其在检测前先进行结构化推理，输出标签和证据性rationale。

Result: 仅1.5B参数的READER在分布偏移场景下持续优于GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2等100-1000倍规模的检测器和提示基线。

Conclusion: 引入结构化推理机制可以有效提升AI文本检测器的泛化能力和可解释性，且无需依赖超大模型规模。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have made it increasingly difficult to distinguish human-written text from AI-generated content. Many existing detectors train supervised neural classifiers that achieve strong in-distribution performance but are often opaque and can degrade substantially under distribution shift. We present READER, a reasoning-enhanced AI text detector that outputs both a human/AI label and a structured rationale describing the evidence for its decision. A key component of our approach is READ, a curated supervision set of rationales and verdicts. We fine-tune an LLM on READ to build READER, which reasons before detecting at inference time. Despite having only 1.5B parameters, READER consistently outperforms existing detectors as well as prompted, high-capacity LLM baselines (GPT-5.2, Gemini-3-Pro, and DeepSeek-V3.2), which are 100 to 1000 times larger in scale.

</details>


### [76] [Knowing but Not Showing: LLMs Recognize Ambiguity but Rarely Ask Clarifying Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25284)
*Jinyan Su,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.CL
