Conclusion: TAPO通过结合多维度奖励分解和增强提示微调，有效提升了自回归模型在多维度作文评分任务上的表现，且具有良好的跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Multi-trait essay scoring aims to provide fine-grained evaluation of writing quality across multiple dimensions. However, how to effectively post-train autoregressive scoring models remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose Trait-Aware Policy Optimization (TAPO), a post-training framework tailored to autoregressive multi-trait scoring. Our method decomposes rewards along both the sample and trait dimensions, combining global scoring consistency, trait-level accuracy, format validity, and inter-trait dependency preservation. In addition, we enhance supervised fine-tuning with enhanced prompts, allowing the model to internalize trait semantics before preference optimization. Experiments across multiple backbone models show that our method consistently improves multi-trait scoring performance over supervised fine-tuning and scalar-reward optimization baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness and transferability of trait-aware post-training for essay scoring.

</details>


### [114] [Selective Latent Thinking: Adaptive Compression of LLM Reasoning Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25745)
*Hui Xie,Jie Liu,Ziyue Qiao,Joaquin Vanschore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SLT是一个选择性压缩冗余推理步骤为潜在表示、同时保留关键步骤为显式CoT的框架，在数学推理任务上相比潜在推理基线准确率提升22.7%，同时将推理链长度减少58.4%而准确率仅下降2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式CoT推理虽能提升LLM推理能力，但长自回归轨迹导致高推理成本。现有潜在推理方法将推理过程均匀压缩，导致精度关键的中间步骤被过度压缩，推理准确率下降。

Method: SLT框架包含：(1) 轻量解码器预测即将到来的短推理片段；(2) 基于置信度的门控机制确定可可靠压缩的最长跨度；(3) 接受跨度编码为紧凑潜在表示提升效率，不确定/精度关键推理保留为显式CoT；(4) 三阶段训练策略：跨度级潜在压缩、可靠性感知的未来推理预测、轨迹级强化学习优化正确性与成本权衡。

Result: 在四个数学推理基准上，SLT相比潜在推理基线准确率提升22.7%，相比显式CoT推理链长度减少58.4%且准确率仅下降2.8%。

Conclusion: 选择性压缩策略在保持推理精度的同时显著降低了推理成本，验证了混合潜在-显式推理路径的有效性。

Abstract: Explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning substantially improves the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs), but incurs high inference cost due to lengthy autoregressive traces. Existing latent reasoning methods offer a promising alternative, yet they often treat reasoning as uniformly compressible, causing precision-critical intermediate steps to be overly compressed and thereby degrading reasoning accuracy. In this work, we propose Selective Latent Thinking (SLT), a framework that selectively compresses redundant reasoning spans into latent representations while preserving precision-critical spans as explicit CoT within the same reasoning trajectory. Specifically, SLT first uses a lightweight decoder to anticipate a short upcoming reasoning span, and then applies confidence-based gating to determine the longest span that can be reliably compressed. The accepted span is encoded into a compact latent representation to improve reasoning efficiency, while uncertain or precision-critical reasoning remains in explicit CoT form to preserve accuracy. To learn this selective compression policy, SLT adopts a three-stage training strategy that combines span-level latent compression, reliability-aware future reasoning prediction, and trajectory-level reinforcement learning to optimize the trade-off between answer correctness and reasoning cost. Extensive experiments across four mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SLT achieves 22.7\% higher accuracy than latent reasoning baselines at comparable compression ratios, while reducing reasoning chain length by 58.4\% with only 2.8\% accuracy degradation compared to explicit CoT,Our code can be found in https://github.com/hunshi34/SLT.

</details>


### [115] [StreamProfileBench: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained User Profile Inference in Real-World Streaming Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25758)
*Sizhe Wang,Feiyu Duan,Juelin Wang,Liwen Zhang,Feiyu Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamProfileBench，一个面向流式用户画像的大规模基准测试，包含12万+条来自7000+真实用户的跨平台帖子，通过免标注评估框架揭示了大模型在持续用户画像更新中的保守性偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户画像评估多基于静态数据快照，忽略了用户生成内容持续到达、细粒度画像快速演变的现实，有必要建立能够评估大模型在流式场景下用户画像能力的基准。

Method: 1) 形式化流式用户画像为连续状态维护任务；2) 构建包含12万+帖子、7000+真实用户、5个平台的高真实性数据集；3) 利用用户兴趣的时间相关性提出免标注评估框架；4) 在14个主流大模型上进行实验。

Result: 连续画像更新仍是一个开放挑战；模型存在系统性保守偏差，倾向于过度保留过去兴趣而未能识别兴趣衰减；消融实验验证了流式范式的实用性和必要性。

Conclusion: 现有大模型在动态用户画像更新方面表现不足，存在保守偏差，未来需要针对流式场景改进模型能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have reshaped user profiling, yet current evaluations mainly focus on static data snapshots. This paradigm overlooks the reality of personalized systems, where User-Generated Content (UGC) arrives continuously and fine-grained profile evolve rapidly. To bridge this gap, we introduce StreamProfileBench, a large-scale benchmark for fine-grained streaming user profiling. We formalize streaming user profiling as a continuous state maintenance task and curate a highly authentic dataset comprising over 120,000 UGC posts from 7,000+ real users across five diverse platforms. By leveraging the temporal correlation of user interests, we further propose a novel, annotation-free evaluation framework. Extensive experiments across 14 leading LLMs reveal that continuous profile updating remains an open challenge. Models exhibit a systemic conservative bias, over-retaining past interests while failing to recognize interest decay. Ablation experiments further validate the practical utility and necessity of the streaming paradigm.

</details>


### [116] [Double Triangle Annotation: A Scalable Human-in-the-Loop Framework for High-Precision Historical Document Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25781)
*Yi Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双层人类参与框架Double Triangle Annotation，利用跨模型共识实现大规模历史文档结构化信息提取的高精度标注，在法国医疗目录语料上达到0.003的词错误率，超过85%的字段被自动接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史文档结构化信息提取需要高精度真值标注，但传统人工标注成本高昂，而基于大语言模型的自动化流程易产生幻觉，亟需一种兼顾效率与精度的大规模标注方法。

Method: 提出双三角标注（Double Triangle Annotation）框架：第一层由两个架构独立的多模态大语言模型并行标注文档，一致时自动接受，分歧交由人类陪审团裁决；第二层对两个这样的系统进行交叉校验，残余冲突升级至领域专家。核心假设是模型间误差独立性，无需分布先验或任务特定校准。

Result: 在Guides Rosenwald法国医疗目录语料（1887-1906）上，最终词错误率达0.003；大规模应用中，模型共识自动接受超过85%的13,595个字段；发布了首个Rosenwald Guides结构化提取基准真值。

Conclusion: 该框架通过多模型共识与人类校验的层级结合，在保证高精度的同时大幅降低人工标注成本，且随模型能力提升自主性进一步增强，为历史文档处理领域提供了可扩展的标注范式与基准数据集。

Abstract: Evaluating structured-information extraction from historical documents at scale requires high-precision ground-truth annotations, yet traditional manual labeling is expensive and fully automated pipelines built on large language models are prone to hallucination. We propose Double Triangle Annotation, a two-layer human-in-the-loop framework that leverages cross-model consensus to automate the majority of annotation work while ensuring high-precision outputs. In the first layer, two architecturally independent Multimodal Large Language Models annotate each document in parallel; when they agree, the label is auto-accepted, and disagreements are routed to a human jury. A second layer cross-checks two such systems against each other, escalating residual conflicts to a domain expert. The framework rests on a single assumption -- error independence between models -- requires no distributional priors or task-specific calibration, and becomes more autonomous as model capability improves. On the Guides Rosenwald, a corpus of French medical directories spanning 1887-1906, the framework achieves a final Word Error Rate of 0.003. Applied at scale, model consensus auto-accepts over 85% of 13,595 fields. We release the resulting benchmark -- the first structured-extraction ground truth for the Rosenwald Guides -- to support future work on historical document processing.

</details>


### [117] [Adaptive Graph Refinement and Label Propagation with LLMs for Cost-Effective Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25814)
*Hongtao Wang,Renchi Yang,Haoran Zheng,Xiangyu Ke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Alper框架，将脏数据实体解析中的匹配与聚类整合为迭代式概率标签传播过程，结合图传播的弱信号和LLM的强查询，在预算约束下优化信号选择，实验证明优于传统级联流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阻塞-匹配-聚类级联范式存在缺陷：产生静态稀疏图，存在遗漏边（阻塞失败）和噪声边（匹配错误），导致错误传播和次优聚类，尤其当施加刚性传递性时问题更严重。

Method: 提出Alper统一框架，将匹配和聚类整合为迭代式概率标签传播过程：1) 在全局动态图上进行标签传播；2) 自适应整合"弱但廉价"的图传播信号与"强但昂贵"的LLM成对查询；3) 将信号选择建模为预算约束下的累积边际增益最大化问题，用贪心算法求解并提供理论保证。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上的实验表明，Alper一致优于最先进的级联流水线方法。

Conclusion: 匹配和聚类本质上是协同优化的过程，统一框架相比解耦级联范式能显著提升脏数据实体解析的效果。

Abstract: Dirty entity resolution (ER), which identifies records referring to the same real-world entity from a single, messy dataset, is a fundamental task in data management and mining. However, the dominant blocking-matching-clustering paradigm for ER suffers from critical flaws. Its cascaded, decoupled workflow essentially produces a static, sparse graph plagued by missing edges (due to blocking failures) and noisy links (due to matching errors), causing error propagation and yielding suboptimal clusters, particularly when rigid transitivity is imposed in the clustering. We contend that matching and clustering are fundamentally synergistic, both optimizing for the construction of an ideal entity graph. Building upon this insight, we propose Alper, a unified framework that integrates these steps into an iterative probabilistic label propagation process over a global, evolving graph. Unlike disjoint blocking, Alper refines the graph structure and labels dynamically by adaptively integrating "weak but cheap" signals from graph propagation with "strong but expensive" LLM-based pairwise queries. For higher cost-effectiveness, we formulate the signal selection as a constrained optimization problem maximizing cumulative marginal gain under a query budget, solved via our greedy algorithm with provable theoretical guarantees. Our extensive experiments over eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that Alper is consistently superior to state-of-the-art cascaded pipelines.

</details>


### [118] [Fine-Tuning Over Architectural Complexity: Broad-Coverage PII Detection on PIIBench with DeBERTa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25816)
*Pritesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究PII（个人身份信息）检测系统在异构文本上的覆盖范围问题。通过多源PIIBench数据集（82种实体类型，10个源数据集）对DeBERTa模型进行微调，比较了直接标记分类微调、源条件层次模型（SC+H）和三阶段课程扩展（SC+H+Curr）三种方法。结果表明，直接微调在5,000条测试集上F1达0.6476，在完整100,002条测试集上F1达0.6455，显著优于所有已发表系统（最高仅0.1723），说明多样化任务特定训练数据和简单加权交叉熵目标比架构和课程复杂性更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PII检测系统通常在狭窄的源或领域边界内训练，导致在异构文本上部署时覆盖范围有限，缺乏跨源、多实体类型的统一检测能力。

Method: 基于DeBERTa模型提出三种方法：1）直接标记分类微调；2）源条件层次模型（SC+H）；3）三阶段课程扩展（SC+H+Curr）。在包含82种实体类型、10个源数据集的修正多源PIIBench上训练，使用加权交叉熵损失函数。

Result: 直接微调在5,000条测试集上F1达到0.6476（SC+H为0.5899，SC+H+Curr为0.2772），完整100,002条测试集上F1为0.6455（SC+H为0.5894）。直接微调在82种细粒度实体类型中获胜54种，在所有10种粗粒度组中获胜。已发布最强比较系统仅达F1 0.1723。

Conclusion: 多样化的任务特定训练数据和简单的加权交叉熵目标对广覆盖PII检测的贡献大于架构复杂性和课程学习策略。

Abstract: Personally identifiable information (PII) detection systems are frequently trained within narrow source or domain boundaries, limiting coverage when deployed on heterogeneous text. We study model fine-tuning on a corrected multi-source PIIBench preparation spanning 82 retained entity types across ten source datasets. We evaluate three DeBERTa-based approaches: direct token classification fine-tuning, a source-conditioned hierarchical model (SC+H), and a three-phase curriculum extension (SC+H+Curr). Against eight published comparator systems on a reproducible 5,000-record held-out subset (test_5k), direct fine-tuned DeBERTa achieves F1 0.6476, while SC+H and the curriculum variant achieve 0.5899 and 0.2772 respectively; the strongest published comparator reaches only 0.1723. Because validation initially favoured SC+H, we perform a final streamed evaluation on the complete 100,002-record held-out split. Direct fine-tuning remains superior, achieving F1 0.6455 versus 0.5894 for SC+H. Entity-level analysis shows that direct fine tuning wins 54 of 82 fine entity types and all ten coarse groups by support-weighted entity F1, while SC+H retains localised advantages on 28 types. The results indicate that diverse task-specific training data and a simple weighted cross-entropy objective contribute more to broad-coverage PII detection than the tested architectural and curriculum complexity.

</details>


### [119] [Clarify, Abstain or Answer? Strategising in Conversation with Belief-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25831)
*Joris Baan,Wilker Aziz,Barbara Plank,Raquel Fernández*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出信念增强生成（BAG）方法，通过让LLM对自身采样的K个回应进行推理，自主决定采用回答、澄清或放弃的策略，在多轮模糊问答中提升准确率和策略忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对自身不确定性的利用局限于解码或选择性预测等狭窄任务，且需人工干预，无法直接控制生成策略。需要一种让模型自主感知不确定性并调整对话行为的方法。

Method: BAG方法将LLM自身采样的K个回应作为信念状态，通过提示词将这些样本注入上下文，让模型推理后自主选择对话策略（回答、澄清或放弃），无需额外训练或外部工具。

Result: 在六个模型上的多轮模糊问答任务中，BAG显著提升了QA准确率，且策略决策比仅用提示的基线方法更忠实于信念状态。但区分"需要澄清"与"应该放弃"仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 通过对自身信念状态的显式推理，LLM能更有效地处理不确定场景，但澄清与放弃的精细化区分仍有待改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) define a distribution over text, which can be viewed as a probabilistic representation of uncertainty: sampling K responses yields a belief state - responses a model deems plausible. Existing work exploits this representation for narrow tasks like either decoding or selective prediction, and often requires manual interventions, not controlling generation directly. We propose Belief-Augmented Generation (BAG): grounding LLMs in their own belief state via the prompt and letting them reason over these K samples to decide on a conversational strategy: answer, clarify, or abstain. In a multi-turn ambiguous QA setting, we find that LLMs by default rarely clarify or abstain, ignoring uncertainty about the input or facts. BAG improves QA accuracy across six models and yields strategy decisions more faithful to the belief state than prompt-only baselines. Disentangling when to clarify from when to abstain, however, remains challenging.

</details>


### [120] [On the Limits of Model Merging for Multilinguality in Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25846)
*Seth Aycock,Fedor Vitiugin,Aleksandr Umnov,Christof Monz,Khalil Sima'an*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了单语预训练模型的合并效果，发现合并会导致性能崩溃，而表征相似性是模型合并的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以将模型合并技术应用于单语预训练模型，以实现多语言能力的一致提升。

Method: 通过控制实验，对比混合预训练、模型合并和单语预训练三种设置下的模型表现。

Result: 单语预训练在对应语言上表现强劲，但合并任何单语模型的组合都会因干扰导致性能崩溃。

Conclusion: 模型合并的灵活性在微调阶段有效，但并不简单地适用于语言特定的预训练阶段，表征相似性是合并的前提。

Abstract: Endowing models with consistent multilingual performance can be achieved by mixing pre-training data, or post-training approaches such as language-specific model merging. In this work, we test whether merging can be applied to monolingually pre-trained models. We conduct a controlled study on the efficacy of mixed, merged, and monolingual pre-training setups. We find that while monolingual pre-training results in strong in-language performance, merging any combination of monolingual models leads to performance collapse due to interference. Our analysis suggests representational similarity is a prerequisite for model merging. We therefore conclude that the flexibility of merging in fine-tuning does not extend trivially to language-specific pre-training.

</details>


### [121] [TIAR: Trajectory-Informed Advantage Reweighting for LLM Abstention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25850)
*Muyu Pan,Shu Zhao,Nan Zhang,Philip Shin,Varun Parekh,Vijaykrishnan Narayanan,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory-Informed Advantage Reweighting (TIAR)方法，利用GRPO训练中的多条轨迹作为置信度信号动态计算弃权优势，以改进大语言模型的弃权学习，在AbstentionBench的六个评估类别中五个类别取得最优弃权F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型弃权学习主要使用三元奖励机制来鼓励真实性，但该方法对弃权决策的奖励是静态的，未能充分利用训练过程中产生的信息来动态调整知识边界。本文旨在通过轨迹信息来更精确地判断模型何时应该弃权（即不回答），从而减少模型幻觉。

Method: 本文方法TIAR在GRPO训练框架下，利用多个采样轨迹作为模型对查询的置信度信号。具体而言，通过分析轨迹间的一致性来动态计算每条轨迹的弃权优势，而非使用固定的三元奖励。这种方法使得模型能够根据自身对知识的掌握程度自适应地决定是否回答。

Result: 在AbstentionBench基准测试上，TIAR在六个评估类别中的五个类别取得了最优的弃权F1分数，在31个基准数据集中有17个数据集超越了静态三元基线方法，同时完全保留了基线的准确率。

Conclusion: 本文证明了轨迹信息可以作为模型置信度的有效信号，并可以通过动态优势重加权来改进弃权学习。该方法在不牺牲准确率的前提下，显著提升了模型的弃权能力，为减少大语言模型幻觉提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: This paper investigates large language model (LLM) abstention learning, specifically using ternary reward, which incentivize truthfulness in large language models. This paper extends that idea by moving from a ternary reward to a Trajectory-Informed advantage reweighting, dynamically re-weights the abstention reward during Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) training. The objective of this work focuses on abstention learning instead of improving truthfulness, serving as an exploration into hallucination reduction. The novelty of this paper lies in methodological innovation, advantage re-weighting, and benchmark selection. Leveraging GRPO's multiple trajectories as a natural abstention signal, this method uses a reward signal to explore knowledge boundaries and encourage consistency. By demonstrating that trajectories can be used as a confidence indicator of the policy relative to the query, they are then used to dynamically calculate the abstention advantage. AbstentionBench is used as the evaluation benchmark, as this work aims to contribute to the field of abstention learning. All datasets on the benchmark were tested against this method and various baselines. Empirical results demonstrate that TIAR achieves state-of-the-art abstention F1 scores across five of six evaluation categories, outperforming the static ternary baseline on 17 of 31 benchmark datasets while fully preserving baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [122] [Mitigating Provenance-Role Collapse in Long-Term Agents via Typed Memory Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25869)
*Zhengda Jin,Bingbing Wang,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemIR提出了一种类型化的记忆中间表示，通过将长期记忆结构化为分离原始证据、检索线索和事实主张的原子单元，并限制事实授权仅来自支持的主张原子，从架构层面解决了LLM智能体的源监控错误问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体将历史交互存储为非结构化的扁平文本，导致"出处-角色坍缩"——智能体无法区分信息来源的角色（证据、线索还是事实主张），产生源监控错误这一认知脆弱性。

Method: MemIR设计了类型化记忆中间表示：(1) 将长期记忆写入为分离原始证据、检索线索和真值主张的接地原子，事实授权仅限于支持的主张原子；(2) 通过多路径原子投影和出处范围利用，将异构检索结果转换为以主张为中心的候选束和规范化事实接口，用于答案生成。

Result: 在LoCoMo和BEAM-100K数据集上，MemIR在需要源追踪、时间接地和碎片化证据聚合的任务上持续优于现有记忆基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过架构级别的结构化记忆表示，MemIR有效解决了LLM智能体的源监控错误，验证了将认知约束融入记忆中间表示的可行性。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for persistent LLM agents, yet prevailing architectures store historical interactions as unstructured, flat text. This unconstrained storage induces provenance-role collapse, a critical failure mode where agents suffer from source-monitoring errors. To resolve this cognitive vulnerability at the architectural level, we propose MemIR, a typed Memory Intermediate Representation that operationalizes source monitoring as a structural constraint. MemIR writes long-term memory into grounded atoms that separate raw evidence, retrieval cues, and truth-bearing claims, with factual authorization restricted to supported claim atoms. It then applies multi-route atomic projection and provenance-scoped utilization to transform heterogeneous retrieval hits into claim-centered candidate bundles and a normalized fact interface for answer generation. Experiments on LoCoMo and BEAM-100K demonstrate that MemIR consistently outperforms existing memory baselines, especially on tasks requiring source tracking, temporal grounding, and aggregation of fragmented evidence.

</details>


### [123] [Causal Tongue-Tie: LLMs Can Encode Causal Direction, But Their Yes/No Outputs Fail to Express](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25891)
*Ziyi Ding,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在因果推理问答中存在“因果舌结”现象：其隐藏状态包含正确答案（线性探针准确率约0.97），但口头输出却回到常识答案（准确率约0.5），揭示内部有信号但语言接口无法正确表达的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现LLM在因果推理中隐藏状态编码与最终口头回答之间存在系统性不匹配，质疑仅凭输出准确性评判模型因果推理能力的可靠性。

Method: 在反常识CLadder数据集上，使用固定线性探针从模型隐藏状态解码因果推理结果，并与模型的Yes/No口头回答进行对比，量化两者间的准确率差距。

Result: 线性探针从隐藏状态恢复正确答案的准确率约0.97，而口头回答准确率仅约0.5，存在约+0.5的准确率差距。错误回答分为两类：内部无信号 vs 有信号但语言接口无法表达。

Conclusion: 对仅依赖输出结果的因果推理基准测试提出双向警示：基准“正确”不意味着模型真正理解，基准“错误”也不意味着模型完全不能推理。单一准确率数字支持的因果推理能力论断需要重新审视。

Abstract: We find a mismatch between what large language models encode about a causal question and what they answer. On anti-commonsense CLadder items, a fixed linear probe recovers the evidence-supported answer from the model's hidden state (accuracy approximately 0.97), while the spoken Yes/No reverts to the commonsense one (accuracy approximately 0.5). We call this approximately +0.5 gap Causal Tongue-Tie: a wrong Yes/No decomposes into two separable failure modes: no internal signal versus a signal the verbal interface cannot say. The implication cuts both ways for output-only causal benchmarks: a benchmark "correct" need not mean the model has understood, and a benchmark "wrong" need not mean it cannot. Sweeping claims about whether LLMs can do causal reasoning, drawn from a single accuracy number, deserve a second look.

</details>


### [124] [Universal Activation Verbalizer: A Unified Framework for Cross-Model Activation Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25903)
*Haiyan Zhao,Zirui He,Guanchu Wang,Ali Payani,Yingcong Li,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出通用激活词化器（UAV），使用共享解码器解释异构捐赠者模型的隐藏表示，通过轻量适配器将捐赠者激活转换为解码器嵌入空间中的软标记，并支持仅适配器迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活词化方法局限于自解释（每个模型只能解释自身激活），无法跨模型解释隐藏表示。

Method: UAV学习轻量适配器将捐赠者激活映射到共享解码器嵌入空间；通过冻结解码器侧LoRA并仅训练新适配器实现适配器迁移。

Result: 在分类、事实检索和要点摘要任务上，UAV与强自解释基线具有竞争力，并实现了跨模型家族和规模的交叉模型词化。

Conclusion: 解码器侧调优主要提升任务行为，而适配器提供激活所基于的事实和语义信息，用于忠实解释。

Abstract: Activation verbalization explains hidden representations in natural language, but existing methods are mostly limited to self-explanation, where each model explains only its own activations. We introduce Universal Activation Verbalizer (UAV), a framework that uses a shared decoder to explain activations from heterogeneous donor models. UAV learns a lightweight adapter that converts donor activations into soft tokens in decoder's embedding space, and further supports adapter-only transfer by reusing a frozen decoder-side LoRA while training only a new adapter for another donor. Across classification, fact retrieval, and gist summarization, UAV remains competitive with strong self-explanation baselines while enabling cross-model verbalization across model families and scales. Ablations show that decoder-side tuning mainly improves task behavior, whereas the adapter provides the activation-grounded factual and semantic information needed for faithful explanations.

</details>


### [125] [Does Continued Pretraining on a Learner Corpus Improve Automated Essay Scoring on English Proficiency Tests? Evidence from EFCAMDAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25924)
*Duy Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在EFCAMDAT学习者语料库上进行领域自适应持续预训练（DAPT）对基于Transformer的自动评分系统（AES）的影响，发现全语料DAPT效果参差不齐，而基于CEFR等级的目标性DAPT（特别是B1-B2数据）能更可靠地提升领域内评分性能，但跨数据集迁移能力并未一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer模型通常在通用域英语上预训练，难以充分表征二语学习者的写作特点，需要探索领域自适应预训练是否能提升自动评分性能。

Method: 在EFCAMDAT学习者语料库上对三种Transformer编码器进行DAPT，在FCE和IELTS数据集上评估领域内评分和少样本跨数据集迁移性能，并进行基于CEFR水平的分层消融分析。

Result: 全语料DAPT在不同模型、数据集和指标上表现不一；性能差异部分源于EFCAMDAT与下游数据在熟练度、体裁和交际目的上的不匹配；基于CEFR的目标性子集DAPT（尤其B1-B2）比全语料DAPT更可靠地提升领域内评分，但跨数据集迁移无一致改善。

Conclusion: 当持续预训练数据与下游评估场景充分对齐时，学习者写作语料上的DAPT有助于英语评估的领域内AES，但不能自动提升不同英语水平测试数据集间的迁移能力。

Abstract: Recent automated essay scoring (AES) studies increasingly use pretrained transformer models, but these models are usually pretrained on general-domain English and may under-represent second-language learner writing. This study investigates whether domain-adaptive continued pretraining (DAPT) on the EFCAMDAT learner corpus improves transformer-based AES for English proficiency tests. We apply DAPT to three transformer encoders and evaluate them on FCE and IELTS in both in-domain scoring and few-shot cross-dataset transfer. Full-corpus DAPT produces mixed results across models, datasets, and metrics. Further analyses suggest that these mixed effects are partly explained by mismatches in proficiency, genre, and communicative purpose between EFCAMDAT and the downstream datasets. A proficiency-based ablation shows that targeted DAPT using CEFR-aligned subsets improves downstream scoring more reliably than full-corpus DAPT, especially for FCE with B1--B2 data. However, these gains do not consistently improve cross-dataset transfer. Overall, the findings suggest that continued pretraining on a learner-writing corpus can benefit in-domain AES for English assessment when the pretraining data is sufficiently aligned with the downstream assessment settings. However, it does not automatically improve transferability across different English proficiency test datasets.

</details>


### [126] [Thaka at KSAA-2026 Task 2: Regularized Fine-Tuning for Arabic Speech Diacritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25928)
*Meshal Alamr,Hassan Alqaeri,Abdullah Aldahlawi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了KSAA-2026共享任务Task 2（阿拉伯语语音听写与自动标音）的夺冠系统。该系统在仅有2,327个训练样本且禁止使用外部数据的条件下，通过微调CATT-Whisper多模态模型，结合R-Drop一致性正则化、Optuna优化高权重衰减超参数和Focal Loss，并在推理时对4个模型检查点进行200次随机前向传播的蒙特卡洛Dropout平均，最终以23.26%的词错误率（WER）获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语语音听写与自动标音任务面临训练数据稀缺（仅2,327个样本）且禁止使用外部数据的挑战，需要设计有效的正则化策略来防止过拟合，同时利用多模态架构融合语音和文本特征。

Method: 1）基于CATT-Whisper架构：结合预训练CATT文本编码器与冻结的Whisper语音编码器，实现字符级多模态建模；2）训练正则化：采用R-Drop一致性正则化、Optuna优化的高权重衰减超参数、Focal Loss；3）推理策略：对4个模型检查点在softmax概率层面进行200次随机前向传播的蒙特卡洛Dropout平均。

Result: 在主要排行榜指标（含词尾变位且包含无标音位置）上达到23.26%的词错误率（WER），在所有参赛队伍中排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过组合多种正则化策略（R-Drop、高权重衰减、Focal Loss）和蒙特卡洛Dropout推理平均，可以在极低资源条件下有效提升多模态语音识别与自动标音系统的性能，验证了所提方法的鲁棒性和有效性。

Abstract: We describe the winning system for Task 2 of the KSAA-2026 Shared Task on Arabic Speech Dictation with Automatic Diacritization. The task requires producing fully diacritized Arabic text from speech audio and undiacritized transcripts, with only 2,327 training samples available and no external data permitted. Our system fine-tunes CATT-Whisper, a character-level multimodal model combining a pretrained CATT text encoder with a frozen Whisper speech encoder. The key to our approach is training regularization: R-Drop consistency regularization, Optuna-optimized hyperparameters with high weight decay, and Focal Loss. At inference, we average 200 stochastic forward passes across four model checkpoints using Monte Carlo Dropout at the softmax probability level. The system achieves 23.26% WER on the primary leaderboard metric (with case endings, including no-diacritic positions), placing 1st among all participants.

</details>


### [127] [QUIET: A Multi-Blank Cascaded Story Cloze Benchmark for LLM Creative Generation Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25955)
*Bo Zou,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出QUIET基准测试，通过多空白级联故事完形填空和基于信息理论的自动化评分协议，实现对LLM创意生成能力的客观评估，替代了依赖主观判断的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM创意评估方法存在两个问题：一是基准测试（如Story Cloze Test）采用多项选择识别范式，测量的是辨别能力而非生成能力；二是基于评分标准和LLM-as-Judge的方法依赖主观评估，缺乏客观自动化机制。

Method: 构建包含N个空白（10-20个）的完整结构故事，每个空白有显式内容约束，空白间存在级联依赖关系。模型以开放生成方式填充所有空白，采用信息理论自动化评分协议，计算复合分数：score = satisfy * (1 + lambda * surprise)，其中lambda=1.0。

Result: 不满足约束的创意答案得零分；满足约束但平庸的答案得分低；同时满足约束且令人惊讶的答案得分高。

Conclusion: QUIET通过客观、自动化的方式直接评估LLM的创意生成能力，无需人工评分，为创意评估提供了可重复、可量化的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face a dual challenge in creative capability evaluation: existing benchmarks (e.g., Story Cloze Test, HellaSwag) measure models' discriminative ability over narrative continuation using multiple-choice recognition paradigms, rather than directly measuring creative generation capability; rubric-based scoring and LLM-as-Judge methods rely on subjective dimension assessment or natural language model outputs, and cannot provide objective, automated scoring mechanisms.
  This paper proposes QUIET (Quality Understanding via Interlocked Evaluation Testing), a diagnostic benchmark for LLM creative capability based on multi-blank cascaded story cloze. QUIET sets N blanks (10-20) in a story with complete structure, with each blank accompanied by an explicit content constraint, and cascade dependency relationships between blanks -- the content filled into earlier blanks constrains the feasible solution space for later blanks. The evaluated model (or human participants) fills all blanks in open-ended generation mode; the results are scored by an information-theoretic automated scoring protocol without human grading.
  The scoring protocol directly operationalizes the "calibrated surprise" theoretical framework (Zou & Xu, 2026a). For each blank k, a composite score is computed: score = satisfy * (1 + lambda * surprise), where lambda = 1.0. Here, "satisfy" measures how well the blank filling satisfies the content constraint (objective logical reasoning judgment, not subjective aesthetic scoring), and "surprise" measures the degree of surprise given that the constraint is satisfied. Creative answers that do not satisfy the constraint score zero; answers that satisfy the constraint but are mediocre score low; answers that satisfy the constraint and are surprising score high.

</details>


### [128] [PolyGnosis 2.0: Enhancing LLM Reasoning via Agentic Harness Engineering for Polymarket and OSINT Insight Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25958)
*Daren Wang,Hong Xu,Jiawen Xian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PolyGnosis 2.0多智能体架构，通过融合Polymarket异常信号与全球OSINT（GDELT）数据提取预测情报，定义"视角错配"作为高α交易信号，系统评估反射循环、工具调用、分治分区和链式思维等"驾驭工程"技术的效能，发现结构分区对多维对齐是必要的，而终端反射会导致逻辑漂移，并识别出所有智能体配置中普遍存在的"共识偏差"，最终确定了帕累托最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注通用智能体性能，缺乏对预测市场中"视角错配"（Polymarket情绪与全球媒体流之间的叙事分歧）这一高α信号的系统性提取与量化分析，同时缺少对"驾驭工程"技术在金融高噪声环境下的严格效能评估。

Method: 构建PolyGnosis 2.0多智能体架构，融合Polymarket异常信号与GDELT全球OSINT数据流；系统性评估反射循环、工具调用、分而治之分区（D&C）和链式思维（CoT）等驾驭工程技术；以人类专家基准进行实证对比评估。

Result: 结构分区是多维对齐的必要条件，但无约束的终端反射会导致逻辑漂移；所有智能体配置在叙事推理中均存在普遍的"共识偏差"，需要确定性验证；识别出在精度、延迟和token开销三方面达到帕累托最优的配置。

Conclusion: PolyGnosis 2.0通过帕累托最优配置实现了专业级分析精度，为预测市场中的自主智能提供了稳健蓝图，同时强调结构分区的重要性、终端反射的风险以及共识偏差的普遍性。

Abstract: This paper introduces PolyGnosis 2.0, a pioneering multi-agent architecture designed to extract predictive intelligence by synthesizing Polymarket anomaly signals with global Open Source Intelligence (OSINT) streams, specifically Global Database of Events, Language, and Tone (GDELT). We define and target "Perspective Mismatches", the narrative divergence between Polymarket sentiment and global media flows, as high-alpha trading signals. Moving beyond generic agentic superiority, we rigorously quantify the efficacy of "Harness Engineering" techniques, including reflection loops, tool-calling, divide-and-conquer partitioning (D&C), and chain-of-thought (CoT), within high-noise financial domains. Our empirical evaluation against human-expert benchmarks reveals that while structural partitioning is mandatory for multi-dimensional alignment, unconstrained terminal reflection actively induces logical drift. Furthermore, we identify a pervasive "consensus bias" across all agent configurations during narrative reasoning, necessitating deterministic validation. Ultimately, we isolate a Pareto-optimal configuration that achieves professional-grade analytical precision while minimizing latency and token overhead, providing a robust blueprint for autonomous intelligence in prediction markets.

</details>


### [129] [Triplet-Block Diffusion RWKV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25969)
*Ke Lin,Yiyang Luo,Zhaolong Su,Yunya Song,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出B³D-RWKV，一种融合RWKV的O(L)推理效率与双向离散扩散的扩散RWKV变体，通过三重块布局方法实现，在8任务套件上达到可比精度，平均解码吞吐量提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果Transformer存在严格顺序解码和每步二次注意力成本的问题；线性时间因果模型与离散扩散模型各自解决了部分问题，但两者本质不兼容（扩散需要双向注意力，因果模型是单向的），亟需统一架构。

Method: 提出B³D-RWKV，采用三重块布局方法（triplet-block layout），将RWKV的O(L)推理效率与并行、双向离散扩散相结合，实现因果与双向注意力的统一。

Result: B³D-RWKV-7.2B在8任务套件上达到与现有模型可比的准确率，并在解码吞吐量上平均实现1.6倍加速。

Conclusion: 成功统一了线性时间因果模型与双向离散扩散架构，在保持精度前提下显著提升解码效率。

Abstract: Causal Transformer language models suffer from strictly sequential decoding and a quadratic per-step attention cost. While linear-time causal models and discrete diffusion models each address these weaknesses, their integration remains inherently inconsistent: diffusion requires bidirectional attention, while causal models are unidirectional. To unify these architectures, we propose $B^3D-RWKV$, a diffusion RWKV variant that integrates the model's $O(L)$ inference efficiency with parallel, bidirectional discrete-diffusion through a \emph{triplet-block layout} method. $B^3D-RWKV-7.2B$ reaches comparable accuracy on an 8-task suite versus existing models while significantly outperforming baselines in decoding throughput with an average of $\mathbf{1.6\times}$ speedup.

</details>


### [130] [Anticipate and Learn: Unleashing Idle-Time Compute in Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25971)
*Haoyi Hu,Qirong Lyu,Xianghan Kong,Weiwen Liu,Jianghao Lin,Zixuan Guo,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ProAct，一种利用空闲时间主动预测并满足用户需求的智能体架构，配合ProActEval基准测试，在任务效率、用户负担和幻觉率方面显著优于反应式基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体仅在收到用户提示后才响应，空闲时间被浪费，无法主动为未来用户需求做准备，忽略了提升服务效率和质量的重要机会。

Method: ProAct通过分析对话历史与持久记忆来预测即将出现的需求，在空闲期间迭代获取信息，填补知识空白并准备证据。同时构建ProActEval基准，包含200个场景、40个领域，涵盖可预测需求链和多样化用户认知特征。

Result: 在ProActEval上，任务所需交互轮次减少14.8%，用户努力降低11.7%，幻觉率下降28.1%。在MemBench上，反思准确性达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: ProAct验证了主动式智能体的有效性，将空闲计算资源转化为预判和准备能力，显著提升了任务效率和可靠性。

Abstract: While AI agents demonstrate remarkable capabilities in reasoning and tool use, they remain fundamentally reactive: they compute responses only after explicit user prompts. This paradigm ignores a critical opportunity: the idle time between interactions is largely wasted, leaving agents unable to prepare for future user needs. To bridge this gap, we introduce ProAct, a proactive agent architecture that leverages idle-time compute to anticipate and fulfill likely upcoming user needs. By analyzing evolving dialogue history together with persistent memory, ProAct predicts upcoming needs and iteratively acquires information, allowing the agent to resolve knowledge gaps and prepare evidence before the user initiates a query.To rigorously evaluate proactive capabilities, we also introduce ProActEval, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 200 scenarios across 40 domains, featuring predictable need chains and diverse user cognitive profiles. Empirical results demonstrate significant advantages over reactive baselines. ProAct accelerates task completion by reducing required turns by 14.8%, decreases user effort by 11.7%, and cuts hallucination rates by 28.1% on ProActEval. Furthermore, MemBench evaluations confirm that ProAct achieves state-of-the-art reflective accuracy, underscoring its sustained and robust performance.

</details>


### [131] [Creative Quality Alignment: Expert Tacit Knowledge Transfer via Chain-of-Thought Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25977)
*Bo Zou,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文实证实现了校准惊喜（Calibrated Surprise）创意质量指标，在严格工程条件下（低数据成本、小基座模型）验证了其可行性，发现约100条专家CoT标注足以通过架构对偶性实现创意质量对齐（CQA），并识别了现有对齐数据集在受众建模和现实逻辑覆盖方面的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证校准惊喜（Calibrated Surprise）这一创意质量指标在工程层面是否成立，并探索在严格条件下（低数据成本、小基座模型）能否实现高质量的创意对齐。

Method: 采用BC协议生成约100条专家思维链（CoT）标注作为训练数据，通过创意质量对齐（CQA）方法进行工程实现，利用单条件分布架构中欣赏端与生成端的对偶性实现校准迁移。

Result: 在低数据成本和小基座模型的严格条件下，约100个CoT示例足以实现创意质量对齐；发现了现有对齐数据集在受众建模和现实逻辑覆盖方面的系统性偏倚。

Conclusion: 校准惊喜指标在工程层面成立；欣赏端与生成端之间的架构对偶性是少量样本即有效的结构性原因，而非纯经验性观察（区别于LIMA）。

Abstract: This paper provides an empirical implementation of the creative quality metric proposed in Calibrated Surprise (Zou & Xu, 2026a). The question this paper addresses is: does this mathematical claim hold at the engineering level?
  To make the answer as general as possible, we deliberately choose the strictest engineering conditions: low data cost and a small base model. Training data comes from approximately 100 expert chain-of-thought (CoT) annotations produced by the BC Protocol (Zou & Xu, 2026b).
  We also identify a data bias: most publicly available alignment datasets are skewed toward craft-related knowledge, while audience modeling and reality-logic coverage are systematically weak.
  We use the term Creative Quality Alignment (CQA) to describe this class of engineering methods. We also offer a supporting theoretical observation: in an LLM with a single conditional distribution architecture, calibrating the appreciation side automatically transfers to the generation side via architectural duality. This is the structural reason why ~100 CoT examples are sufficient -- not a purely empirical observation like LIMA (Zhou et al., 2023).

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Verifiable Secure Aggregation via Dual Servers with Linear Tags in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24054)
*Yufei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轻量级密码原语（伪随机函数和双服务器架构）的安全可验证联邦学习聚合方案，在保持通信开销与明文聚合相当、验证标签大小恒定的同时，实现了用户隐私保护、端到端安全聚合与验证，并在20K输入维度下将用户计算时间降至18ms（比OPSA快约7倍），验证时间降至9.5ms（比OPSA快约2.4倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习存在服务器利用梯度反转攻击泄露用户隐私或操纵聚合结果的问题，影响全局模型的效用，亟需一种既能保护用户隐私又能验证聚合正确性的高效方案。

Method: 采用伪随机函数（PRFs）和非共谋双服务器架构，实现轻量级密码聚合与服务器间的相互验证，通信开销与明文聚合相当，验证标签大小恒定。

Result: 在20K输入维度下，用户计算时间降至18ms（约OPSA的1/7），验证时间降至9.5ms（约OPSA的1/2.4），显著降低了计算和验证开销。

Conclusion: 该方案在保护用户隐私的同时实现了端到端的安全可验证聚合，且开销低、可扩展性强，适用于大规模参与者参与的联邦学习场景。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training by aggregating local updates without requiring raw data sharing. However, prior studies have shown that servers can exploit gradient inversion to compromise user privacy or manipulate aggregation results, undermining the utility of the global model. To address these concerns, we propose a secure and verifiable aggregation scheme with lightweight cryptographic primitives for FL. Our method leverages pseudo-random functions (PRFs) and a non-colluding dual-server architecture to achieve secure aggregation with mutual server verification, while maintaining communication overhead comparable to plaintext aggregation and a constant verification tag size. Crucially, it preserves user privacy and achieves end-to-end secure aggregation with verification. Moreover, our scheme significantly reduces both user computation and verification overhead, making it suitable for FL with a large number of participants. For instance, with an input dimension of 20K, user computation time is reduced to 18 ms, approximately 7$\times$ faster than OPSA, while verification time decreases to 9.5 ms, approximately 2.4$\times$ faster than OPSA.

</details>


### [133] [Microbenchmarking Cloud Cryptographic Workloads for Privacy-Preserving Healthcare IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24063)
*Jeremiah L. Webb,Laxima Niure Kandel,Deepti Gupta,Lavanya Elluri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对AWS和Azure的FaaS平台与KMS集成下的核心密码学工作负载（SHA HMAC、AES、ECC、RSA）进行了全面的微基准测试，分析了x86_64和Arm64架构、6种编程语言、多种内存配置和实例类型对性能和成本效率的影响，为医疗物联网应用确定了最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学操作是云安全架构的核心组件，但其在不同云服务、硬件架构和编程语言实现下的综合性能特征尚不明确；特别是医疗物联网设备高度脆弱且频繁成为攻击目标，但其云安全架构中的密码学性能权衡仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 在AWS和Azure的FaaS与KMS集成环境下，使用EC2和Azure VM的突发优化实例类型，对SHA HMAC生成、AES加解密、ECC签名生成与验证、RSA加解密等核心密码学工作负载进行微基准测试。测试覆盖x86_64和Arm64两种CPU架构、Rust/Go/Python/Java/C#/TypeScript六种编程语言、多种内存分配配置和不同实例类型。

Result: 识别出FaaS环境中密码学工作负载的最优配置，在性能、成本效率之间取得平衡，为医疗物联网应用提供安全及时的数据保护。

Conclusion: 通过多维度的系统化基准测试，揭示了密码学操作在不同云平台、硬件架构和编程语言之间的性能差异与权衡，为医疗物联网云安全架构的优化设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Cryptographic operations are an essential component of cloud security architectures; their comprehensive performance characterization across different cloud services, hardware architectures, and programming language implementations remains unknown. Specifically, healthcare IoT devices are highly vulnerable and frequently targeted, yet the cryptographic performance trade offs in their cloud security architectures remain poorly understood. This research presents an extensive microbenchmark study evaluating the performance of core cryptographic workloads, including SHA HMAC generation, AES encryption, decryption, Elliptic Curve Cryptography (ECC) signature generation and verification, and RSA encryption, decryption, across Function as a Service (FaaS) integrated with Key Management Services (KMS) from Amazon Web Services (AWS) and Microsoft Azure. We evaluate FaaS platforms using Elastic Compute Cloud (EC2) instances and Azure Virtual Machines, specifically using burst optimized instance types to analyze performance under typical cloud workload patterns. The benchmark encompasses a comprehensive multi dimensional analysis spanning two CPU architectures (x86 64 and Arm64), six widely adopted programming languages (Rust, Go, Python, Java, C#, and TypeScript), multiple memory allocation configurations, and diverse instance types to capture the complex interplay between these factors. This study identifies optimal configurations for cryptographic workloads in FaaS environments, improving performance and cost efficiency while enabling secure and timely data protection for healthcare IoT applications.

</details>


### [134] [Unlocking Apple's Private Cloud Compute: An Analysis of Privacy-Preserving Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24239)
*Yannik Dittmar,Marvin Jerome Stephan,Thomas Völkl,Matthias Hollick,Jiska Classen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对Apple的Private Cloud Compute (PCC)系统进行了逆向工程，评估其隐私保护声称，并开放了非公开接口以支持自定义查询和独立基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动设备AI解决方案依赖大量敏感数据，引发隐私担忧。Apple的PCC声称不存储用户数据且用户输入与账户不可关联，但存在二进制不透明、缺乏可复现构建和符号等问题，限制了对其隐私保护和高精度声称的验证。

Method: 对PCC在移动设备上的实现进行逆向工程，评估隐私方面；开放本地设备上的非公开接口以支持自定义PCC查询；独立基准测试PCC模型。

Result: 实现了超越Apple预期用例的访问级别，独立完成了PCC模型的基准测试，并公开了PCC基准测试框架供未来研究使用。

Conclusion: PCC的隐私保护声称因二进制不透明而难以验证，本文通过逆向工程和开放接口的方式为学术界提供了评估手段，但需进一步研究以确认其可信度。

Abstract: Many existing Artificial Intelligence (AI) solutions on mobile devices rely on an extensive collection of sensitive data, raising privacy concerns and often requiring storage for both context and model improvement. Apple's Private Cloud Compute (PCC) aims to address this by emphasizing mobile device integration and a privacy-first design. The central claim of PCC is that it does not store any user data and that user input and user accounts are unlinkable.
  While most of the PCC system specifications are public, compiled binaries add a layer of opaqueness. There are no reproducible builds, and there are no symbols within those binaries, creating potential discrepancies between the specification and what is shipped to the user. Additionally, the underlying models and interfaces for querying PCC are not openly accessible, limiting academic evaluation of model properties, such as accuracy. This poses a challenge in assessing whether a privacy-preserving approach like PCC is actually trustworthy while also providing high-quality answers.
  We are the first to reverse-engineer the PCC implementation on mobile devices to evaluate privacy aspects and to open its non-public interfaces on local devices to support custom PCC queries. We demonstrate this level of access beyond Apple's intended use cases by independently benchmarking the PCC model. We enable future research by making our PCC benchmarking framework publicly available.

</details>


### [135] [Concept Drift Adaptation Using Self-Supervised and Reinforcement Learning In Android Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24294)
*Ahmed Sabbah,Mohammad Kharma,Mohammad Alkhanafseh,Radi Jarrar,Samer Zein,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的Android恶意软件检测器自适应维护框架，通过自监督学习固定编码器、测量潜在漂移并用PPO控制器选择低成本维护动作，在非平稳部署条件下实现了时间性能、记忆保留和维护成本之间的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android恶意软件检测器在部署后因概念漂移导致性能下降，而完全重训练成本过高。

Method: 将部署时的维护建模为序贯决策问题：初始化时通过自监督学习学习稳定潜在表示并冻结编码器；在固定表示空间中测量潜在漂移；使用可训练适配器和分类头进行轻量级下游适配；基于PPO的控制器根据检测器状态（当前效用、记忆集保留、潜在漂移指标、更新成本）选择低成本维护动作。

Result: 在模拟器和真实Android恶意软件数据集（静态与动态特征）上，RL控制器提供了强大的成本感知自适应策略，始终保持在最优策略之列，在时间性能、记忆保留和维护成本之间实现了有利平衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效应对部署后的概念漂移，以低成本方式维护检测器性能。

Abstract: Android malware detectors often degrade after deployment because of concept drift, while full retraining at each maintenance step is costly. We propose a chronological adaptive maintenance framework that models deployment-time maintenance as a sequential decision problem. The framework learns a stable latent representation through self-supervised learning during initialization, freezes the encoder, measures latent drift in the fixed representation space, and performs lightweight downstream adaptation using a trainable adapter and classification head. A proximal policy optimization controller selects low-cost maintenance actions based on the detector state, including current utility, retention on a fixed memory set, latent drift indicators, and update cost. We evaluate the framework under a causal deployment-style protocol on emulator and real Android malware datasets with static and dynamic features. Results show that the RL controller provides a strong cost-aware adaptation strategy, consistently remaining among the top-performing policies while achieving a favorable balance between temporal performance, memory retention, and maintenance cost under non-stationary deployment conditions.

</details>


### [136] [An Empirical Evaluation of LLM-Generated Code Security Across Prompting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24298)
*Mohammed Kharma,Ahmed Sabbah,Mohammad Alkhanafseh,Mohammad Hammoudeh,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对五种大语言模型和四种编程语言生成的代码进行了安全质量评估，提出了一种弱点感知的零样本思维链（WA-0CoT）提示策略。实验发现，提示策略虽能影响漏洞类别的构成分布，但无法显著降低漏洞频率或密度，提示工程本身不足以可靠地提升代码安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的代码常忽视安全问题（如弱加密、输入验证不当），但缺乏系统性的安全质量评估，尤其是多种提示工程方法对代码安全性的影响尚不明确。

Method: 在五种LLM和四种编程语言（Java、C++、C、Python）上进行实证评估；提出WA-0CoT提示策略，利用CWE映射为提示注入安全上下文；采用卡方检验进行统计分析。

Result: 提示策略在各方法间未显著降低漏洞频率或密度；但提示策略（包括WA-0CoT）显著影响了漏洞类别（CWE）的构成分布，且效果因编程语言而异。

Conclusion: 安全感知提示能改变生成弱点的结构分布，但仅靠提示工程不足以可靠降低整体漏洞水平；需要结合语言感知和模型感知的提示设计来评估和提升代码安全性。

Abstract: The growing use of Large Language Models (LLMs) for automated code generation has enhanced software development efficiency, but often at the cost of security. Generated code frequently overlooks critical concerns, leaving it vulnerable to issues such as weak encryption and improper input validation. To investigate this problem, we present a comprehensive empirical evaluation of the security quality of LLM-generated code across five LLMs and four programming languages (Java, C++, C, and Python), examining the impact of multiple prompt engineering methods. We introduce a weaknesses-aware zero-shot chain-of-thought (WA-0CoT) prompting strategy that enriches prompts with security context using CWE mappings to guide model reasoning. Our empirical analysis, supported by chi-square tests, finds no statistically significant reductions in vulnerability frequency or density across prompt methods. However, prompting strategies, including WA-0CoT, systematically influence the compositional distribution of CWE categories, with effects varying by programming language. These findings suggest that while security-aware prompting alters the structure of generated weaknesses, prompt engineering alone is insufficient to reliably reduce overall vulnerability levels. The results highlight the importance of language-aware and model-aware prompt design when evaluating the security properties of LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [137] [Enhancing Reliability in LLM-Based Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24300)
*Mohammed F. Kharma,Mohammad Alkhanafseh,Ahmed Sabbah,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MA-CoT框架，在代码生成的思维链提示中嵌入CWE缓解引导和语言感知安全机制，使安全缺陷减少57.6%-94.5%，是唯一能持续提升安全可靠性的提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型代码生成在功能正确性上表现良好，但安全可靠性差，且不同语言和提示策略间安全性不一致。现有提示工程主要优化功能正确性，未能确保持续稳定的安全输出。

Method: 提出Mitigation-Aware Chain-of-Thought (MA-CoT)框架，在思维链中嵌入任务特定的CWE缓解引导和语言感知安全防护。在3个LLM、3种编程语言、4种提示策略下，基于200任务的基准数据集和LLMSecEval外部验证集进行评估，采用静态分析+专家验证的评估方法。

Result: MA-CoT在基准数据集上将总安全发现从92降至39（减少57.6%），在LLMSecEval上从73降至4（减少94.5%）。高严重性发现分别减少56.7%和95.6%。Zero-shot和CoT策略不可靠，在C语言中甚至可能增加漏洞。MA-CoT是唯一能持续提升安全可靠性的策略。

Conclusion: MA-CoT能显著降低生成代码的安全漏洞，但残余风险集中在与操作系统和工具链相关的加固模式中，说明除了提示策略外，还需结合安全优先的原语（secure-by-construction primitives）来进一步提升代码安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for code generation, but their security reliability remains inconsistent across languages and prompting strategies. Existing prompt engineering improves functional correctness but rarely ensures consistent security outcomes. We introduce the \textit{Mitigation-Aware Chain-of-Thought (MA-CoT)} framework, which embeds task-specific CWE mitigation guidance and language-aware safeguards to reduce recurring vulnerabilities in generated code. We evaluate MA-CoT across three LLMs (gpt-5, claude-4.5, gemini-2.5), three programming languages (C, Java, Python), and four prompting strategies (Vanilla, Zero-shot, CoT, MA-CoT) on a 200-task primary dataset, with external validation on LLMSecEval. Using static analysis with expert validation, MA-CoT reduces total security findings from 92 to 39 (57.6\%) on the primary dataset and from 73 to 4 (94.5\%) on LLMSecEval. High-severity findings (Blocker + Critical) drop from 90 to 39 (56.7\%) and from 45 to 2 (95.6\%), respectively. Across both datasets, MA-CoT is the only strategy that consistently improves security reliability; Zero-shot and CoT are less reliable and may increase vulnerability, especially in C. We further introduce a strict layered attribution of vulnerability drivers (language-core vs. stack layers) and show that residual risk concentrates in hardening-oriented patterns (e.g., OS- and toolchain-dependent), motivating secure-by-construction primitives alongside prompting.

</details>


### [138] [Reframing LLM Agent Security as an Agent-Human Interaction Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24309)
*Peiran Wang,Ying Li,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了LLM智能体安全本质上是一个智能体-人交互（AHI）问题。通过对59篇学术论文、21个生产智能体系统和26个安全插件的系统性分析，发现三大类人机安全机制（策略规范、运行时审批、作用域配置）占据行业实践主导地位，而学术界重点研究的技术（意图锚定、信任标注）零落地。当前人机交互安全机制存在认知负担与安全保证间的根本性权衡。作者提出三点贡献：论证人类参与不可或缺、量化产学差距、提出AHI安全研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体安全研究多聚焦于纯算法方案，忽视了人类参与在安全决策中的核心作用。作者通过系统性实证分析，揭示学术界与工业界在安全机制部署上存在巨大鸿沟，并指出当前人机安全机制存在严重缺陷（认知负担与安全保证的权衡），从而呼吁将AHI安全作为一个独立的研究领域确立起来。

Method: 系统性文献综述与实证分析：截至2026年4月，对59篇学术论文、21个生产智能体系统、26个安全插件进行结构化比较。通过编码分析识别安全机制类别（策略规范、运行时审批、作用域配置、意图锚定、信任标注等），统计部署频率并对比学术界与工业界的关注差异。

Result: 三大类人机安全机制（策略规范14/21、运行时审批15/21、作用域配置16/21）在工业界广泛部署，而学术界重点研究的意图锚定和信任标注零落地。当前人类参与机制存在认知负担与安全保证的根本性权衡，用户陷入审批疲劳与智能体失控之间的两难困境。通过LLM与人类意图对齐的系统比较，论证了人类参与在现有能力水平下不可替代。

Conclusion: LLM智能体安全应被重新定义为AHI问题。提议将AHI安全提升为"一流的研究公民"，建立独立的设计原则、评估方法和理论基础。提出三个方向的研究议程，以弥合产学差距并解决人类参与机制的根本性权衡。

Abstract: We argue that LLM agent security is fundamentally an agent-human interaction (AHI) problem, not a purely algorithmic one. To substantiate this position, we conduct a systematic analysis of 59 academic papers, 21 production agent systems, and 26 security plugins as of April 2026. Our analysis reveals a striking pattern: the three widely deployed human-centric security mechanisms (policy specification, runtime approval, and scope configuration) dominate industry practice, each adopted by at least 14 of 21 systems (14, 15, and 16, respectively), while the categories most heavily studied in academia (intent anchoring and trust labeling) see zero production deployment. Yet current human participation mechanisms are far from satisfactory: they suffer from a fundamental trade-off between cognitive burden and security guarantees, leaving users caught between approval fatigue and uncontrolled agent autonomy. We make three contributions. First, through a systematic comparison of LLM-based and human-based intent alignment, we argue that human participation in agent security decisions is indispensable given current capabilities. Second, we quantify a pronounced industry-academia mismatch: the security mechanisms that practitioners actually deploy receive scant research attention, while the approaches that researchers favor remain undeployed. Third, we propose a three-direction research agenda and call for AHI security to be recognized as a first-class research citizen, one that demands its own design principles, evaluation methods, and theoretical foundations.

</details>


### [139] [Five Queries Are Enough: Query-Efficient and Surrogate-Free Membership Inference Attacks on RAG via Entailment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24312)
*Nguyen Linh Bao Nguyen,Wanlun Ma,Viet Vo,Alsharif Abuadbba,Minghong Fang,Jun Zhang,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MEntA，一种基于自然语言蕴含的高效、隐蔽且无需替代模型的成员推理攻击方法，仅需5次查询即可在RAG系统中以高达0.991 AUC的精度检测特定文档是否存在，攻击成本比现有方法降低65倍，且能绕过现有防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在减少LLM幻觉的同时引入了新的隐私风险：模型输出可能泄露检索语料库中是否存在特定文档（成员推理攻击）。现有攻击方法依赖易被检测的模板化查询或需要大量非模板化查询，成本高且实用性低。作者希望探索在有限预算、无替代模型、隐蔽且防御无关的条件下，能否利用非模板化查询实现有效的成员推理攻击。

Method: 提出MEntA（Membership Entailment Attack），核心思路是利用自然语言蕴含（NLI）最大化每次查询的信息增益。攻击者提出低成本、广泛的信息寻求性问题，然后通过测量模型响应与候选文档之间的蕴含关系来判断文档是否存在于检索语料库中。该方法无需昂贵的影子模型（shadow models）和大量查询预算。

Result: 在NFCorpus、SCIDOCS和TREC-COVID三个数据集上，MEntA仅需5次查询即可达到最高0.991 AUC，在同等条件下比现有方法高出0.20-0.50 AUC。在最新的RAG防御机制下依然有效，而现有检测器要么无法检测到MEntA，要么对良性查询产生高误报率。攻击总成本相比现有最先进攻击降低65倍。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了RAG系统中存在现实且低成本的隐私泄露风险，证明了在有限预算下进行隐蔽成员推理攻击的可行性，强调了发展隐私感知的检索与防御机制的紧迫性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has become central to large language model (LLM) deployments, grounding responses in enterprise or proprietary data to reduce hallucinations. However, this design introduces a new privacy risk: model outputs may signal the presence of specific documents in the retrieval corpus, enabling membership inference attacks (MIAs) that leak sensitive information. Existing MIAs are feasible, but they often rely on easily detected templated queries or require many non-templated yet costly and repetitive queries, limiting practicality. We ask: Can an adversary launch a limited-budget, surrogate-free, stealthy, and defense-agnostic membership inference attack using non-templated queries? We present MEntA (Membership Entailment Attack), a query-efficient MIA that leverages natural-language entailment to maximize information gained per query. By asking low-cost, broad, information-seeking questions and measuring entailment between model responses and candidate documents, MEntA eliminates the need for costly shadow models and large query budgets. Across NFCorpus, SCIDOCS, and TREC-COVID, MEntA achieves up to 0.991 AUC with only 5 queries, outperforming prior methods by 0.20 to 0.50 AUC under equivalent conditions. It remains effective under state-of-the-art (SOTA) RAG defenses, while current detectors either miss MEntA or flag benign queries at high rates. Regarding cost, MEntA reduces total attack cost by up to 65 $\times$ lower compared to SOTA attacks under the same attack setting. Our findings expose the feasibility of realistic, low-cost privacy leakage in RAG systems and highlight the urgent need for privacy-aware retrieval and defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [140] [Steering Beyond the Support: Adversarial Training on Unsupervised Jailbroken Activation Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24535)
*Luoyu Chen,Weiqi Wang,Zhiyi Tian,Chenhan Zhang,Feng Wu,Jianhuan Huang,Ahmed Asiri,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无监督潜在方向发现的双层对抗训练框架，用于零样本越狱防御。内层通过从拒绝态有害请求激活中外推来模拟多样化的越狱激活，外层训练势诱导引导场将这些对抗性越狱状态推回拒绝区域，同时保持良性不变。在三种LLM和六种经典越狱家族上，攻击成功率大多低于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全引导方法本质上是监督式的，依赖于静态且有限的训练集，而真实越狱攻击不断演变且往往分布外，导致对未知攻击的防御失败。本文旨在解决对未见越狱攻击的防御失败问题。

Method: 提出双层对抗训练框架：内层通过无监督潜在方向发现，从拒绝态有害请求激活中外推模拟多样化的越狱激活，扩展真实越狱激活子空间的覆盖范围；外层训练势诱导引导场，将这些对抗性越狱状态推入拒绝区域，同时保持良性样本不变。

Result: 在三种LLM和六种经典越狱家族上，攻击成功率大多低于5%，训练过程中子空间覆盖率的提升有助于解释泛化能力的改善。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对未知越狱攻击的强零样本防御，子空间覆盖率的动态增长是泛化能力提升的关键因素。

Abstract: Jailbreak prompts can trigger harmful completions on aligned LLMs, In accordance, safety steering has been proposed: test-time activation interventions that steer jailbreak activations to trigger refusal while preserving benign utility. However, existing steering methods are fundamentally supervised and tied to a static, limited training set, whereas real jailbreaks evolve and are often out-of-distributed from the training set, leading to failures on unseen attacks. In this paper, we tackle the failure on unseen jailbreaks problem, base on unsupervised latent direction discovery. We propose a bi-level adversarial training framework for zero-shot jailbreak defense. In the inner step, we simulate diverse jail-broken activations by extrapolating from refusal-state harmful-request activations via unsupervised latent direction discovery, which expands the coverage of real jailbreak activation subspaces. In the outer step, we train a potential-induced steering field to push these adversarial jailbroken states into refusal regions while keeping benign unchanged. Across three LLMs and six classical jailbreak families, our method achieves strong defense with attack success rates mostly below 5%, and rising subspace coverage throughout training helps explain the improved generalization.

</details>


### [141] [AI-Driven Adaptive Adversaries and the Erosion of Cryptographic Trust in Public Key Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24542)
*Petar Radanliev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在人工智能驱动的自适应对抗优化下，公钥密码学（PKC）安全性的削弱问题。核心问题是算法中心的密码安全模型与操作层面的攻击现实之间的日益脱节——攻击者利用实现层面的可观察性而非破解密码原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码安全模型过于关注算法理论安全性，忽视了实际攻击中对手通过实现层面的侧信道信息（如时序、功耗、电磁辐射等）进行攻击的现实。这种模型与现实的错位导致安全评估存在严重缺陷。

Method: 本文通过分析人工智能驱动的自适应对抗优化对PKC安全性的侵蚀，揭示了攻击者如何利用实现层面的可观察性来绕过理论上的密码学安全保障。

Result: 研究发现，在AI驱动的自适应优化下，基于实现层面信息的攻击能够显著削弱PKC的实际安全性，使得传统算法中心的安全模型暴露出结构性缺陷。

Conclusion: 需要重新审视密码安全模型，将实现层面的攻击向量纳入考量，以弥合理论安全与实战安全之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This paper examines the erosion of Public Key Cryptography (PKC) security under adaptive adversarial optimisation driven by artificial intelligence. The problem addressed is the growing mismatch between algorithm-centric cryptographic security models and operational attack realities, where adversaries exploit implementation-level observability rather than breaking cryptographic primitives.

</details>


### [142] [Ellipsoid Control: A White-list Jailbreak Defense via Benign Latent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24552)
*Luoyu Chen,Weiqi Wang,Zhiyi Tian,Feng Wu,Ahmed Asiri,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于白名单视角的对抗越狱攻击防御方法Ellipsoid Control，通过拟合良性数据椭球约束来引导拒绝响应，在不依赖黑名单有害样本的前提下，同时提升安全性与保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表示工程（RepE）的防御方法依赖黑名单监督，即从有害或越狱数据中学习激活变换，但这类数据本质上是不完备且不断演化的，导致模型对未见攻击脆弱；同时该方法无法区分防御是拟合了已知有害分布还是真正保护了良性潜在区域。

Method: 提出Ellipsoid Control测试时防御方法：1）通过投影梯度下降在任意输入上引发拒绝回答；2）从大量良性数据中拟合各向异性的良性几何椭球，作为约束限制更新步长，最小化对良性潜在几何结构的扭曲。

Result: 在多个大语言模型、多种越狱攻击、良性任务和安全边界评估上，Ellipsoid Control在增强安全性的同时更好地保持了模型效用。

Conclusion: 白名单视角的防御方法有效，通过保护良性潜在区域而非拟合有害分布，能够在拒绝回答时保持模型对良性输入的响应能力。

Abstract: Representation engineering (RepE) defenses have shown strong robustness against jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs). However, these methods fundamentally rely on black-list supervision: they learn jailbreak-to-refusal activation transformations from harmful or jailbreak data that are inherently incomplete and continuously evolving. Hence, the performance of RepE-based defenses becomes tightly coupled to the quality and coverage of collected harmful samples, leaving models vulnerable to unseen attacks. This reliance also obscures the distinction between defenses that fit known harmful distributions and defenses that protect a benign latent region without estimating the harmful distribution. We adopt the opposite, the white-list perspective, by leveraging the accessibility and abundance of benign data. The goal is to elicit refusal on arbitrary inputs while ensuring that harmless inputs are not falsely rejected. This shifts the core research question to: How can we design a robust benign-latent preservation mechanism such that the benign latent distribution remains intact while refusal is elicited? To answer this, we propose Ellipsoid Control, a test-time defense. It performs projected gradient descent that can elicit refusal on arbitrary inputs, aiming to improve defense effectiveness. At the same time, an anisotropic benign-geometry ellipsoid is fitted from abundant benign data to constrain the update to minimize distortion of the benign latent geometry. This tight constraint helps preserve model utility. Across multiple LLMs, jailbreak attacks, benign tasks, and safety-boundary evaluations, Ellipsoid Control consistently enhances safety while better preserving utility, demonstrating the effectiveness of the white-list approach for jailbreak defense

</details>


### [143] [Analyzing Concentration, Temporal Routines and Targeting in Public Ransomware Leak Site Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24559)
*Lea Müller,York Yannikos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究利用325个勒索软件组织发布的27,000余条数据泄露站点帖子，分析发现勒索软件组织在受害者集中度、时间规律和目标选择上并非随机行为，而是表现出明显的活动集中性、时间规律性和选择性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件已从自动化攻击转向"人为操控"模式，针对特定组织以获取更高赎金。攻击者在数据泄露站点公开受害者信息的行为留下了可追踪的痕迹，为研究勒索软件组织的操作规律提供了可能。

Method: 收集来自325个勒索软件组织的27,000余条数据泄露站点帖子，以此作为行为痕迹数据集，从受害者集中度、时间规律性和目标选择性三个维度进行分析。

Result: 勒索软件组织的行为并非完全随机，数据泄露站点上的可观察痕迹显示出活动集中性、时间规律性和选择性模式。

Conclusion: 勒索软件组织存在可识别的操作规律，这为理解其行为模式、制定防御策略提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Ransomware has grown to become one of the most damaging types of cybercrime, affecting private and public organizations in any sector. While early types of ransomware targeted many victims via automated attacks, ransomware groups have started to specifically target organizations and companies in the expectation of receiving larger ransoms. To increase the pressure on victims, most groups host so-called data leak sites, where information about their victims is made public. The shift towards 'human-operated' ransomware together with easily accessible behavioral traces available from data leak sites makes research investigating operational regularities of ransomware groups of interest. Using leak site posts as behavioral traces of ransomware groups, we created a dataset consisting of over 27,000 posts from 325 groups. Based on this dataset, we analyzed victim concentration, temporal routines and targeting regularities. Our findings suggest that groups do not behave entirely random. Instead, the observable traces found on leak sites show concentration of activity, temporal routines and selective patterns.

</details>


### [144] [Demystifying the Mythos or Disrupting Bugonomics? From Zero-Day Asymmetry to Defender Remediation Throughput](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24632)
*Alfredo Pesoli,Herman Errico,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从"漏洞经济学"视角分析LLM驱动的漏洞发现：LLM降低了候选漏洞生成、代码理解、测试构建、影响证明和报告编写的成本，但并未解决防御方在验证、分类、修复和发布方面的瓶颈。短期内真正的变化不是更多零日漏洞，而是防御方修复吞吐量的压力增大，尤其在开源生态中尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多强调LLM在漏洞发现方面的能力提升，但很少从经济成本和激励机制的角度系统分析LLM驱动漏洞发现对攻防双方的实际影响。作者希望填补这一空白，提供一个更全面的"漏洞经济学"分析框架。

Method: 采用漏洞经济学（bugonomics）分析框架，结合Anthropic Mythos Preview、Mozilla Firefox协作的公开数据，以及公开的利用市场定价锚点和漏洞奖励计划数据，进行定性加定量的经济分析。

Result: LLM使候选漏洞生成、代码理解、测试构建、影响证明和报告编写在代码库规模上变得更便宜。但防御方的瓶颈从"发现更多漏洞"转向"吸收、验证、分类、打补丁和发布更大流量的报告"。开源生态中，LLM辅助发现增加了报告量，但维护方的验证、分类、资金和发布能力并未同步扩展。

Conclusion: 近期的转变不是更多零日漏洞的出现，而是防御方修复吞吐量的压力增大：低信号候选漏洞变得更便宜，证据丰富的修复变得更重要，稀缺的能力向维护者审查和发布工作转移。LLM对安全生态的实质性影响在于改变攻防经济学的分布，而非单纯提升攻击能力。

Abstract: Recent demonstrations of large language models producing candidate and confirmed vulnerabilities in production software have renewed the narrative that AI will reshape offensive and defensive security. Headlines emphasize capability; they rarely interrogate costs and incentives. This paper examines LLM-driven vulnerability discovery through a bugonomics lens: the operational economics of producing, proving, prioritizing, and fixing security-relevant defects. Historically, the most visible high-end bugonomics was offense-priced because production-grade zero-days and exploit chains were expensive specialist outputs for governments, brokers, and offensive vendors. Defender-side bugonomics already existed in vulnerability research, reward programs, and vendor remediation work; LLM-assisted systems change its scale and distribution. They make candidate generation, code comprehension, harness construction, proof-of-impact drafting, and report preparation cheaper at codebase scale. Exploits and proofs of concept remain important, but in defender workflows they primarily prove impact, guide prioritization, and justify remediation. The resulting bottleneck is not only finding more bugs; it is absorbing, validating, triaging, patching, and shipping a larger stream of reports. Using public data from Anthropic's Mythos Preview and Mozilla Firefox collaborations, along with public exploit-market price anchors and vulnerability reward programs, we argue that the near-term shift is not simply more zero-days. It is a move toward broader defender remediation throughput: low-signal candidates become cheaper, evidence-rich remediation become more important, and scarce capacity shifts toward maintainer review and release work. The effect is acute in open source, where LLM-assisted discovery can increase report volume while maintainer-side validation, triage, funding, and release capacity may not scale.

</details>


### [145] [RouteScan: A Non-Intrusive Approach to Auditing MoE LLMs Safety via Expert Routing Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24817)
*Bo Lv,Zhiheng Xu,KeDong Xiu,Ruyi Ding,Tianhang Zheng,Zhibo Wang,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出RouteScan，一种通过GPU级专家路由遥测（而非内容）来非侵入式检测MoE大模型中 harmful behaviors 的审计框架，在保护用户隐私的同时实现了高检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于内容的审计方法需访问用户提示、模型输入或输出，暴露敏感用户信息，造成了LLM安全与用户隐私之间的根本矛盾；而MoE模型中稀疏专家路由在不同输入下产生不同的GPU执行足迹，可被利用来实现非侵入式审计。

Method: RouteScan利用预填充阶段专家模块分配的活跃GPU线程数作为微架构指纹，构建轻量级检测管道，隔离跨域不变风险指标以实现恶意提示的精确识别。

Result: 在具有不同路由设计的开源MoE LLM上，RouteScan在未见过的有害领域上AUROC超过0.93，在新型越狱包装下超过0.96；反向测试表明收集的专家路由遥测对提示重建提供的信息有限。

Conclusion: RouteScan在保护用户隐私的前提下实现了强泛化能力的有害行为检测，相比基于内容的审计方法具有实际的隐私优势。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have become an increasingly important paradigm for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs). As MoE models are increasingly deployed in real-world services, safety auditing becomes necessary to verify whether these models produce or facilitate harmful behaviors during operation. However, existing content-based auditing methods typically require access to user prompts, model inputs, or generated outputs, potentially exposing sensitive user information and creating a fundamental tension between LLM safety and user privacy. On the other hand, we observe that, in MoE models, sparse expert routing maps different inputs to activate different expert-execution patterns, producing measurable footprints in low-level GPU execution telemetry. Inspired by this observation, we propose RouteScan, a non-intrusive auditing framework for detecting harmful behaviors through GPU-level expert routing telemetry. Specifically, RouteScan utilizes the number of active GPU threads allocated to expert modules during the prefilling phase as a discriminative micro-architectural fingerprint, and builds a lightweight detection pipeline that isolates cross-domain invariant risk indicators for the precise identification of malicious prompts. Comprehensive evaluations on open-source MoE LLMs with distinct routing designs demonstrate that RouteScan achieves strong generalization, with an AUROC exceeding 0.93 on unseen harmful domains and 0.96 under novel jailbreak wrappers. Moreover, empirical inversion tests show that the collected expert routing telemetry provides limited information for prompt reconstruction, suggesting a practical privacy advantage over content-based auditing methods.

</details>


### [146] [Reflect-Guard: Enhancing LLM Safeguards against Adversarial Prompts via Logical Self-Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24834)
*Lixing Lin,Juli You,Yue Li,Luyun Lin,Yiqing Wang,Zhen Zhang,Moxuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Reflect-Guard通过对Llama-Guard-3-8B进行参数高效微调（QLoRA），使其具备思维链自我反思能力，仅用1000个样本和0.5%参数更新，在WildGuardTest上将F1从0.770提升至0.842，对对抗性提示的召回率从0.513提升至0.921；在JailbreakBench上将攻击成功率从10.3%降至1.8%（相对降低82.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全分类器（如Llama Guard）能有效检测明显有害提示，但对通过角色扮演、虚构框架和间接请求伪装恶意意图的对抗性越狱攻击仍存在脆弱性。

Method: 方法包括三个步骤：(1) 从GPT-4o-mini蒸馏分析推理能力到结构化的反思标注；(2) 通过QLoRA对Llama-Guard-3-8B进行参数高效微调；(3) 使模型在给出安全判定前生成逻辑性的自我反思。

Result: WildGuardTest上F1从0.770提升至0.842（+7.2pp），对抗性提示召回率从0.513提升至0.921（+40.8pp）；JailbreakBench上攻击成功率从10.3%降至1.8%（相对降低82.5%）。仅使用1000个训练样本，更新约0.5%参数（~42M）。

Conclusion: 教会安全分类器推理对抗性意图（而非仅对表面模式进行分类）是实现鲁棒LLM安全的有前景方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) safety classifiers such as Llama Guard are effective at detecting overtly harmful prompts but remain vulnerable to adversarial jailbreak attacks that disguise malicious intent through role-play scenarios, fictional framing, and indirect requests. We present Reflect-Guard, a method that augments LLM-based safety classifiers with chain-of-thought self-reflection capabilities through parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Our approach distills analytical reasoning from GPT-4o-mini into structured reflection annotations, then trains Llama-Guard-3-8B via QLoRA to generate logical self-reflections before issuing safety verdicts. Using only 1000 training examples and updating just 0.5% of model parameters (~42M), Reflect-Guard achieves substantial improvements on two challenging benchmarks. On WildGuardTest, F1 score improves from 0.770 to 0.842 (+7.2 pp), with recall on adversarial prompts increasing from 0.513 to 0.921 (+40.8 pp). On JailbreakBench, the attack success rate drops from 10.3% to 1.8%, representing an 82.5% relative reduction. These gains are especially pronounced on adversarial inputs, where the explicit reasoning step enables the model to see through obfuscation techniques that defeat standard pattern-matching approaches. Our results demonstrate that teaching safety classifiers to reason about adversarial intent, rather than simply classify surface patterns, is a promising direction for robust LLM safety.

</details>


### [147] [SEED: Semi-supervised Continual MalwarE Detection for Tackling ConcEpt Drift on a BuDget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24903)
*Suresh Kumar Amalapuram,Bikraj Shresta,Siva Ram murthy Chebiyam,Bheemarjuna Reddy Tamma,Sumohana S Channappayya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SEED方法，在有限标注条件下，通过结合二元交叉熵目标、半监督持续学习和主动学习，利用SVD投影和余弦距离不确定性量化，有效检测未知恶意软件，在BODMAS和AndroZoo数据集上相比HCL*分别提升40%和14%的AUT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于层次对比损失(HCL)的恶意软件检测方法在完全标注数据下表现良好，但安全领域标注数据获取困难，在部分标注设置下HCL性能显著下降，尤其对缺乏强语义结构的数据集（如BODMAS）检测未知恶意软件效果不佳。

Method: 提出SEED方法：1）对部分标注的已见任务，使用SVD将未标注样本投影到已见数据构建的表示空间，配对标注样本增强表示一致性；2）对完全无标注的未见任务，用余弦距离量化不确定性，选择最不确定样本供分析师标注；3）定制二元交叉熵目标；4）引入延迟缓冲区更新策略减少重放中的标签噪声传播。

Result: 在仅使用20%标注数据的已见任务上，SEED在BODMAS上AUT提升40%，在AndroZoo上提升14%，在APIGraph上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SEED在有限监督下有效提升未知恶意软件检测性能，延迟缓冲区更新策略增强了学习稳定性。

Abstract: Machine learning based malware detectors become obsolete over time due to concept drift in benign and malware applications. Recent methods rely on fully labeled data and use hierarchical contrastive loss (HCL) with active learning to improve robustness against drift by exploiting semantic structure in malware representations. However, obtaining labeled data in the security domain is difficult. Under partially labeled settings, HCL suffers significant performance degradation in detecting unseen malware, especially on datasets such as BODMAS where strong semantic structure may not exist. In this paper, we propose SEED, a semantic-structure-agnostic method for malware detection under limited supervision. SEED combines a tailored binary cross-entropy objective with semi-supervised continual learning and active learning. For partially labeled seen tasks, unlabeled samples are projected into a representation space constructed from previously seen data using singular value decomposition, and paired with suitable labeled samples to encourage representation consistency. For unseen tasks with fully unlabeled data, uncertainty is quantified using cosine distance in representation space, and the most uncertain samples are selected for analyst labeling. We evaluate SEED on both Windows and Android malware datasets. Using only 20% labeled data on seen tasks, SEED achieves average AUT improvements of 40% on BODMAS and 14% on AndroZoo for unseen malware detection compared to HCL* (the semi-supervised adaptation of HCL), while remaining competitive on APIGraph. Finally, we introduce a delayed buffer update strategy to reduce label noise propagation during replay and improve learning stability.

</details>


### [148] [Memory-Induced Tool-Drift in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24941)
*Mahavir Dabas,Jihyun Jeong,Ming Jin,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并系统研究了LLM Agent中记忆诱导的工具偏移（memory-induced tool-drift）现象：存储在长期记忆中的个性偏差（如成本意识、不耐烦、风险偏好等）会在不相关上下文中静默影响工具调用。通过MEMDRIFT基准测试和7个前沿模型验证，该现象普遍存在，标准防御措施无法彻底消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent系统同时结合长期记忆（用于个性化）和工具调用接口（用于执行动作），但两者结合时，记忆中的个性偏差可能在不适用的上下文中错误影响工具调用，这一系统性漏洞此前未被研究。

Method: 构建MEMDRIFT基准测试（105个场景，覆盖5个偏差维度、7个专业领域），通过自动化对抗性流水线生成；在7个前沿模型（含扩展推理模型）和3种生产级记忆架构上评估工具偏移；扫描288个MCP服务器上的6,062个工具，识别易受影响的参数；进行机制分析（隐式引导向量、注意力重分布）。

Result: 有偏记忆使偏移分数最高增加+3.6分（1-5分制）；工具偏移在三种生产记忆架构下持续存在；扫描发现608个易受影响参数，并在验证子集上确认偏移存在。机制上，有偏记忆作为隐式引导向量，推动激活沿与显式行为指令相同的潜在方向移动，并将注意力从任务相关上下文重新分配到与目标参数具有表面关键词重叠的记忆条目。

Conclusion: 记忆诱导的工具偏移是一个系统性漏洞，标准防御（基于提示的相关性指令和记忆过滤器）能减少但无法消除偏移。随着Agent代表用户执行越来越重要的操作，需要在记忆管理和工具调用生成的交叉点上开发专门防御。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents combine long-term memory for personalization with tool-calling interfaces for taking actions in the world -- a combination underpinning contemporary production systems. We study a previously unexamined failure of this combination: when personality-driven biases stored in memory (cost-consciousness, impatience, risk tolerance, etc.) silently affect tool calls in contexts where they are not applicable. We call this memory-induced tool-drift and operationalize it through MEMDRIFT, a benchmark of 105 scenarios spanning five bias dimensions and seven professional domains, generated through an automated adversarial pipeline. Across seven frontier models -- including those with extended reasoning -- biased memories raise deflection scores (a judge-scored measure of parameter deviation from unbiased baselines) by up to $+3.6$ points on a 1--5 scale. Tool-drift persists when memory management is handled by three production memory architectures. The phenomenon affects real-world tools: scanning 6{,}062 tools across 288 verified MCP servers, we flag 608 with susceptible parameters and confirm tool-drift on a validated subset. Mechanistically, biased memories act as implicit steering vectors, pushing activations along the same latent directions as explicit behavioral instructions. They also redistribute attention from task-relevant context toward memory entries with surface-level keyword overlap to the target parameter. Standard defenses -- prompt-based relevance instructions and memory filters -- reduce drift but do not eliminate it. As agents take increasingly consequential actions on a user's behalf, memory-induced tool-drift represents a systematic vulnerability that current safeguards do not address, motivating dedicated defenses at the intersection of memory management and tool-call generation.

</details>


### [149] [Security in the Fine-Tuning Lifecycle of Large Language Models: Threats, Defenses,Evaluation, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25073)
*Wenjuan Li,Yitao Liu,Runze Chen,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了大语言模型微调全生命周期的安全问题，提出三阶段（微调前、微调中、微调后）攻击与防御框架，并通过统一实验发现：攻击效果高度依赖模型且非单调递增，单阶段防御难以跨阶段泛化，指出未来研究方向包括配置鲁棒防御、跨阶段防御组合及嵌入空间攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述缺乏覆盖微调全生命周期的统一安全框架，且缺少跨阶段的攻击-防御对比实证评估。

Method: 将攻击与防御机制按干预时机分为微调前、微调中、微调后三个阶段，在统一模型、硬件和协议设置下进行跨阶段对比实验。

Result: 攻击效果高度依赖模型且非单调：权重重写攻击对早期模型有效但对现代开源LLM失效；跨语言后门攻击在1B-4B模型上完全失败；纯良性样本可破坏指令微调模型的安全对齐；单阶段防御难以跨阶段泛化；防御效果取决于模型架构和对齐状态。

Conclusion: 识别出配置鲁棒防御、跨阶段防御组合、嵌入空间攻击等关键开放问题，并提出具体未来研究方向。

Abstract: Background: Fine-tuning is central to adapting pre-trained Large Language Models (LLMs) to downstream tasks, but its reliance on training data, parameter updates, and reusable components opens entry points for attackers. Threats have evolved from data poisoning and weight tampering to agent manipulation and interface exploitation, yet existing reviews lack a unified framework spanning the full fine-tuning lifecycle. Objective: This paper presents a systematic survey of LLM fine-tuning security and establishes a lifecycle-based framework for comparing attacks and defenses, complemented by unified empirical evaluation. Methods: We divide attack and defense mechanisms into three phases by intervention timing: pre-tuning, during-tuning, and post-tuning. Within each phase, strategies are reviewed and contrasted to expose their evolution and limitations. Representative methods are then evaluated under a unified model, hardware, and protocol setup, with cross-phase experiments pairing attacks and defenses from different phases. Results: Attack effectiveness is highly model-dependent and non-monotonic with scale: weight-editing attacks effective on earlier models lose impact on modern open-source LLMs; cross-lingual backdoor transfer, reported as near-perfect at larger scales, fails entirely on tested 1B-4B models; and purely benign samples can compromise safety alignment in instruction-tuned models. Single-phase defenses rarely generalize across phases, and defense effectiveness depends jointly on model architecture and alignment state. Conclusion: We identify key open problems (configuration-robust defense, cross-phase defense composition, and embedding-space attacks beyond behavioral assumptions) and propose concrete future research directions.

</details>


### [150] [Pre-Characterization of Electromagnetic Side-Channel Leakage Using Publicly Available Information: A Case Study on E-Voting Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25142)
*Leonardo Teodoro,Kemuel L. Vieira,Saulo Queiroz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了巴西电子投票机（BVM）界面在电磁侧信道（TEMPEST）攻击下的脆弱性。通过使用公开信息模拟BVM的VGA显示，发现其高对比度、简洁界面和禁止电子设备等设计特征导致独特的电磁频谱特征，甚至可穿透墙壁被探测到。虽未使用真实硬件，但结果揭示了系统对TEMPEST攻击的潜在风险，并建议研究自适应干扰器等防护措施。这是首次针对国家正式采用的电子投票系统进行TEMPEST攻击研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究巴西电子投票机（BVM）界面是否在TEMPEST电磁侧信道攻击下存在信息泄露风险，特别是其设计特征是否会产生独特的可被截获的电磁频谱特征。

Method: 利用公开的选举机构信息（技术规格、运行规则、官方界面）模拟BVM的VGA显示器，使用软件无线电（SDR）截获电磁辐射信号，分析其频谱特征。

Result: BVM界面的高对比度、最少化屏幕信息、投票站内禁止电子设备等设计特征导致了简单且高度独特的频谱特征，该特征甚至可穿透墙壁被观测到。

Conclusion: 现有设计特征使BVM对TEMPEST攻击存在潜在脆弱性，需要进一步研究防护对策（如自适应干扰器）；这是首次针对国家正式采用的电子投票系统进行TEMPEST攻击研究。

Abstract: In this work, we study the interface of the Brazilian e-Voting Machine (BVM) in the context of electromagnetic side-channel threats commonly referred to as TEMPEST attacks. In a TEMPEST attack against video displays, an eavesdropper uses Software-Defined Radios (SDRs) to recover sensitive information by intercepting electromagnetic emanations generated during video signal transmission. We emulate the BVM using a VGA monitor by leveraging publicly available information disclosed by the electoral authority, including technical specifications, operational rules of the system, and the official BVM interface. Based on this setup, we investigate whether the BVM interface gives rise to a distinctive spectral signature observable through its unintended electromagnetic emissions.
