Our findings show that design characteristics relevant to a nationwide electoral process -- such as high image contrast, minimal on-screen information, and the prohibition of other electronic devices within the polling station -- result in a simple and highly distinctive spectral signature that can be observed even through a wall in our experiments. Although our experiments do not involve actual BVM hardware, the results raise concerns regarding the system's susceptibility to TEMPEST attacks and highlight the need for further research on protective countermeasures. In this context, our findings may support the design of automatic jammers capable of adaptively targeting compromising frequencies. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study investigating TEMPEST attacks in the context of an electronic voting system officially adopted by a country.

</details>


### [151] [KYA: A Framework-Agnostic Trust Layer for Autonomous Systems with Verifiable Provenance and Hierarchical Policy Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25376)
*Kolawole Quadri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KYA 是一个面向自主系统的开源信任与治理层，包含五项原语：四门入站应用流水线、仅收紧组合代数、跨主体类型的信任评分统一、可审计交互乘数放大、以及双轴委托归因。系统在 22 个智能体框架间框架无关，纯函数评分器 p99 延迟亚毫秒，约 1,800 ops/sec，四门流水线在 SQLite 上 1,200/1,200 拒绝伪造/过期/松弛/未批准的推荐，检测 89% 的对抗探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可观测性工具（如可观测性监控）只能告诉运维人员智能体何时变慢，但无法检测智能体何时出错、漂移、泄露或悄然失控。需要一套信任与治理层来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出五项原语：(1) 四门入站应用流水线（Ed25519签名验证+多锚点固定、过期校验、仅收紧组合、操作员默认批准）；(2) 三通道多租户层次上的仅收紧组合代数；(3) KYP（Know Your Principal）跨主体类型信任评分统一；(4) 基于 AIVSS 加性基线的可审计交互乘数放大；(5) 双轴委托归因（静态观测门控委托信任溢价+零配置运行时编排器责备）。框架无关设计，支持 22 个智能体框架。

Result: 纯函数评分器 p99 延迟亚毫秒，系统在 20 个并发工作线程下约 1,800 ops/sec，HMAC 链完整性端到端保持。四门流水线在 SQLite 上以亚毫秒 p99 延迟 100%（1,200/1,200）拒绝伪造、过期、松弛和未批准的推荐。检测 89% 的 1,200 个来自 PyRIT 和 Garak 的对抗探测，包括拓扑引导的多智能体攻击。

Conclusion: KYA 为自主系统提供了框架无关的信任与治理层，有效检测智能体异常行为，抵御对抗攻击，并在延迟和吞吐量上保持良好性能。项目以 Apache 2.0 协议开源。

Abstract: Observability tells operators when an agent is slow. KYA tells operators when an agent is wrong, drifting, leaking, or quietly going rogue. We present KYA (Know Your Agents), an open-source trust and governance layer for autonomous systems composed of five primitives: (1) a four-gate inbound apply pipeline composing Ed25519 signature verification with multi-anchor pinning, persist-time expiry, only-tighten composition, and operator-approval-as-default; (2) an only-tighten composition algebra over a three-channel multi-tenant hierarchy (platform default,tenant override, signed external recommendation); (3) KYP -- Know Your Principal, a schema-level unification of trust scoring across human users, AI agents, and service accounts; (4) auditable interaction-multiplier amplification over an AIVSS-shaped additive baseline, with bounded asymmetric per-interaction multipliers carrying stable audit codes; and (5) two-axis delegation attribution combining a static observation-gated delegation-trust premium with zero-config runtime orchestrator-blame at three SDK hook surfaces. KYA is framework-agnostic across 22 agent frameworks. The pure-function scorer runs sub-millisecond at p99 and the system sustains ~1,800 ops/sec at 20 concurrent workers with HMAC chain integrity preserved end-to-end. The four-gate inbound apply pipeline rejects forged, expired, loosening, and unapproved recommendations on every trial (1,200 / 1,200) with sub-millisecond p99 latency on SQLite. KYA detects 89% of 1,200 adversarial probes from PyRIT and Garak, including the recently-published topology-guided multi-agent attack. The system is available under Apache 2.0 as the veldt-kya package on PyPI (release candidate at submission time; stable v0.1.0 forthcoming)

</details>


### [152] [Evo-Attacker: Memory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Tool Attacks on LLM-MAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25389)
*Bingyu Yan,Xiaoming Zhang,Jinyu Hou,Chaozhuo Li,Ziyi Zhou,Yiming Hei,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Evo-Attacker，一种基于自演化、记忆增强强化学习的工具攻击方法，通过动态攻击记忆和Attack-Flow GRPO优化，实现对LLM-MAS的有效攻击，揭示了工具使用场景的防御需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-MAS对工具输出存在隐式信任，形成严重攻击面，而现有工具攻击方法受限于领域特异性或固定静态模板，缺乏泛化性和演化能力。

Method: 将工具攻击建模为自演化、记忆增强的强化学习过程：构建动态攻击记忆库，通过深思熟虑推理检索对抗模式并在关键时机进行干预；提出Attack-Flow GRPO，通过终端结果优化中间推理步骤，解决长时域信用分配问题。

Result: 综合实验表明Evo-Attacker持续优于基线方法，展现出强大的泛化能力和演化能力。

Conclusion: Evo-Attacker的有效性凸显了为LLM-MAS设计防御性工具安全机制的必要性。

Abstract: While Large Language Model-based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) demonstrate remarkable capabilities in solving complex tasks by orchestrating specialized agents and external tools, the implicit trust in tool outputs creates a critical attack surface. Existing tool attacks are limited by domain specificity or fixed and static templates. To address these challenges, we propose Evo-Attacker, which formulates the tool attack as a self-evolving, memory-augmented reinforcement learning process. Evo-Attacker constructs a dynamic attack memory and employs deliberative reasoning to retrieve adversarial patterns and strategize modifying interventions at critical moments. Furthermore, we introduce Attack-Flow GRPO to optimize intermediate reasoning steps via terminal outcomes, addressing the long-horizon credit assignment challenge. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that Evo-Attacker consistently outperforms baselines, highlighting its generalization and evolutionary capabilities and the urgent need for defensive tool safeguards.

</details>


### [153] [Heimdall: Formally Verified Automated Migration of Legacy eBPF Programs to Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25411)
*Vishnu Asutosh Dasu,Monika Santra,Md Rafi Ur Rashid,Ashish Kumar,Saeid Tizpaz-Niari,Gang Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现eBPF程序中存在六类经内核验证器仍无法捕获的源级漏洞，包括信息泄露（10个开源程序存在可完全解码的先前事件泄露），并提出Heimdall——一个利用大语言模型将遗留libbpf C程序翻译为Aya Rust的自动化管道，通过迭代修复、静态分析安全引擎和符号执行等价性检查，在102个程序中以94.1%的成功率生成形式化等价翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核eBPF验证器仅检查低级内存安全和终止性，无法强制执行初始化纪律、模式一致性、错误处理等高级源级属性，导致存在可通过验证但实际存在数据损坏、信息泄露和错误执行的源级bug。

Method: Heimdall管道：1) 使用LLM将libbpf C程序翻译为Aya Rust；2) 迭代修复编译和内核验证器失败；3) 通过静态分析安全引擎拒绝Rust-Aya中的不安全逃逸机制；4) 通过符号执行和Z3约束求解进行等价性证明。

Result: 在102个eBPF程序中，Heimdall成功生成96个形式化等价翻译（94.1%），并首次识别出10个开源eBPF程序中通过验证但仍存在信息泄露的漏洞。

Conclusion: Heimdall是首个自动化实现生产级eBPF程序内存安全语言迁移的系统，并提供每个程序级别的形式化行为等价保证。

Abstract: Extended Berkeley Packet Filter (eBPF) programs are kernel extensions used for networking, observability, and security enforcement in the Linux kernel. The in-kernel eBPF verifier checks low-level memory safety and termination on eBPF programs, but it does not enforce many higher-level source-level properties, such as initialization discipline, schema consistency, or error handling. We document six classes of source-level bugs that compile, pass the kernel verifier, and can silently corrupt data, leak previously traced events to userspace, or yield incorrect enforcement outcomes. Among these, we identify previously unreported information leaks in ten open-source eBPF programs whose ring-buffer or stack-resident event records carry fully decodable prior traced events, including user-identifying paths and recurring kernel-text return addresses sufficient to recover the KASLR slide on every event, into userspace. To harden such verifier-accepted buggy programs and support safe migration, we present Heimdall, an automated pipeline that uses large language models to translate legacy libbpf C programs to Aya Rust. Heimdall iteratively repairs compilation and kernel-verifier failures, rejects unsafe escape hatches in Rust-Aya with a static analysis safety engine, and proves per-program equivalence to the original via symbolic execution and Z3-based equivalence checking. Across 102 eBPF programs, Heimdall produces 96 formally proven-equivalent translations (94.1%). Heimdall is the first system to automate memory-safe-language migration of production eBPF programs with per-program formal guarantees that the migration preserves observable behavior.

</details>


### [154] [Referential Security as a New Paradigm for AI Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25673)
*Dan Ristea,Vasilios Mavroudis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"指涉安全"（referential security）作为AI评估的新范式，解决持续更新的AI系统导致安全评估与具体系统脱节的问题，通过将模型身份视为可经验验证的属性，确保评估结果在动态系统的全生命周期中保持可重复性、纵向审计有效性和跨提供者等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统持续更新（权重、提示、检索机制、滥用分类器、推理设置、服务基础设施等），但公开模型名称保持不变，导致安全评估针对的是表面标签而非可识别和可区分的具体系统。这使得评估结果不可靠，无法锚定到特定工件。

Method: 提出"指涉安全"范式，将根本安全问题从"模型是否安全"扩展为"后续各方能否确定某个安全声明针对的是哪个系统"，将模型身份重构为可经验验证的属性，将指涉稳定性与实质安全声明分离。

Result: 该框架使三个在当前实践中难以处理的关键工作流变得可处理：可重复评估、纵向审计有效性和跨提供者等价性。

Conclusion: 通过将评估锚定于可验证的工件，该方法确保安全审计和监管发现在动态系统的全生命周期中保持其经验效用。

Abstract: Security evaluations inherently depend on stable identifiers. Any finding, audit, or regulatory decision must remain attached to the specific artifact it pertains to. Continuously updated artificial intelligence systems violate this core assumption, with public model designations remaining static while underlying weights, prompts, retrieval mechanisms, misuse classifiers, inference settings, and serving infrastructures undergo unannounced modifications. Consequently, current evaluations frequently apply to superficial labels rather than identifiable and distinct systems.
  To resolve this, we propose referential security as a new paradigm for AI evaluation. The fundamental security question extends beyond whether a model is safe to whether subsequent parties can conclusively determine which system a specific safety claim addressed. This approach reframes model identity as an empirically verifiable property and separates referential stability from the substantive security claims it conditions. This framework brings tractability to three critical workflows that current practices handle poorly. Specifically, it enables reproducible evaluation, longitudinal audit validity, and cross-provider equivalence. By grounding these evaluations in verifiable artifacts, our approach ensures that safety audits and regulatory findings maintain their empirical utility across the operational lifecycle of dynamic systems.

</details>


### [155] [An Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Architecture for Cross-Institutional Collaborative RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25716)
*Chenxin Mao,Shangyu Liu,Zhenzhe Zheng,Fan Wu,Jie Wu,Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedRAG提出了一种基于乱序分布式注意力机制的高吞吐、隐私保护的联邦RAG框架，通过数值稳定的特征乱序和令牌置换，在不暴露明文的情况下解耦注意力执行与数据定位，无需专用硬件或模型重训练，实现了与现有安全基线相比最高62倍的延迟降低且模型效用损失小于0.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格的隐私法规导致跨机构领域知识库集成时出现严重的"数据孤岛"问题，而Transformer的自注意力机制要求跨节点访问分布式键值缓存，与联邦RAG系统的分布式推理需求存在根本性冲突。

Method: 提出Scrambled Distributed Attention协议，利用数值稳定的特征乱序和令牌置换，将乱序计算动态委派给协作节点，在不解密数据的情况下实现注意力执行与数据定位的解耦。

Result: 模型效用损失小于0.1%，与现有安全基线相比最高实现62倍延迟降低，维持了实际可用的人类阅读吞吐量。

Conclusion: FedRAG在无需专用硬件或模型重训练的条件下，有效防御中间状态反演攻击，为跨机构知识协同提供了一种高效、隐私安全的联邦RAG解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) empowers LLMs with external knowledge, making cross-institutional domain-specific knowledge base integration a highly promising deployment paradigm. Despite this potential, strict privacy regulations create severe "data silos" that obstruct such collaboration. Building federated RAG systems requires distributed inference, but the Transformer's self-attention mechanism fundamentally conflicts with this by mandating cross-node access to distributed Key-Value caches. To address this challenge, we present FedRAG, a high-throughput, privacy-preserving federated RAG framework. At its core is a novel Scrambled Distributed Attention protocol that utilizes numerically stable feature scrambling and token permutation. By dynamically delegating scrambled computations to collaborating nodes, our system successfully decouples attention execution from data localization without exposing plaintext. Crucially, our approach requires no specialized hardware or model retraining, circumventing the prohibitive latency and communication overheads of cryptographic solutions while robustly defending against intermediate state inversion attacks. Extensive evaluations demonstrate our framework preserves negligible (<0.1\%) model utility degradation and achieves up to a 62$\times$ latency reduction over existing secure baselines, sustaining practical, human-reading throughput for cross-institutional knowledge synergy.

</details>


### [156] [Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Distribution Statistics Analytics on Mobile Spatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25791)
*Xuhao Ren,Mingyang Zhao,Ruichen Zhang,Liehuang Zhu,Dusit Niyato,Bin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了首个支持移动空间数据高效且隐私保护的分布式统计分析系统，提出了基于八叉树划分的eSpat-B方案和基于KD树与增量DPF的eSpat+方案，在保护数据隐私的同时实现计算开销降低约1.2倍、通信开销降低约20倍，并保持100%准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动环境中大量空间数据的分布式统计分析对实时移动计算至关重要，但移动环境的约束性和动态性加剧了隐私挑战：集中敏感数据存在严重隐私泄露风险，而现有隐私保护技术通常引入过高开销或不准确性。

Method: 1) eSpat-B：利用两个非共谋服务器和基于八叉树划分的改进分布式点函数(DPF)；2) eSpat+：利用K-Dimensional树进行空间划分，结合增量DPF进行统计分析，并设计高效更新算法。

Result: 在真实移动轨迹数据集上，方案实现计算开销降低约1.2倍，通信开销降低约20倍，并保持100%准确性。

Conclusion: 安全分析表明所提方案在整个统计过程中有效保护数据隐私，理论分析与实验结果验证了方案的有效性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of mobile computing technology, massive amounts of spatial data are continuously generated from various mobile terminals and sensing devices, such as smartphones, connected vehicles, and drones. Performing efficient distributed statistical analysis on this data is crucial for real-time mobile computing applications. However, the constrained and dynamic nature of mobile environments exacerbates the privacy challenge: centralizing sensitive data for analysis risks severe privacy leaks, while existing privacy-preserving techniques often introduce excessive overhead or inaccuracies In this paper, we design, implement, and evaluate the first system that supports efficient and privacy-preserving distribution statistics analysis for mobile spatial data. First, we propose eSpat-B, which leverages two non-colluding servers and a newly designed improved distributed point functions (DPF) with octree partitioning. Furthermore, considering the frequent updates of spatial data, we propose another more efficient scheme, eSpat+. The core idea of this scheme is to utilize a K-Dimensional tree for spatial partitioning, combine it with incremental DPF for performing statistics analysis, and design an efficient update algorithm. Security analysis demonstrates that our schemes effectively protect data privacy throughout the statistical process. Theoretical analysis and experimental results on real-world mobile trajectory datasets demonstrate that our proposed schemes achieve a reduction of approximately 1.2* in computation overhead, 20* in communication overhead, and maintain 100% accuracy.

</details>


### [157] [TTPrint: Evidence-Grounded TTP Extraction via Diverge-then-Converge Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25836)
*Yutong Cheng,Changze Li,Raihan Sultan Pasha Basuki,Qian Cui,Wei Ding,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TTPrint提出了一种"先发散后收敛"的MITRE ATT&CK技术提取方法：先广泛提取候选技术，再通过确定性证据定位和权威定义验证进行严格筛选。在TRAM-Clean和TTPrint-Bench基准上分别达到76.48%和87.39%的宏F1分数，领先基线方法63.5%和29.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（基于规则、监督学习、LLM）在从CTI报告中提取ATT&CK技术时难以同时兼顾高召回率（不遗漏技术）和高精度（不产生幻觉），原因在于：规则和监督方法缺乏泛化性，而LLM方法将候选生成与验证耦合在单次推理中导致召回率和精度同时受限。

Method: 采用"先发散后收敛"设计：发散阶段将报告分解为原子行为并广泛提出候选技术；确定性跨度定位阶段将每个候选技术锚定到源文本中的特定证据窗口；收敛验证阶段仅保留同时被局部证据和MITRE权威定义支持的候选技术。此外，贡献了两个评估基准（TRAM-Clean和TTPrint-Bench）用于消除现有基准中的标注噪声。

Result: 在TRAM-Clean和TTPrint-Bench上，TTPrint分别达到76.48%和87.39%的宏F1分数，比领先基线方法分别高出63.5%和29.4%。跨六种LLM的多骨干分析和阈值敏感性研究验证了方法在不同模型选择下的泛化能力，并为参数选择提供了实践指导。

Conclusion: TTPrint通过解耦技术提取与验证过程，有效解决了CTI报告中ATT&CK技术提取任务中召回率与精度的权衡问题，在多个基准上取得了显著优于现有方法的性能，且具有良好的模型泛化性。

Abstract: Extracting MITRE ATT&CK techniques from cyber threat intelligence (CTI) reports is an open-set, multi-label problem requiring both high recall (not missing techniques) and high precision (not hallucinating unsupported ones). Existing methods--rule-based, supervised, and LLM-based--struggle to achieve both: rule-based and supervised approaches lack generalizability across diverse attack descriptions, while LLM-based approaches that couple candidate generation and validation within a single inference step suffer from limited recall and precision simultaneously. We propose TTPrint, which addresses this challenge through a diverge-then-converge design inspired by how human analysts work: first extracting broadly, then verifying rigorously. In the divergent phase, reports are decomposed into atomic behaviors and candidate techniques are proposed broadly. A deterministic span localization stage then anchors each candidate to a specific evidence window in the source text. A convergent verification stage retains only candidates supported by both the localized evidence and the authoritative MITRE definition. We contribute two evaluation resources--a cleaned TRAM benchmark (TRAM-Clean) and a new annotated dataset (TTPrint-Bench)--to address known annotation noise in existing benchmarks and elevate the task to document-level TTP extraction. On TRAM-Clean and TTPrint-Bench, TTPrint achieves 76.48% and 87.39% macro-F1 respectively, outperforming the leading baseline by 63.5% and 29.4%. A multi-backbone analysis across six LLMs and a threshold sensitivity study further demonstrate generalizability across model choices and provide practical guidance for parameter selection.

</details>


### [158] [Proof of Useful Attestation: A Consensus Primitive for Attestation-Native Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25844)
*Stefan Stefanović*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PoUA通过将验证者权重与声誉挂钩，使证明处理成为共识权重的一等公民，并证明了对声誉操纵的成本下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用PoS链中，验证者无论是否正确处理证明工作都获得相同费用，这对主要经济活动为代币转移的链无影响，但对于主要经济为记录证明（如内容溯源、AI输出归因、门限签名凭证、供应链收据）的链而言，这种无差异化成为问题。

Method: 验证者投票权重 = 质押代币 × 声誉标量，声誉在[r_min, r_max]范围内累积，更新方式为加性、费用加权、不可转移、每epoch封顶。引入链级自适应燃烧分数tau_burn，证明声誉膨胀的非可恢复成本下限为 tau_burn * Delta_r / (eta * alpha_eff)。推荐v0校准下r_max/r_min在[4,10]区间，对资本充裕攻击者的成本溢价为纯质押PoS的4到10倍。

Result: 给出了声誉膨胀成本的下界形式化证明（Lemma 1），设计了六层女巫与磨削攻击防御机制，并通过蒙特卡洛策略搜索进行了经验验证，推导了磨削检测器的显式阈值。

Conclusion: PoUA是一个机制设计提案，具备形式化的经济下限和继承的BFT安全性与活性，但并非完整的密码学安全证明。

Abstract: Validators on generic Proof of Stake chains earn the same fees whether they handle attestation work correctly or selectively censor it. For chains whose main activity is moving tokens around, that indifference is fine. For chains whose primary economic activity is recording attestations (content provenance, AI-output attribution, threshold-signed credentials, supply-chain receipts), the indifference becomes a problem.
  Proof of Useful Attestation (PoUA) makes attestation handling first-class in the consensus weighting itself. Validator vote weight is the product of bonded stake and a reputation scalar in [r_min, r_max] that accumulates from valid attestation work. The reputation update is additive, fee-weighted, non-transferable, and capped per epoch. We prove a cost-to-grind floor (Lemma 1): under chain-wide adaptive burn fraction tau_burn, the non-recoverable cost an adversary pays to inflate reputation by Delta_r is bounded below by tau_burn * Delta_r / (eta * alpha_eff). Under the recommended v0 calibration (r_max/r_min in [4, 10]), the cost premium against a capital adversary is 4x to 10x over equivalent pure-stake PoS at steady state.
  The paper specifies the mechanism, six layered Sybil and grinding defenses, empirical Monte Carlo strategy-search across the full layered defense, and grinding detectors with explicit threshold derivations. It is a mechanism-design proposal with a formal economic floor and inherited BFT safety and liveness, not a complete cryptographic security proof. This release incorporates feedback from Jiangshan Yu (University of Sydney) and Marko Vukolić (Bitcoin Scaling Labs).

</details>


### [159] [Broken Object Level Authorization in the Wild: An Empirical Taxonomy from 100+ Bug Bounty Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25865)
*Bandana Kaur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对200份HackerOne漏洞报告（2021-2026）进行了大规模实证分析，发现78.5%被确认为BOLA漏洞，其中基于操作的对象级BOLA（41.7%）和直接对象引用BOLA是两大主要类型，同时揭示了HackerOne标签计数严重高估BOLA信号（21.5%为误报），并发现11.9%的垂直权限提升漏洞及GraphQL全局ID被系统性利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BOLA（对象级授权失效）虽被列为最关键的API安全漏洞，但现有研究几乎全是概念性的，缺乏大规模实证分析。作者旨在通过公开的漏洞奖励报告进行系统性的实证研究，填补这一空白。

Method: 构建200份HackerOne漏洞报告的可复现抽样框架（2021-2026，标记为IDOR或不当访问控制），经三标准筛选后得到107份可分类报告。采用LLM辅助的架构补全流程，结合人工裁决，依据六类BOLA分类法进行分类。

Result: 107份报告中84份（78.5%）确认为BOLA；Action-Level Object BOLA占41.7%，与直接对象引用BOLA并列为两大主要类型；约21.5%严格标准下不在范围内，说明标签计数严重高估；11.9%为垂直权限提升；GraphQL全局ID被系统性利用。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Action-Level Object BOLA在实践中的高发性（但被历史指南低估），指出标签计数方法的误导性，并发现GraphQL全局ID的系统性利用风险。结果对API安全测试协议、开发者教育和OWASP指南具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Broken Object Level Authorization (BOLA) is consistently ranked the most critical API security vulnerability, yet the existing literature remains almost entirely conceptual. This paper presents one of the first large-scale empirical analyses of BOLA in publicly disclosed bug bounty reports. We constructed a reproducible sampling frame of 200 HackerOne disclosures tagged IDOR or Improper Access Control (2021-2026) and applied a three-criterion inclusion filter, yielding 107 fully classified reports. Classification used an LLM-assisted schema-completion procedure under constrained, human-adjudicated criteria against a six-family BOLA taxonomy. Of 107 classified reports, 84 (78.5%) were confirmed in-scope BOLA. Action-Level Object BOLA, defined by unauthorized state-changing actions on another user's objects, accounts for 41.7% of confirmed cases and emerges alongside Direct Object Reference BOLA as one of the two dominant families observed in the dataset. This shows a pattern historically underrepresented in practitioner guidance. Approximately 21.5% of classified reports are out-of-scope under strict criteria, indicating that tag-counting on platforms like HackerOne significantly overstates the BOLA-specific signal. We report distributions across family, action type, authorization direction, industry sector, identifier format, and exploit mechanism. Key secondary findings include an 11.9% rate of vertical (user-to-admin) privilege failures and systematic exploitation of GraphQL Global IDs across major platforms. Findings have direct implications for API security testing protocols, developer education, and OWASP guidance.

</details>


### [160] [Capability and Robustness Cannot Both Be Free: An Information-Theoretic Bound for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25889)
*Jianwei Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度证明了视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略在离散动作空间中，能力（策略动作与最优动作的互信息）与鲁棒性（对抗扰动下保留的互信息）之和存在一个与策略无关的上界，其由任务熵和对抗信道容量决定。在OpenVLA-7B上，该上界约为31 nats，而现有策略已消耗约24%，留下有限的鲁棒性提升空间。通过252个高斯VLA单元和48个OpenVLA-7B实验验证了该界限的严格性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在对抗扰动下表现脆弱，经验性防御方法在鲁棒性和干净准确率之间存在权衡，但缺乏理论解释该权衡是否存在根本性下限，本文旨在证明该下限存在并给出定量界限。

Method: 利用数据处理不等式(Data Processing Inequality)的两次应用结合互信息非负性，推导出能力与鲁棒性之和的上界。首先建立像素级别的宽松上界（任务熵+对抗信道容量），再通过编码器特定推论将信道限制到策略相关子空间，收紧上界。通过高斯VLA模型和OpenVLA-7B实验验证界限。

Result: 证明了VLA离散动作策略下，能力与鲁棒性之和存在与策略无关的理论上界。在OpenVLA-7B上，像素级界限约5000 nats（宽松），编码器特定界限约31 nats（紧致），现有策略已消耗约24%的紧致预算。252个高斯VLA单元和48个OpenVLA-7B实验零违反该界限。

Conclusion: VLA模型的鲁棒性提升存在理论天花板，编码器特定松弛量可作为防御论文的标准比较轴。开源了所有代码、清单和结果。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly deployed on real robots, where each predicted action is executed and each failure carries a safety cost. They reach high success rates on clean inputs but collapse under small adversarial perturbations. A $16/255$ PGD attack on OpenVLA-7B drops LIBERO success from above $95\%$ to under $5\%$. Empirical defenses recover some robustness at a cost in clean accuracy, but the literature does not say whether the trade-off has a theoretical floor. We prove that it does. For any VLA policy with discrete actions, the sum of capability (mutual information between policy action and oracle action) and robustness (mutual information preserved under adversarial perturbation, net of trivial channel leakage) is upper-bounded by a policy-independent budget: task entropy plus adversarial channel capacity. The proof is two applications of the Data Processing Inequality plus MI non-negativity. The pixel-level bound is loose on current models ($\sim 10^3$ nats), but an encoder-specific corollary restricts the channel to the policy-relevant subspace, reducing the budget from $\sim 5{,}000$ to $\sim 31$ nats on OpenVLA; the policy already consumes $\sim 24\%$ of this tighter budget, leaving limited room for simultaneous robustness improvement. We validate the bound across $252$ closed-form Gaussian-VLA cells and $48$ OpenVLA-7B $\times$ LIBERO $\times$ PGD cells (zero violations). We propose encoder-specific slack as a normalized comparison axis for defense papers, and release all code, manifests, and results.

</details>


### [161] [Semantic Validation of Packer Identification Tools: Characterization, Repair, and Downstream Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25923)
*Fangtian Zhong,Zhuoyun Qian,Mengfei Ren,Yili Jiang,Jiaqi Huang,Yunming Pang,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解包器作为可执行语义合约的框架，用于验证和修复加壳识别工具中的语义错误。通过对11个开源工具和6个VirusTotal工具的大规模研究发现，语义错误普遍存在，主要源于不完整的签名和不稳定的启发式逻辑。修复后，加壳识别覆盖率提升高达58.6%，下游恶意软件分类性能平均提升13.6%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加壳识别工具是恶意软件分析的关键基础，但其语义正确性很少被验证。工具可能返回看似合理但语义错误的加壳标签，导致解包失败和下游分析不可靠。现有方法缺乏自动化的语义验证手段。

Method: 提出一种语义验证框架，核心思想是将解包器用作可执行语义合约：若工具预测了某个加壳家族，对应的解包器应能恢复出可分析的程序内容。基于此构建了检测、定位和修复语义错误的系统化流水线。

Result: 对11个开源工具和6个VirusTotal工具进行了首次大规模语义错误实证研究。发现语义错误普遍存在且具有重复性模式，主要源于不完整的签名和不稳定的启发式逻辑。修复后加壳识别覆盖率提升高达58.6%，下游恶意软件分类性能平均提升13.6%以上。

Conclusion: 语义验证对于构建可信的恶意软件分析流水线至关重要，本文框架能够有效检测和修复加壳识别工具中的语义错误。

Abstract: Packer identification tools are a critical foundation of malware analysis, directly affecting unpacking, behavioral analysis, malware classification, and threat attribution. However, their semantic correctness is rarely validated. In practice, a tool may return a plausible packer label that is nevertheless semantically wrong, leading to failed unpacking and unreliable downstream analysis. This paper presents a semantic validation framework for testing and repairing packer identification tools. Our key idea is to use unpackers as executable semantic contracts. If a tool predicts a packer family, the corresponding unpacker should recover analyzable program content. This enables automatic test oracles without requiring manually labeled ground truth. Building on this idea, we develop a systematic pipeline for detecting, localizing, and repairing semantic faults in existing packer identification tools. We then conduct the first large-scale empirical study of semantic bugs in eleven open-source packer identification tools and six proprietary VirusTotal tools. Our results reveal that semantic bugs are widespread and recurring, largely due to incomplete signatures and unstable heuristic logic. After repair, packer identification coverage improves by up to 58.6%, and downstream malware classification performance improves by more than 13.6% on average. These findings show that semantic validation of packer identification tools is essential for building trustworthy malware analysis pipelines.

</details>


### [162] [Building an Adversarial Malware Dataset by Family and Type: Generation, Evasion, and Poisoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25937)
*David Košťál,Martin Jureček*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含44,347个家族标签和33,596个类型标签的对抗性恶意软件样本数据集，对EMBER分类器的逃逸率分别达到98.35%和92.20%；仅注入0.5%的完全错误标签对抗样本即可将重新训练的分类器逃逸率从26.1%提升至92.8%，展示了恶意软件分类管道对数据投毒攻击的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性恶意软件数据集缺乏规模化和标准化，且对数据投毒攻击在恶意软件检测场景下的影响研究不足，亟需公开数据集以推动相关研究。

Method: 基于RawMal-TF真实恶意软件数据集，使用对抗性恶意软件生成器套件构建两类对抗性PE文件（家族标签和类型标签），通过EMBER评分和VirusTotal分类进行标注，并设计数据投毒实验（注入0.5%错误标签样本）评估重新训练后的分类器逃逸率变化。

Result: 家族标签和类型标签数据集对EMBER分类器的逃逸率分别为98.35%和92.20%；仅0.5%的投毒数据使重新训练的分类器逃逸率从26.1%飙升至92.8%。

Conclusion: 公开数据集可促进对抗性恶意软件、投毒攻击及基于机器学习的恶意软件检测系统鲁棒性的未来研究，强调当前检测系统在面对定向投毒攻击时的严重脆弱性。

Abstract: We present a dataset of adversarial malware samples derived from the public RawMal-TF collection of real-world malware binaries. Using a suite of adversarial malware generators, we construct two sets of adversarial PE files: 44,347 family-labelled samples and 33,596 type-labelled samples, achieving evasion rates of 98.35 % and 92.20 % against the EMBER classifier, respectively. Each adversarial binary is accompanied by detailed metadata, including EMBER scores and VirusTotal classifications. We further demonstrate the susceptibility of malware classification pipelines to data poisoning attacks through a series of training experiments. Injecting fully mislabelled adversarial samples representing only 0.5 % of the training data in the family-labelled dataset increases the evasion rate against the re-trained classifier from 26.1 % to 92.8 %. The dataset is publicly released to facilitate future research on adversarial malware, poisoning attacks, and the robustness of machine-learning-based malware detection systems.

</details>


### [163] [Shielded but Lightweight: Building Practical Confidential Containers with ARM CCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26018)
*Liantao Song,Yiming Zhang,Fengwei Zhang,Yan Ding,Bin Zhou,Jie Yu,Yusong Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Fasco是一个基于ARM CCA架构的轻量级机密容器运行时，通过硬件强制隔离和系统域设计，在降低启动延迟和性能开销的同时保持较小的可信计算基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于microVM的机密容器架构软件栈复杂，导致高启动延迟和大资源开销，不适合短生命周期容器工作负载。

Method: 基于ARM CCA设计Fasco，将每个容器直接实例化为独立的Container Realm，利用硬件强制隔离保证机密性和完整性；引入System Realm提供系统服务和资源管理；通过异常转发和共享缓冲区实现不同容器域间的隔离。

Result: 在ARMv8硬件上的原型评估显示，Fasco相比现有机密容器架构降低了启动延迟和性能开销，同时保持了较小的TCB。

Conclusion: Fasco通过硬件级隔离和精简架构设计，有效解决了微VM架构在短生命周期容器场景下的性能瓶颈，为多租户环境提供了更高效的机密容器方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of cloud-native technologies has created an urgent need for security. Currently, confidential containers are increasingly deployed in multi-tenant environments. Existing confidential container designs mainly adopt a microVM-based architecture. Although this approach improves inter-container isolation, its complex software stack leads to high startup latency and significant resource overhead, making it unsuitable for short-lived container workloads. In this paper, we propose Fasco, a lightweight confidential container runtime based on the ARM Confidential Compute Architecture (CCA). Fasco directly instantiates each container as an independent Container Realm, leveraging CCA's hardware-enforced isolation to ensure the confidentiality and integrity of application data inside the container. In addition, Fasco introduces a dedicated System Realm to provide system services and resource management for container realms. Through exception forwarding and shared buffers, Fasco ensures isolation among different container realms. We have implemented a prototype of Fasco and evaluated its performance on ARMv8 hardware. Experimental results show that Fasco reduces the startup latency and performance overhead of existing confidential container architectures while maintaining a small TCB.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [In Search of the Ingredients of Open-Endedness: Replicating Picbreeder with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23908)
*Sam Earle,Kay Arulkumaran,Andrew Dai,Akarsh Kumar,Julian Togelius,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了用前沿视觉语言模型（VLM）替代人类用户进行开放端搜索（Picbreeder平台），发现VLM生成结果与人类基线存在明显差异，并通过引入探索噪声、行为多样性和记忆机制分析了造成差异的因果因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人工智能体是否具备人类式的开放端、无指导发现能力，即能否持续生成新颖且有意义的成果。

Method: 复现Picbreeder实验，用前沿视觉语言模型（VLM）替代人类用户，并通过添加探索噪声、行为多样性（agent间）和叙事动量（记忆）来研究影响输出差异的因果因素。

Result: VLM系统与人类基线在输出上存在明显的定性差异，并通过系统发育复杂度、视觉和语义显著性及新颖性等指标进行了量化表征。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在开放端无指导发现能力上与人类存在差距，探究性噪声、行为多样性和记忆等机制对输出特征有一定影响。

Abstract: We are in the midst of large-scale industrial and academic efforts to automate the processes of scientific, technological and creative production through AI-driven assistants. Historically, a fundamental property of these processes in their human form has been their open-endedness: their capacity for generating a seemingly endless supply of novel and meaningful new forms. Do artificial agents have any capacity for such fruitful unguided discovery? To answer this question, we turn to Picbreeder, the canonical exemplar of human-driven open-ended search, in which users collaboratively generated a diverse library of images through interactive evolution of small neural networks. We replicate Picbreeder, replacing human users with frontier Vision Language Models (VLMs). We observe clear qualitative differences between the output of our system and the historical human baseline, and attempt to characterize them using metrics of phylogenetic complexity and visual and semantic salience and novelty. In an effort to identify some of the causal factors contributing these differences, we study the addition of exploratory noise to the agents' selection process, of behavioral diversity between agents, and of narrative momentum in the form of memory of past actions. We make our code available at https://github.com/smearle/picbreeder-vlm.

</details>


### [165] [Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23909)
*Noam Michael,Daniel BenShushan,Jacob Bien,Don A. Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLMs在多样化任务中的置信度校准问题，发现LLMs与人类类似，整体上过度自信（置信度高于准确率），但这一倾向受难易效应调节：困难任务上过度自信更严重，简单任务上则表现出显著的不自信。研究还开发了LifeEval测试来评估不同难度下的模型校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在不同任务上的置信度校准情况，验证其是否像人类一样存在过度自信的倾向，并考察任务难度对校准效果的影响。

Method: 通过预先注册的研究，系统性地评估LLMs在多样化任务上的置信度与准确率之间的差异，并开发LifeEval测试基准来评估不同难度水平下的模型校准效果。

Result: 当前LLMs整体上置信度高于准确率，表现出过度自信；困难任务上过度自信最严重，简单任务上则表现出显著的不自信（underconfidence）。

Conclusion: LLMs的置信度校准存在与人类类似的难易效应（hard-easy effect），需要针对不同难度水平改进校准方法。

Abstract: We investigate the calibration of large language models' (LLMs') confidence across diverse tasks. The results of our preregistered study show that the current crop of LLMs are, like people, too sure they are right: confidence exceeds accuracy, on average. Importantly, however, this tendency is moderated by a powerful hard-easy effect, wherein overconfidence is greatest on difficult tests; by contrast, easy tests actually show substantial underconfidence. We develop LifeEval, a test for evaluating model calibration across levels of difficulty.

</details>


### [166] [How Much Thinking is Enough? Quantifying and Understanding Redundancy in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23926)
*Zhiyuan Zhai,Xinkai You,Wenjing Yan,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了推理模型的冗余度，发现前沿推理模型在数学推理任务中步骤级冗余度高达61%-93%，并从理论上证明这种冗余是长度无关结果奖励的结构性后果，而非模型特定缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理模型虽然能解决复杂问题，但付出了巨大的延迟、GPU时间和能源代价。然而，这些推理过程中的大量重述、验证和循环自省到底有多少是真正必要的，从未在大规模上进行过测量，也没有从第一性原理得到解释。本文旨在填补这两个空白。

Method: 形式化定义推理冗余度：在正确推理轨迹中，能够截断的末尾分段步骤的最大比例，使得模型被迫终止思考并输出答案后仍能得到正确答案。在四个前沿推理模型和两个数学基准上大规模量化步骤级冗余度。从理论上证明，在长度无关的结果奖励下，不存在有限最优期望停止时间，冗余是结构性的。

Result: 在8个(模型, 基准)条件下，步骤级冗余度高达61%-93%；其中六个条件下中位关键前缀仅为一个分段步骤；冗余度随问题难度增加而下降，但在MATH-500的Level-5最难问题上，冗余度仍达46%-85%。理论证明该冗余是长度无关结果奖励的结构性后果。

Conclusion: 过度思考不是个别模型需要修复的bug，而是当前推理模型训练方式的结构性属性。这一发现对推理模型的效率优化具有重要启示。

Abstract: Reasoning-capable large language models solve hard problems by emitting long chains of thought, paying heavily in latency, GPU time, and energy. Casual inspection of their traces reveals extensive reformulation, verification, and circular self-reflection, yet how much of this deliberation is actually necessary has never been measured at scale or explained from first principles. This paper closes both gaps.
  We formalise reasoning redundancy directly in terms of the reasoning model itself: the redundancy of a correct trace is the largest fraction of its trailing segmented steps that can be truncated while $π$, forced to terminate thinking and emit a final answer, still produces the correct answer. A large-scale quantification across four frontier reasoning models and two mathematical benchmarks shows that step-level redundancy is consistently high -- between 61% and 93% across the 8 (model, benchmark) conditions we study, with the median critical prefix equal to a single segmented step in six of the eight conditions -- that the finding is robust to the choice of judge family, and that although $ρ$ decreases with problem difficulty on MATH-500, all four models remain substantially redundant ($ρ\in [46\%, 85\%]$) even on the hardest Level-5 problems.
  We then prove that this redundancy is a structural consequence of length-agnostic outcome rewards, not a model-specific artefact: under any such reward, no finite expected stopping time is optimal. The result holds regardless of RL algorithm, base model, data distribution, or whether the policy is obtained via RL or distillation; over-thinking is therefore not a bug to be patched in individual models but a structural property of how current reasoning models are trained. Code: https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/how-much-thinking-is-enough

</details>


### [167] [Toward Reliable Design of LLM-Enabled Agentic Workflows: Optimizing Latency-Reliability-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23929)
*Ya-Ting Yang,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了LLM启用的多智能体工作流中延迟、可靠性和成本之间的基本权衡，提出了LLM和非LLM智能体的性能模型，并给出了顺序工作流在延迟和成本约束下的最优设计策略，包括注水式令牌分配策略和影子价格形式的可靠性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统越来越多地采用由多个交互智能体组成的工作流，其中部分由大语言模型驱动，部分由传统计算模块驱动。目前缺乏对这些工作流中延迟、可靠性和成本之间基本权衡的系统性分析。

Method: 提出了LLM和非LLM智能体的性能模型，使用参数化指数可靠性函数刻画LLM智能体中推理令牌和输出令牌对输出质量的影响；研究了延迟和成本约束下的顺序工作流设计问题。

Result: 得到了注水式（water-filling）令牌分配策略，以及用影子价格形式表征的最优工作流可靠性。

Conclusion: 为LLM启用的多智能体工作流提供了理论化的性能建模与优化框架，揭示了延迟-可靠性-成本三者之间的基本权衡关系。

Abstract: Modern AI systems increasingly rely on workflows composed of multiple interacting agents, some powered by large language models (LLMs) and others by conventional computational modules. This paper analyzes the fundamental tradeoffs between latency, reliability, and cost in LLM-enabled agentic workflows. We introduce performance models for both LLM and non-LLM agents that capture the relationship between computational effort and output quality, incorporating the impact of reasoning and output tokens for LLM agents using a parametric exponential reliability function. Then, we study the design of sequential workflows under latency and cost constraints. Main results include a water-filling token allocation policy and characterizations of optimal workflow reliability in terms of shadow prices.

</details>


### [168] [Quantum Frog: Emergent Cooperation and Difficulty Scaling in a Quantized-Time Cooperative Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23930)
*Saad Mankarious*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款基于"量化时间"机制的双人合作游戏Quantum Frog，使用强化学习（Tabular Q-Learning、DQN、IDQN、MAPPO）分析了交通密度、单智能体最优策略、合作鸿沟以及涌现的联合策略等四个设计问题。研究发现：(i) 量化时间机制使得"冲刺策略"（每步直上）普遍最优；(ii) 加入一个不协调的第二玩家比单专家玩家面对六倍交通更难；(iii) 协同训练将联合成功率提升32-34个百分点，并将回合步数从~90降至~6；(iv) 涌现的合作策略是同步冲刺而非复杂的位置协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了系统地探究Quantum Frog这款基于量化时间机制的双人合作游戏的设计空间，回答四个关键设计问题：游戏难度如何随交通密度变化、单智能体的最优策略是什么、独立双智能体与协同双智能体之间的合作鸿沟有多大、以及当智能体被激励合作时会涌现出什么样的联合策略。

Method: 使用五种强化学习算法（Tabular Q-Learning、DQN、IDQN、MAPPO）在五个递增阶段训练智能体，针对1到6辆车的交通密度进行评估，以分析游戏设计中的关键问题。

Result: (i) 量化时间机制使"冲刺策略"（每步直上）成为普遍最优策略；(ii) 加入一个不协调的第二玩家难度超过单专家面对六倍交通；(iii) 协同训练（MAPPO）相比独立训练（IDQN）将联合成功率提升32-34个百分点，并将回合步数从~90降至~6；(iv) 涌现的合作策略是同步冲刺而非复杂协调。

Conclusion: 研究发现为Quantum Frog的商业设计提供了经验指导，并揭示了环境机制在塑造多智能体学习动态中的关键作用——共享激励足以在时间关键型合作任务中对齐智能体行为。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{Quantum Frog}, a two-player cooperative game built on a novel \emph{quantized-time} mechanic in which the environment advances only when a player acts. Inspired by the classic arcade game Frogger, Quantum Frog requires two frogs to cross an 8$\times$8 grid of traffic and reach the far side together. We use reinforcement learning (RL) as an analytical lens to answer four design questions: (1) how does game difficulty scale with traffic density, (2) what is the optimal single-agent policy and why, (3) how large is the cooperation gap between independent and cooperative two-agent play, and (4) what joint strategy emerges when agents are incentivised to cooperate? We train agents through five escalating stages, Tabular Q-Learning, Deep Q-Network (\DQN), Independent \DQN~(\IDQN), and Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimisation (\MAPPO\ with a centralised critic), evaluating each against traffic densities of one to six cars. Our key findings are: (i) the quantized-time mechanic makes a \emph{rush strategy} (moving directly upward at every step) universally optimal, as time exposure to traffic is minimised; (ii) adding an uncoordinated second player is harder than sextupling the traffic for a single expert player; (iii) cooperative training recovers +32--34 percentage points of joint success rate relative to independent agents and reduces episode length from $\sim$90 to $\sim$6 steps; and (iv) the emergent cooperative strategy is synchronised rushing, not complex positional coordination, illustrating that shared incentives alone suffice to align agents in time-critical cooperative tasks. These findings provide concrete, empirically grounded guidance for the commercial design of Quantum Frog and offer broader insights into the role of environment mechanics in shaping multi-agent learning dynamics.

</details>


### [169] [BODHI: Precise OS Kernel Specification Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23931)
*Zhiming Chang,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BODHI是一种领域知识提示方法，通过结构化C到Python翻译指南（覆盖15类翻译模式）增强少样本提示，显著提升LLM在操作系统内核形式化规约生成任务上的表现，最佳配置（Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI）达到96.73% Pass@1，相比基线提升11%-32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统内核的形式化验证需要精确的系统调用规约，手动编写需要深厚的领域知识，而现有LLM在OSV-Bench基准上最高Pass@1仅55.10%，亟需自动化方法来弥合通用代码生成与形式化规约合成之间的差距。

Method: 提出BODHI领域知识提示方法：在标准少样本提示基础上加入结构化C-to-Python翻译指南，涵盖15类领域特定翻译模式；借鉴结构化思维链(SCoT)思想，按关注点分离组织指南，将前提条件提取和后置条件生成作为独立类别处理。

Result: 在9个模型（来自Anthropic、Mistral、Amazon、DeepSeek、Meta、Alibaba六家厂商，覆盖稠密、混合专家和推理架构）上，BODHI均带来提升，增益范围+11%至+32%。最佳配置Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI达到96.73% Pass@1。BODHI同时减少了语法错误和语义错误，对指令遵循能力足够的模型效果最强。

Conclusion: 领域知识注入是一种模型无关的技术，能够显著弥合通用代码生成与形式化规约合成之间的差距；方法的有效性依赖于模型具备足够的指令遵循能力以利用结构化参考材料。

Abstract: The formal verification of operating system kernels requires precise specifications that capture the intended behavior of system calls. Writing these specifications manually demands deep domain expertise, motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) to automate the process. However, in OSV-Bench, a benchmark of 245 specification generation tasks derived from the Hyperkernel OS kernel, the best reported Pass@1 is 55.10%. We propose a domain knowledge prompting method (BODHI), which augments the standard few-shot prompt with a structured C-to-Python translation guide covering 15 categories of domain-specific translation patterns. Inspired by Structured Chain-of-Thought (SCoT) prompting, the guide organizes translation by separation of concerns, addressing pre-condition extraction and post-condition generation as distinct categories. Evaluated on nine models from six providers (Anthropic, Mistral, Amazon, DeepSeek, Meta, Alibaba), covering dense, mixture-of-experts and reasoning architectures, BODHI improves every model tested, with gains ranging from +11% to +32%. The best configuration (Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI) reaches 96.73% Pass@1. BODHI reduces both syntax and semantic errors, with the strongest effect on models that have sufficient instruction-following capability to utilize structured reference material. These results demonstrate that domain knowledge injection is a model-agnostic technique that substantially bridges the gap between general-purpose code generation and formal specification synthesis.

</details>


### [170] [When Correct Beliefs Collapse: Epistemic Resilience of LLMs under Clinical Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23932)
*Boyu Xiao,Xiuqi Tian,Xuwen Song,Haochun Wang,Guanchun Song,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Med-Stress压力测试框架评估LLM在临床对话中的信念稳定性，发现医学知识与鲁棒性之间存在分离；提出两种缓解方法：RBED（推理时防御）和R-FT（训练时方法），后者几乎完全消除信念变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在临床对话中即使初始诊断正确，也可能在持续压力下出现严重的多轮迎合行为（sycophancy），放弃正确诊断。现有医学基准测试无法捕捉这一失效模式。

Method: 提出Med-Stress压力测试框架用于评估递增压力下的信念稳定性；提出两种缓解方法：RBED（基于角色的认知防御，推理时轻量级方法）和R-FT（面向韧性的微调，训练时方法，将基于证据的抗压能力内化）。

Result: 在9个前沿LLM上发现：高初始诊断能力不意味高信念稳定性，存在知识与鲁棒性之间的巨大鸿沟；R-FT几乎完全消除信念变化，显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 临床对话中的多轮迎合行为是一个严重但被忽视的问题；医学知识与鲁棒性分离；训练时方法R-FT能有效解决这一问题，为构建可信的临床LLM提供了方向。

Abstract: Despite strong medical benchmark accuracy, LLMs can exhibit severe multi-turn sycophancy in clinical dialogue, abandoning initial correct diagnosis under escalating pressure. We propose \textbf{\textsc{Med-Stress}}, a targeted stress test framework that evaluates belief stability under escalating pressure. Across nine frontier large language models (LLMs), we find a clear dissociation between medical knowledge and robustness: high initial diagnostic capability does not imply high belief stability, yielding large knowledge-robustness gaps for several LLMs. To mitigate this failure mode, we propose a lightweight inference-time defense, \textbf{\texttt{RBED}} (\textbf{R}ole-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{E}pistemic \textbf{D}efense), and \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} (\textbf{R}esilience-oriented \textbf{F}ine-\textbf{T}uning), a training-time approach that internalizes evidence-based resistance to pressure. Experiments show that \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} nearly eliminates belief change and substantially improves robustness.

</details>


### [171] [Practical Quantum CIM Empowerment via All-Domestic-Core Agentic Large Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23934)
*Wang Rui,Lu Diannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将飞秒激光泵浦的相干伊辛机（CIM）与基于LLM的智能体系统（LangGraph+LangChain）集成，实现了QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代和文献方案的快速验证，并全部基于国产大模型和国产CIM硬件完成，同时发现智能体辅助量子计算迭代积累的知识能反向增强智能体自身的问题解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算设备（尤其是相干伊辛机）在求解NP完全问题上具有潜力，但建模过程对非专家存在显著门槛，且对专家而言约束权重和建模方法的繁琐迭代消耗大量精力，亟需自动化辅助手段。

Method: 将飞秒激光泵浦的相干伊辛机与LLM驱动的智能体系统（基于LangGraph和LangChain框架）集成，利用大语言模型执行QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代和文献方案验证等建模任务，并全部基于国产大模型和国产CIM硬件实现。

Result: LLM能够有效执行量子建模中的各项任务，实现了完全依赖国产大模型和国产硬件对量子CIM的实用赋能；同时发现智能体在辅助量子计算迭代过程中积累的知识能反向增强其自身问题解决能力的新范式。

Conclusion: 该工作成功实现了大模型与量子计算的稳健技术集成，为后续研究奠定基础，但也指出了当前两大前沿领域（大模型和量子计算）存在的持续挑战，同时意外发现了一种知识双向增强的新范式有望解决这些挑战。

Abstract: Quantum computing devices are recognized as powerful tools for solving NP-complete problems. However, the intricacy of their modeling presents notable barriers for non-specialists, while the tedious iteration of constraint weights and modeling methodologies also consumes substantial effort on the part of experts. To address these challenges, this study integrates a femtosecond laser-pumped Coherent Ising Machine (CIM) with an LLM-driven agentic system by leveraging the LangGraph and LangChain frameworks. Comprehensive investigations demonstrate that large language models (LLMs) can effectively perform such tasks in modeling as QUBO/Ising model calibration, constraint weight decision iteration and rapid validation of literature-reported schemes. Notably, all these tasks can be fully implemented based on domestic large models, combined with domestically developed CIM hardware, we truly achieve the practical empowerment of quantum CIM that fully relies on all-domestic agentic large models and hardware. This work successfully realizes robust technological integration, laying a solid foundation for subsequent research. Nevertheless, it also identifies the persisting challenges in the two cutting-edge fields of large models and quantum computing at the current stage. Encouragingly, we unexpectedly discover a promising new paradigm where accumulated knowledge from agent-assisted quantum computing iterations reciprocally enhances the agent's own problem-solving capability, thereby addressing these challenges.

</details>


### [172] [BoxLitE: A Faithful Knowledge Base Embedding Based on Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23937)
*Bruno F. Lourenço,Hesham Morgan,Ana Ozaki,Aleksandar Pavlović,Emanuel Sallinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BoxLitE，一种针对DL-Lite^H知识库的嵌入模型，支持凸优化，并证明任何可满足的DL-Lite^H知识库都存在弱忠实嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱嵌入方法虽能将概念映射到凸区域以表示TBox中的层次结构，但凸性的优势在实际学习任务中很少被充分利用。本文旨在设计一种既能利用凸性优势又能保证忠实性的知识库嵌入模型。

Method: 提出BoxLitE模型，将知识库嵌入任务形式化为凸优化问题，通过将概念映射到凸区域（盒状区域）来同时编码ABox事实和TBox本体知识，支持DL-Lite^H表达力。

Result: 理论上证明了对任何可满足的DL-Lite^H知识库，BoxLitE都能生成弱忠实嵌入；实践上展示了如何将嵌入任务转化为凸优化问题并获得具有忠实性保证的嵌入。

Conclusion: BoxLitE首次实现了支持凸优化的DL-Lite^H知识库嵌入，在保证弱忠实性的同时充分利用了凸区域表示的层次结构优势，为知识库嵌入的凸优化方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Knowledge base (KB) embeddings aim at combining the capability of classical knowledge graph embeddings to generalize the information present in facts, the ABox, with conceptual knowledge represented in an ontology language, the TBox. Several authors have recently explored the idea of mapping concepts to convex regions in a vector space. This is useful to represent hierarchies, typically present in TBoxes, since more general concepts can be mapped to larger regions, containing those regions associated with more specific concepts. However, the power of convexity is rarely leveraged during the actual learning tasks. Here, we introduce BoxLitE, a KB embedding model for DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ that allows for convex optimization. We show that for any satisfiable DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ KB, there is a BoxLitE embedding that is a weakly faithful model. As a proof of concept, we show how to formulate the KB embedding task as a convex optimization problem and how to obtain embeddings with such desirable faithfulness properties.

</details>


### [173] [Authority Inversion in LLM-Mediated Ubiquitous Systems: When Models Trust Users Over Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23938)
*Long Zhang,Zi-bo Qin,Wei-neng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在多模态融合中存在"权威反转"现象：数值传感器数据因格式劣势被自然语言用户声称支配，导致近零传感器信任度（AAI = -0.805）。作者提出几何框架、审计指标（CIR/AAI）和几何权威校准方法（GAC），使HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%，并翻转80.2%的错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在融合异构输入（传感器测量值与用户声称信息）时，隐式权威分配机制未被研究，且数值传感器数据在自然语言面前因格式劣势无法有效融入模型推理，这在物理传感需优先的场景中构成严重可靠性隐患。

Method: 提出几何上下文融合框架，设计两种可计算审计指标——上下文融合比（CIR）和权威对齐指数（AAI）；提出推理时层级干预方法——几何权威校准（GAC），通过理论引导的因果注入抑制用户侧错误权威。

Result: 在4个模型（4B-35B参数，三种架构）和4个数据集共576个冲突实例上：AAI = -0.805，Cohen's d = -2.14；GAC将HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%；理论引导因果注入翻转80.2%错误决策（随机对照<0.4%）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中介系统中的权威分配必须显式审计并按应用场景配置，而非隐式默认。数值传感器数据与自然语言输入存在固有格式不对称性，需通过几何层面的干预手段加以校准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly fuse heterogeneous inputs in ubiquitous systems. Yet, how LLMs implicitly allocate authority when sensor measurements and user claims conflict remains unexamined, raising critical reliability concerns for deployments where physical sensing must retain priority. Unlike explicit traditional fusion, LLMs bury authority allocation within learned representations. We discover this allocation is severely format-dependent: numerical sensor data fails to integrate into answer-relevant model directions, allowing natural-language claims to dominate the final decision, a phenomenon we term \textbf{Authority Inversion}.To diagnose and mitigate this, we develop a geometric framework of context integration, introduce two computable audit metrics, specifically the Context Integration Ratio (CIR) and Authority Alignment Index (AAI), and propose Geometric Authority Calibration (GAC), an inference-time layer-level intervention to suppress misplaced user authority. Evaluating four models (4B to 35B parameters, three architectures) across four datasets totaling 576 conflict instances reveals extreme inversion: on numerical tasks, models exhibit near-zero sensor trust (AAI = -0.805, Cohen's d = -2.14), unaffected by model capacity. Validating our geometric framework, theory-guided causal injection flips 80.2\% of incorrect decisions (vs. <0.4\% for random controls). Practically, GAC improves HAR accuracy from 0 -- 1.6\% to 21.9 -- 27.5\%, outperforming prompting baselines. Ultimately, authority allocation in LLM-mediated systems must be explicitly audited and application-specifically configured rather than left implicit.

</details>


### [174] [DRIVE: Modeling Skills at the Reasoning and Interaction Levels for Web Agents under Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23939)
*Xirui Liu,Sihang Zhou,Yanning Hou,Rong Zhou,Haoyuan Chen,Maolin He,Siwei Wang,Hao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DRIVE框架，通过将Web智能体的历史经验分离为自然语言推理技能（抽象可迁移的任务逻辑）和程序化交互技能（具体可执行的页面操作），并引入场景感知协调机制和技能级反思，解决了经验混合存储导致的能力积累困境，在WebArena五个领域平均任务成功率52.8%，超越无技能基线7.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将Web智能体的推理知识和交互知识统一存储，导致抽象表示缺乏可执行性、具体表示无法跨域泛化的两难困境，限制了智能体在新网站上的能力积累。

Method: 提出DRIVE双级技能建模框架：1) 自然语言推理技能捕获可迁移的任务逻辑；2) 程序化交互技能将抽象动作锚定到可执行操作；3) 场景感知协调机制基于任务语义自适应检索和调用双级技能；4) 技能级反思识别分层故障模式，实现针对性技能库扩展与精炼。

Result: 在WebArena五个领域平均任务成功率达52.8%，超越无技能基线7.3个百分点。消融实验证实推理技能和交互技能提供不同且互补的收益。

Conclusion: 分离可迁移任务逻辑与可执行页面操作是有效的，双级技能建模能显著提升Web智能体的泛化能力和能力积累效率。

Abstract: Web agents require both high-level reasoning (for task decomposition) and low-level interactions (for page elements manipulation) to conduct different tasks. However, these knowledge types differ fundamentally: reasoning knowledge (e.g., booking a flight requires first searching for routes) is abstract and transferable across websites, while interaction knowledge (e.g., clicking the Search button at a specific coordinate on Site A) depends heavily on page-specific contexts. Existing methods store experiences uniformly. This creates a dilemma: abstract representations lose executability on concrete pages, while concrete representations fail to generalize across domains. This entanglement limits capability accumulation: on new websites, agents either fail to recognize reusable task logic due to surface-level differences or attempt infeasible actions from outdated page structures. To disentangle them, we propose DRIVE, a dual-level skill modeling framework separating historical experience into natural language reasoning skills, which capture transferable task logic, and programmatic interaction skills, grounding abstract actions to executable operations. A scene-aware coordination mechanism adaptively retrieves and invokes these dual-level skills based on task semantics. DRIVE also uses skill-level reflection to identify hierarchy-specific failure modes, enabling targeted skill library expansion and refinement. Experiments across five WebArena domains show DRIVE attains an average task success rate of 52.8%, exceeding the skill-free baseline by 7.3 percentage points. Further ablations show reasoning and interaction skills provide distinct, complementary benefits, supporting separation of transferable task logic from executable page-level operations.

</details>


### [175] [Residual Drift Dominates Contradiction in Multi-Turn Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23940)
*Sebastien Kawada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现多轮推理系统的主要失败模式并非逻辑矛盾，而是"可满足漂移"（内部状态一致但返回答案悄悄违反先前承诺）。作者构建了DRIFT-Bench基准测试集（816个问题，3个约束域），评估了4种方法在4个开源模型上的表现，发现MUS-Repair方法效果最佳，但修复后的残留错误98-100%仍是可满足漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多轮推理系统的主要失败模式，揭示其并非预期的逻辑矛盾，而是一种更隐蔽的"可满足漂移"现象。

Method: 构建DRIFT-Bench基准（包含816个测试问题，覆盖3个约束域），使用求解器辅助标注；评估4种方法在4个开源模型（8B-120B参数）上的表现；提出MUS-Repair方法（将最小不可满足子集反馈给生成器）。

Result: MUS-Repair在所有设置中表现最优（比最佳非MUS基线高+1.8到+15.0个百分点）；修复后残留错误中98-100%为可满足漂移，矛盾错误降至接近零。

Conclusion: 多轮推理系统的主要失败模式是可满足漂移而非逻辑矛盾；可靠的多轮系统需要单独验证返回答案是否尊重已维护的状态。

Abstract: How do multi-turn reasoning systems fail? The expected answer is logical contradiction, in which the system's maintained state becomes unsatisfiable. We show that the dominant mode is instead satisfiable drift, where the internal state stays consistent while the returned answer silently violates prior commitments. We build DRIFT-Bench (Decomposing Reasoning Into Failure Types), a solver-instrumented benchmark of 816 test problems across three constraint domains, and evaluate four methods on it across four open-weight models (8B-120B parameters). MUS-Repair, which feeds minimal unsatisfiable subsets back to the generator, is strongest in every setting (+1.8 to +15.0 pp over the best non-MUS baseline). But the central finding is what repair leaves behind. After structured feedback, models rarely contradict themselves. They forget. Residual errors are 98-100% satisfiable drift across all settings, while contradiction drops to near zero. Reliable multi-turn systems must separately validate that the returned answer respects the maintained state. Code is available at https://github.com/kaons-research/drift-bench.

</details>


### [176] [MEMOR-E: In-Context and Fine-Tuned LLM Personalization for Alzheimer's Assistive Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23941)
*Maissa Abir Smaili,Eren Sadikoglu,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMOR-E，一款配备交互式平板界面的四足移动机器人，通过微调大语言模型模拟阿尔茨海默病的阶段性认知行为，并结合上下文学习生成非诊断性认知摘要，用于个性化辅助互动和可解释的人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病导致记忆和语言能力逐步衰退，降低患者日常生活独立性，需要社交辅助机器人提供支持。

Method: 开发MEMOR-E机器人平台；使用235名患者的音频转录和合成的健康对照数据微调LLM以模拟阶段性认知行为；采用第二个LLM通过上下文学习生成领域和严重度级别的认知错误摘要；集成可解释AI机制。

Result: MEMOR-E能够生成阶段感知的非诊断性认知摘要，支持个性化辅助互动；可解释AI将模型输出转化为透明、人类可读的证据，实现护理人员监督下的可信人机交互。

Conclusion: 微调LLM和上下文学习可有效用于阿尔茨海默病患者的社交辅助机器人，在提供个性化支持的同时保障交互的透明性和可信度。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a neurodegenerative disorder marked by progressive declines in memory and language that reduce independence in daily life, motivating socially assistive robotic support. This paper presents MEMOR-E, a mobile quadruped robot with an interactive tablet interface that assists patients and caregivers through medication reminders, routine guidance, memory oriented interactions, and companionship. We evaluated the feasibility of fine tuning large language models (LLMs) to emulate stage consistent cognitive behavior and interpret responses across standard neuropsychological language tasks, using audio transcriptions from 235 Alzheimer's patients and synthetically generated healthy controls. We also report findings on using in context learning (ICL) in LLMs, where a second LLM produced domain and severity level cognitive error summaries. Our results show that MEMOR-E can generate stage aware, non diagnostic cognitive summaries that support personalized assistive interactions, while explainable AI mechanisms translate model outputs into transparent, human readable evidence to enable caregiver oversight and trustworthy human robot interaction.

</details>


### [177] [A Dynamical Framework for Cognitive Processes Based on Transformations and Semantic Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23942)
*Carlo Cattani,Dioneia Motta Monte-Serrat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合动力系统、范畴论和认知建模的框架，将认知过程描述为通过反馈机制（变换→解释→稳定）迭代演化的状态空间轨迹，并给出计算示例和语言应用，证明其能模拟语境依赖的语义稳定化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知模型缺乏统一的数学框架来同时表达认知状态的变换、解释和稳定化过程，难以将动力系统、范畴论与认知建模有机结合。

Method: 构建状态空间迭代更新规则 X_{t+1}=π(F(f(X_t)))，其中 f 为内部变换，F 为解释映射，π 强制语义等价；引入范畴论形式化刻画组合结构，通过不动点论证和压缩条件分析动力学稳定性。

Result: 通过计算示例定性分析了诱导动力学特征，并在具体语言应用中展示了语境依赖解释如何建模为朝向稳定语义类的轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架将认知统一表征为反馈驱动的、朝向不变解释演化的过程，成功连接了动力系统、范畴论与认知建模三大领域。

Abstract: This paper proposes a structural and dynamical framework for modeling cognitive processes within a cybernetic perspective. Cognitive states are represented as elements of a state space evolving through an iterative update rule of the form
  \[
  X_{t+1} = π\big(F(f(X_t))\big),
  \]
  where $f$ describes internal transformations, $F$ represents interpretative mappings, and $π$ enforces semantic equivalence.
  The model is interpreted as a feedback system integrating transformation, observation, and stabilization. A categorical formulation is introduced to capture compositional structure, while the associated dynamics are analyzed through fixed-point arguments and contraction conditions ensuring stability.
  To demonstrate the operational character of the framework, a computational illustration is provided, together with a qualitative analysis of the induced dynamics. A concrete linguistic application shows how context-dependent interpretation can be modeled as a trajectory toward a stable semantic class.
  The proposed approach connects dynamical systems, category theory, and cognitive modeling, and provides a unified representation of cognition as a feedback-driven process evolving toward invariant interpretations.

</details>


### [178] [Spacetime Formation under Requirements: Contextual Realization and Form-Dependent Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23943)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出量子概率是语境时空形成在固定时空投影下的表现，源于有限表征能力等需求无法在单一布尔事件结构中实现，从而产生非对易性、干涉和量子类概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子认知研究通常用量子概率替换经典概率来解释顺序效应和语境性，但本文认为需要一种更根本的解释：量子概率不是对经典概率的替代，而是固定时空下语境时空形成的投影结果。

Method: 从有限表征容量、单态语义稳定性、语境敏感干预、避免显式语境标签、相干世界形成和主体间可转换性等需求出发，论证当这些需求无法在单一全局布尔事件结构中实现时，在固定时空投影下表现为非对易性、干涉和量子类概率。通过将经典语境簿记成本重新解释为语境时空形成的固定时空影子，将干涉项视为局部经典实现贡献经非平凡胶合后投影回固定经典时空形式时产生的交叉项。

Result: 建立了先验-操作实在论框架：对象性、事件性、概率和时空都被视为需求下的实现形式，客观性由跨观察者和历史依赖的时空形成中保持不变的不变量定义。

Conclusion: 量子概率可以被理解为需求约束下语境时空形成的固定时空投影，而非对经典概率的取代；这为理解顺序效应和语境性提供了新的形而上学基础。

Abstract: Quantum cognition often explains order effects, contextuality, and violations of the law of total probability by replacing classical probability with quantum probability on a fixed event structure. This paper proposes a different interpretation: quantum probability is the fixed-spacetime projection of contextual spacetime formation under finite-state requirements.
  The framework begins not with time, space, objects, or probabilities, but with requirements such as finite representational capacity, single-state semantic stability, context-sensitive intervention, avoidance of explicit context labels, coherent world-formation, and intersubjective transformability. When these requirements cannot be realized within a single global Boolean event structure, the mismatch appears, under fixed-spacetime projection, as noncommutativity, interference, and quantum-like probability.
  Building on prior single-state approaches to contextuality, we reinterpret classical contextual bookkeeping cost as the fixed-spacetime shadow of contextual spacetime formation. Auxiliary memory or context labels in a classical representation correspond, in this account, to holonomy-like mismatch among locally Boolean logic-worlds. The interference term is the cross term generated when locally classical realization contributions are nontrivially glued and projected back into a fixed classical spacetime form.
  The result is a transcendental-operational realist account: objecthood, eventhood, probability, and spacetime are treated as forms of realization under requirements, while objectivity is defined by invariants preserved across observer- and history-dependent spacetime formations.

</details>


### [179] [Accelerating Long-Tail Generation in Synchronous RLHF Training via Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23945)
*Long Zhao,Qinghe Wang,Jiaan Zhu,Youhui Bai,Zewen Jin,Chaoyi Ruan,Shengnan Wang,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAT提出了一种动态张量并行（TP）配置方法，在RLHF生成阶段根据负载特征实时调整TP配置，通过预测引导的在线重配置和轻量级状态迁移机制，将生成延迟降低34.6%，端到端RLHF训练迭代延迟降低27.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF同步三阶段流水线中，生成阶段因响应长度不均衡导致有效批大小迅速缩小，GPU利用率低下；而主流框架采用静态TP配置，无法适应批次动态变化，存在巨大性能优化空间。

Method: 提出两种关键技术：1）预测引导的在线重配置方法，基于离线性能剖析预测延迟收益，仅在收益超过重配置开销时才触发TP调整；2）轻量级在线重配置机制，通过基于成本模型的KV缓存迁移与重计算选择、原地权重重分片和通信组复用，仅更新受TP变更影响的状态和布局。

Result: 基于SGLang和VeRL框架实现，在LLaMA3.1-8B和Qwen3-14B上的评估显示：生成延迟降低最多34.6%，端到端RLHF训练迭代延迟降低最多27.2%。

Conclusion: PAT通过自适应动态TP重配置有效解决了RLHF生成阶段因响应长度不均衡导致的GPU利用率低下问题，验证了在线动态调整并行配置在RLHF场景中的可行性和显著收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a key post-training paradigm for improving model quality. However, the synchronous three-stage RLHF pipeline is often bottlenecked by the generation stage, where response-length skew causes the effective batch size to shrink rapidly during decoding, leaving GPUs underutilized while a few long responses remain unfinished. Mainstream frameworks employ a static tensor parallelism (TP) configuration that cannot adapt to changing batch characteristics, leaving substantial performance headroom unexplored. We propose PAT, an adaptive TP method that dynamically reconfigures TP during the generation stage of each RLHF iteration. PAT introduces two key techniques. First, a predictor-guided online reconfiguration method decides both the reconfiguration point and the target TP configuration based on offline profiling, triggering reconfiguration only when the predicted latency benefit outweighs the reconfiguration overhead. Second, a lightweight online reconfiguration mechanism updates only the states and layouts affected by TP changes: it adapts unfinished decoding states through a cost-model-based choice between KV-cache migration and recomputation, performs in-place weight resharding, and reuses cached communication groups. We implement PAT on top of SGLang and integrate it with the VeRL framework. Evaluations on LLaMA3.1-8B and Qwen3-14B using DeepScaleR show that PAT reduces generation latency by up to 34.6% and end-to-end RLHF training iteration latency by up to 27.2% compared to the original VeRL setup.

</details>


### [180] [Stop Comparing LLM Agents Without Disclosing the Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23950)
*Yunbei Zhang,Janet Wang,Yingqiang Ge,Weijie Xu,Jihun Hamm,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证在长周期任务中，智能体执行框架（harness）对性能的影响常超过所包装的语言模型本身，并提出"约束绑定论题"：框架配置比模型选择更主导性能方差，现有评估协议系统性地将框架增益误归因于模型改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估协议将智能体性能差异主要归因于底层语言模型，忽略了执行框架（harness）的关键作用。作者旨在纠正这种系统性误归因，揭示框架配置才是长周期任务中性能差异的主导因素。

Method: 通过三条论证路径：1) 控制论形式化，将框架建模为闭环控制系统的控制器，LLM为随机策略；2) 基于公开基准、工业部署和控制方差分解的实证分析；3) 提出框架感知的评估框架，包含披露标准和方差分解协议。

Result: 框架引起的性能方差显著超过模型引起的方差，甚至出现模型排名反转的情况；小规模框架改动可产生超越替换模型的效果。

Conclusion: 在框架规格未被披露的情况下，长周期智能体的排行榜比较应被视为不完整且可能具有误导性；应采用框架感知的评估方法来正确归因性能提升来源。

Abstract: This position paper argues that, for long-horizon tasks evaluated across models with comparable frontier capability, the agent execution harness, namely the infrastructure layer that governs context construction, tool interaction, orchestration, and verification around a language model, is often a stronger determinant of agent performance than the model it wraps. We formalize and defend the Binding Constraint Thesis: in this regime, performance variance is governed more by harness configuration than by model choice, and current evaluation protocols therefore systematically misattribute harness-level gains to model improvements. We support this thesis along three lines. First, a control-theoretic formalization treats the harness as the controller of a closed-loop dynamical system and the LLM as the stochastic policy it governs, which explains why small harness changes can produce performance shifts that exceed those obtained by substituting one model for another. Second, published benchmarks, industry deployments, and a controlled variance decomposition show that harness-induced variance can substantially exceed model-induced variance, including cases of model ranking reversal. Third, we propose a harness-aware evaluation framework with a disclosure standard and a variance decomposition protocol. Until harness specifications are disclosed, leaderboard comparisons for long-horizon agents should be treated as incomplete and potentially misleading.

</details>


### [181] [QUIVER: A Formal Framework for Quantifying Perturbation Propagation and Bifurcation in Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23956)
*Prashanti Nilayam,Sankalp Nayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QUIVER是一个用于量化图结构LLM流水线中扰动传播的正式框架，定义了敏感性矩阵、轨迹发散、分岔阈值和分布忠实度四个核心概念，并在8200+追踪数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架缺乏对混合模式输出的异构图节点中扰动传播的量化方法，无法分析节点随机性和执行路径结构差异对系统的影响。

Method: 提出QUIVER框架，包含：(1) 基于类型分派距离度量的敏感性矩阵，将边分类为放大器、吸收器或阈值敏感型；(2) 轨迹发散分解为值漂移、结构路径发散和迭代计数发散；(3) 分岔阈值识别引起结构执行路径改变的最小扰动；(4) 分布忠实度量化节点评估数据集与生产分布的偏差。

Result: 在8200+条追踪数据（32000+对比较）上验证，QUIVER能揭示不同架构的敏感性特征，区分产生相同发散率的机械不同级联模式，仅从观测数据预测易发生轨迹分岔的节点，并定位聚合指标无法发现的陈旧评估伪影。

Conclusion: QUIVER为复合AI系统的稳健性分析提供了形式化工具，能够有效诊断图结构LLM流水线中的扰动传播行为。

Abstract: Compound AI systems that chain multiple LLM calls into directed computation graphs are now the dominant architecture for production AI. Although these architectures leverage heterogeneous nodes with mixed-mode outputs, no existing framework quantifies how perturbations propagate through such pipelines, where nodes are stochastic and execution paths can diverge structurally. We introduce QUIVER, a formal framework for measuring perturbation propagation in graph-structured LLM pipelines. The framework defines: (1) a sensitivity matrix with type-dispatched distance metrics that classifies edges as amplifiers, absorbers, or threshold-sensitive, complemented by occurrence-lift; (2) trajectory divergence decomposing variation into value drift, structural path divergence, and iteration count divergence; (3) bifurcation thresholds identifying the smallest perturbation that causes structural execution path changes; and (4) distribution faithfulness, quantifying when per node evaluation datasets diverge from production distributions. We validate on two production enterprise pipelines and a public DSPy multihop QA pipeline, three structurally distinct architectures. Across 8,200+ instrumented traces (32,000+ pair comparisons), we demonstrate that QUIVER reveals distinct sensitivity profiles across architectures, distinguishes mechanistically different cascade patterns producing identical divergence rates, predicts nodes prone to trajectory bifurcation from observational data alone, and localizes stale evaluation artifacts to specific node-field categories that aggregate metrics cannot surface.

</details>


### [182] [LGMT: Logic-Grounded Metamorphic Testing for Evaluating the Reasoning Reliability of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23965)
*Zenghui Zhou,Man Li,Xiaoke Fang,Xinyi Zhou,Weibin Li,Zheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LGMT框架，利用一阶逻辑的变形关系构建语义不变的测试用例，检测LLM在逻辑推理中的隐藏缺陷。实验表明现有静态基准高估了推理能力，模型对符号级和结论级变化敏感，少样本思维链只能部分缓解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准无法评估LLM在逻辑等价变换下的鲁棒性，往往高估推理能力，需要一种无需标签的、基于逻辑一致性的评估方法。

Method: 基于一阶逻辑（FOL）的变形测试（Metamorphic Testing）框架，从形式逻辑等价关系中导出变形关系，构建语义不变的测试用例，通过跨用例一致性检查检测推理缺陷。

Result: 在六个先进LLM上的实验表明，LGMT发现了大量传统基于参考答案的评估无法暴露的隐藏缺陷；模型对符号级和结论级变化尤为敏感；Few-shot CoT等高级提示方法只能部分缓解问题。

Conclusion: LLM评估应从孤立的正确性转向逻辑不变性下的鲁棒性评估，LGMT提供了一种原则性强且可扩展的推理缺陷诊断方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on logical reasoning benchmarks, yet their reliability remains uncertain. Existing evaluations rely on static benchmarks, which fail to assess robustness under logically equivalent transformations and often overestimate reasoning capability. We propose LGMT (Logic-Grounded Metamorphic Testing), an oracle-free framework that leverages first-order logic (FOL) to evaluate LLM reasoning. By deriving metamorphic relations from formal logical equivalences, LGMT constructs semantically invariant test cases and detects reasoning defects through cross-case consistency checking. Experiments on six state-of-the-art LLMs show that LGMT exposes substantial hidden defects missed by traditional reference-based evaluations. We further find that models are particularly sensitive to symbol-level and conclusion-level variations, and that advanced prompting such as Few-shot CoT only partially mitigates these issues. These results suggest that LLM evaluation should move beyond isolated correctness toward robustness under logical invariance. LGMT provides a principled and scalable approach for diagnosing reasoning failures.

</details>


### [183] [Why We Need World Models for AGI: Where LLMs Fail and How World Models May Outperform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23972)
*Feisal Alaswad,Batoul Aljaddouh,Maher Alrahhal,Poovammal E,Talal Bonny*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在动态推理（LDI）概念框架，指出大语言模型在因果推理、状态跟踪和长程规划中的局限源于序列预测与潜在环境动态推理之间的目标层级不匹配。通过构建纯自然语言规则描述的Flux环境进行案例研究，对比发现基于显式潜在状态空间的强化学习智能体在长程游戏中胜率约79%，而纯文本LLM仅11%，验证了序列预测难以支撑鲁棒的动态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要因果推理、持久状态跟踪和长程规划的设定中表现受限，推测其根本原因是序列预测目标与潜在环境动态推理之间存在目标层级不匹配。

Method: 提出Latent Dynamics Inference (LDI)概念框架，将语言和多模态观测视为潜在转移动力学的部分证据。构建Flux环境——完全由自然语言规则定义的序列推理环境，将规则编译为显式状态转移模拟器，对比LLM纯文本操作与在显式潜在状态空间上训练的强化学习智能体。

Result: 强化学习智能体在长程游戏中胜率约79%，LLM仅11%。LLM出现无效动作、状态跟踪错误和短视推理失败等与不稳定状态跟踪一致的失败模式。

Conclusion: 强序列预测能力本身难以支撑鲁棒的长程动态推理，需要引入持久状态跟踪和转移建模机制。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong performance in language generation and knowledge-intensive tasks, yet remain limited in settings requiring causal reasoning, persistent state tracking, and long-horizon planning. We argue that these limitations may arise from an objective-level mismatch between sequence prediction and reasoning over latent environment dynamics. To formalize this distinction, we introduce Latent Dynamics Inference (LDI), a conceptual perspective that interprets language and multimodal observations as partial evidence of underlying transition dynamics. To empirically investigate this perspective, we introduce Flux, a sequential reasoning environment specified entirely through natural-language rules. As a proof-of-concept case study, the rules are first compiled into an explicit state-transition simulator, illustrating that structured latent transition dynamics can, in some cases, be operationally extracted from textual rule descriptions. This enables a controlled comparison between the LLMs operating purely over textual observations and reinforcement-learning agents trained directly within the extracted latent state space. Within this case study, agents operating with explicit access to the latent state space exhibit substantially more stable behavior in long-horizon gameplay, achieving an aggregate win rate of approximately 79% versus 11% for LLMs. Qualitative analysis further reveals failure modes consistent with unstable persistent state tracking, including invalid actions, state-tracking errors, and short-horizon reasoning failures. The complete implementation of the Flux environment available at https://github.com/FeisalAlaswad/FLUX-RL-Agent Within the evaluated setting, these results suggest that strong sequence prediction alone may struggle to support robust long-horizon dynamic reasoning without mechanisms for persistent state tracking and transition modeling

</details>


### [184] [Towards trustworthy agentic AI: a comprehensive survey of safety, robustness, privacy, and system security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23989)
*Jinhu Qi,Muzhi Li,Jiahong Liu,Yuqin Shu,Dianzhi Yu,Shicheng Ma,Wenqian Cui,Yiyang Zhao,Yiyi Chen,Ruoxi Jiang,Irwin King,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦可信赖的自主型AI系统，从安全与鲁棒性、隐私与系统安全两大核心维度，系统梳理了概念、风险来源及分阶段缓解策略，并构建了统一评估指标与基准（涵盖结果与过程信号），最后讨论了开放挑战（如自进化智能体、运行时监控、隐私个性化）及真实世界安全失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通过增强规划、工具使用、记忆和长程交互构成自主型AI系统，可自主执行复杂任务，但其多步轨迹引入了新的失败模式，挑战了系统的可信赖性。当前缺乏针对高风险部署场景下可信赖自主型AI的聚焦性综述，特别是安全、鲁棒性、隐私和系统安全维度的系统性梳理。

Method: 采用聚焦式综述方法，从安全与鲁棒性、隐私与系统安全两个核心维度展开，每个维度均明确关键概念、识别风险在智能体工作流中出现的阶段、总结针对阶段的缓解策略；将其他可信赖方面（价值对齐、透明度、公平性、问责性）作为相关上下文而非平行章节讨论；整合统一指标与基准评估框架，涵盖结果信号（如约束违反、轨迹完整性、对抗成功率）并提供场景到指标的发布门控指导。

Result: 建立了统一的可信赖评估指标与基准系统，区分了结果信号和过程信号；提供了场景到指标的映射指南，用于发布门控决策；识别了智能体工作流中不同阶段的风险点及对应的缓解策略；通过真实世界开源自主型系统的安全失败案例研究，展示了实际部署中的信任挑战。

Conclusion: 自主型AI系统在高风险场景中的可信赖部署需要同时关注安全/鲁棒性和隐私/系统安全两大维度；当前面临自进化智能体、运行时监控与验证、隐私保护个性化及信任-效用权衡等开放挑战；统一评估框架对推动实际部署决策至关重要。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems -- Large Language Models (LLMs) augmented with planning, tool use, memory, and long-horizon interactions -- can execute complex tasks autonomously, but their multi-step trajectories introduce new failure modes that challenge trustworthiness. This survey provides a focused examination of trustworthy agentic AI through two core dimensions that are critical for high-risk deployments: Safety and Robustness, and Privacy and System Security. For each dimension, we clarify key concepts, identify where risks emerge along the agent workflow, and summarize stage-targeted mitigation strategies. Other trustworthiness aspects (value alignment, transparency, fairness, and accountability) are discussed as relevant context rather than parallel chapters. To support consistent comparison and deployment decisions, we consolidate evaluation into a unified metrics-and-benchmarks hub, emphasizing both outcome and process signals (e.g., constraint violations, trace completeness, and adversarial success rates) and offering scenario-to-metric guidance for release gating. We conclude by outlining open challenges such as self-evolving agents, runtime monitoring and verification, privacy-preserving personalization, and the trust-utility trade-off, and present a case study of real-world security failures in open-source agentic systems. Our goal is to serve as a practical reference for researchers and practitioners building trustworthy agentic systems in high-stakes environments.

</details>


### [185] [Reason--Imagine--Act: Closed-Loop LLM Decision Making with World Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24004)
*Zhengqi Sun,Yiwen Sun,Boxuan Liu,Tailai Chen,Tianxu Guo,Jiabin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Reason-Imagine-Act (RIA)框架，结合LLM推理器与行动条件世界模型，在驾驶决策中实现在线安全验证，显著降低碰撞率（0.20%）并提升到达率（51.10%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动驾驶方法仅依赖语义决策而缺乏物理安全性验证，在线语言推理无显式动力学校验，世界模型多用于离线流程，导致语义意图与物理可行性之间存在决策时隙。

Method: RIA闭环框架：LLM推理器提出行动模板与候选子动作，行动条件世界模型进行短视距滚动预测，安全评分器选择最安全的可执行动作并将反馈传递至下一推理步骤。

Result: 在CARLA点目标协议（1000 episode）下：路线完成率80.05%，到达率51.10%，碰撞率0.20%；在相同闭环接口下，RIA在核心闭环指标上持续优于CARLA TM和MADA等无训练基线。

Conclusion: 通过在线世界模型验证耦合LLM推理，RIA有效弥合了语义意图与物理可行性之间的鸿沟，实现了更安全的自动驾驶决策。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are promising for autonomous driving, but semantics-only decision policies can yield physically unsafe behavior in dynamic traffic. Existing methods either perform online language reasoning without explicit dynamics verification or use world models mainly in offline pipelines, leaving a gap between semantic intent and physical feasibility at decision time. We propose Reason--Imagine--Act (RIA), a closed-loop framework that couples an LLM reasoner with an action-conditioned world model for online safety verification. At each step, the LLM proposes an action template and candidate sub-actions, the world model performs short-horizon rollouts, and a safety scorer selects the safest executable action with feedback to the next reasoning step. Under a unified CARLA point-goal protocol (1000 episodes), RIA achieves 80.05% route completion, 51.10% arrival rate, and 0.20% collision rate. Under the same closed-loop interface, RIA consistently outperforms training-free baselines, including CARLA TM and MADA, on core closed-loop metrics. For reproducibility, code is available at https://github.com/pku-smart-city/source_code/tree/main/RIA.

</details>


### [186] [LC-ERD: Mining Latent Logic for Self-Evolving Reasoning via Consistency-Regulated Reward Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24005)
*Yanyu Chen,Jiyue Jiang,Dianzhi Yu,Zheng Wu,Jiahong Liu,Jiaming Han,Xiao Guo,Jinhu Qi,Yu Li,Yifei Zhang,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LC-ERD提出一种逻辑一致的内生奖励分解框架，通过潜在逻辑专家共识去噪推理流形，并基于IGM原理的多智能体价值分解实现细粒度监督，解决自对齐中标签噪声、粗粒度监督和分布崩溃三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理能力的发展受限于高质量过程数据的稀缺。现有自对齐方法存在三个瓶颈：模仿偏差导致的标签噪声（奖励偏好统计似然而非逻辑真值，造成"正确性幻觉"）、粗粒度监督（全局稀疏结果无法提供逐步指导）以及分布崩溃（信号无法泛化且放大预训练偏见）。
